===== FILE: shared/scoring.js =====
// Explicit padel scoring state machine. Teams are 0 and 1.
// Pure data in / data out so it can be tested exhaustively.

export function createMatch(cfg = {}) {
  const c = { sets: 3, gamesPerSet: 6, goldenPoint: false, singles: false, ...cfg };
  return {
    cfg: c,
    sets: [], // completed sets: [gamesTeam0, gamesTeam1]
    setsWon: [0, 0],
    games: [0, 0],
    points: [0, 0],
    tiebreak: false,
    tbOrder: null,
    tbFirstTeam: null,
    pointsInGame: 0, // points played in current game / tiebreak (drives serve side)
    servingTeam: 0,
    serverIdx: [0, 0], // which of the team's two players serves
    served: [true, false],
    totalPoints: [0, 0],
    winner: null,
  };
}

const other = (t) => 1 - t;

// Who serves the next point.
export function currentServer(m) {
  if (m.tiebreak) {
    const e = m.tbOrder[Math.floor((m.pointsInGame + 1) / 2) % 4];
    return { team: e.team, idx: m.cfg.singles ? 0 : e.idx };
  }
  return { team: m.servingTeam, idx: m.cfg.singles ? 0 : m.serverIdx[m.servingTeam] };
}

// 0 = serve from the "deuce" (right-hand) side, 1 = advantage side.
export function serveSide(m) {
  return m.pointsInGame % 2;
}

function advanceServer(m, fromTeam) {
  const next = other(fromTeam);
  if (m.served[next]) m.serverIdx[next] ^= 1;
  m.served[next] = true;
  m.servingTeam = next;
}

function startTiebreak(m) {
  m.tiebreak = true;
  m.points = [0, 0];
  m.pointsInGame = 0;
  const x = m.servingTeam;
  const y = other(x);
  const xi = m.serverIdx[x];
  const yi = m.served[y] ? m.serverIdx[y] ^ 1 : 0;
  m.tbFirstTeam = x;
  m.tbOrder = [
    { team: x, idx: xi },
    { team: y, idx: yi },
    { team: x, idx: xi ^ 1 },
    { team: y, idx: yi ^ 1 },
  ];
}

function gameWon(m, team, out) {
  m.games[team]++;
  m.points = [0, 0];
  m.pointsInGame = 0;
  out.push({ type: 'game', team });
  const g = m.cfg.gamesPerSet;
  const a = m.games[team];
  const b = m.games[other(team)];
  if ((a >= g && a - b >= 2) || (m.tiebreak && a > b)) {
    setWon(m, team, out);
  } else if (a === g && b === g) {
    startTiebreak(m);
    out.push({ type: 'tiebreak' });
  } else {
    advanceServer(m, m.servingTeam);
  }
}

function setWon(m, team, out) {
  m.sets.push([m.games[0], m.games[1]]);
  m.setsWon[team]++;
  out.push({ type: 'set', team });
  const need = Math.floor(m.cfg.sets / 2) + 1;
  const wasTiebreak = m.tiebreak;
  m.games = [0, 0];
  m.points = [0, 0];
  m.pointsInGame = 0;
  m.tiebreak = false;
  if (m.setsWon[team] >= need) {
    m.winner = team;
    out.push({ type: 'match', team });
    return;
  }
  // the team that received first in a tiebreak serves first in the next set
  advanceServer(m, wasTiebreak ? m.tbFirstTeam : m.servingTeam);
  m.tbOrder = null;
}

export function pointWon(m, team) {
  if (m.winner !== null) return [];
  const out = [{ type: 'point', team }];
  m.totalPoints[team]++;
  m.points[team]++;
  m.pointsInGame++;
  const p = m.points[team];
  const q = m.points[other(team)];
  if (m.tiebreak) {
    if (p >= 7 && p - q >= 2) gameWon(m, team, out);
  } else if (m.cfg.goldenPoint ? p >= 4 : p >= 4 && p - q >= 2) {
    gameWon(m, team, out); // golden point: the point after 40-40 decides the game
  }
  return out;
}

const LABELS = ['0', '15', '30', '40'];

export function displayPoints(m) {
  const [a, b] = m.points;
  if (m.tiebreak) return [String(a), String(b)];
  if (a >= 3 && b >= 3) {
    if (a === b) return ['40', '40'];
    return a > b ? ['AD', '40'] : ['40', 'AD'];
  }
  return [LABELS[Math.min(a, 3)], LABELS[Math.min(b, 3)]];
}

export function pointLabel(m) {
  const [a, b] = m.points;
  if (!m.tiebreak && a >= 3 && b >= 3 && a === b) return m.cfg.goldenPoint ? 'Golden point' : 'Deuce';
  return '';
}

// Public, serialisable view sent to clients.
export function scoreView(m) {
  const need = Math.floor(m.cfg.sets / 2) + 1;
  const g = m.cfg.gamesPerSet;
  let gamePoint = null;
  let setPoint = false;
  let matchPoint = false;
  if (m.winner === null) {
    for (const t of [0, 1]) {
      const copy = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(m));
      const evs = pointWon(copy, t);
      if (evs.some((e) => e.type === 'game')) {
        gamePoint = t;
        if (evs.some((e) => e.type === 'set')) setPoint = true;
        if (evs.some((e) => e.type === 'match')) matchPoint = true;
      }
    }
  }
  const srv = m.winner === null ? currentServer(m) : null;
  return {
    sets: m.sets,
    setsWon: m.setsWon,
    games: m.games,
    points: displayPoints(m),
    label: pointLabel(m),
    tiebreak: m.tiebreak,
    server: srv,
    serveSide: serveSide(m),
    gamePoint,
    setPoint,
    matchPoint,
    totalPoints: m.totalPoints,
    winner: m.winner,
    need,
    gamesPerSet: g,
  };
}
===== END FILE: shared/scoring.js =====

export function sceneFor(eventId, kind) {

===== FILE: server/index.js =====
// HTTP static server + WebSocket game server.

import { QuickQueue } from './quick.js';
import http from 'node:http';
import zlib from 'node:zlib';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { WebSocketServer } from 'ws';
import { Store } from './store.js';
import { RoomManager } from './rooms.js';
import { SERVER_TICK } from '../shared/constants.js';
import { CAREER } from '../shared/career.js';
import { sanitizeLook } from '../shared/looks.js';

const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const MIME = {
  '.html': 'text/html; charset=utf-8', '.js': 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8', '.css': 'text/css; charset=utf-8',
  '.json': 'application/json', '.svg': 'image/svg+xml', '.png': 'image/png', '.ico': 'image/x-icon', '.map': 'application/json',
};
const STATIC = [
  ['/vendor/three/', path.join(ROOT, 'node_modules', 'three')],
  ['/shared/', path.join(ROOT, 'shared')],
  ['/', path.join(ROOT, 'client')],
];

function serveStatic(req, res) {
  let pathname;
  try {
    // never trust the request target: a path like "//" must not be parsed as a URL authority
    pathname = decodeURIComponent(req.url.split('?')[0].replace(/^\/+/, '/'));
  } catch {
    res.writeHead(400); res.end('Bad request'); return;
  }
  if (pathname === '/health') {
    res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'application/json' });
    res.end('{"ok":true}');
    return;
  }
  if (pathname.endsWith('/')) pathname += 'index.html';
  for (const [prefix, dir] of STATIC) {
    if (!pathname.startsWith(prefix)) continue;
    const file = path.resolve(dir, pathname.slice(prefix.length));
    if (!file.startsWith(dir + path.sep) && file !== dir) break; // path traversal guard
    try {
      const st = fs.statSync(file);
      if (!st.isFile()) continue;
      const type = MIME[path.extname(file)] || 'application/octet-stream';
      const gz = (req.headers['accept-encoding'] || '').includes('gzip') && (type.startsWith('text/') || type.startsWith('application/json') || type.startsWith('application/javascript'));
      // vendored libraries never change between deploys: let browsers cache them
      const cache = prefix === '/vendor/three/' ? 'public, max-age=86400' : 'no-cache';
      res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': type, 'cache-control': cache, ...(gz ? { 'content-encoding': 'gzip', vary: 'accept-encoding' } : {}) });
      const stream = fs.createReadStream(file);
      stream.on('error', () => res.destroy());
      if (gz) stream.pipe(zlib.createGzip({ level: 6 })).pipe(res); else stream.pipe(res);
      return;
    } catch { /* try next mapping */ }
  }
  res.writeHead(404, { 'content-type': 'text/plain' });
  res.end('Not found');
}

export function createGameServer({ port = 0, dataDir = process.env.DATA_DIR || path.join(ROOT, 'data'), host } = {}) {
  const store = new Store(dataDir);
  const rooms = new RoomManager(store);
  const quick = new QuickQueue(rooms);
  const onlineCount = () => { let n = 0; for (const ws of wss.clients) if (ws.readyState === 1 && ws.ctx?.token) n++; return n; };
  const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
    try { serveStatic(req, res); } catch (err) {
      console.error('[http]', err.message);
      if (!res.headersSent) res.writeHead(500);
      res.end();
    }
  });
  const wss = new WebSocketServer({ server, path: '/ws', maxPayload: 16 * 1024 });

  wss.on('connection', (ws) => {
    ws.ctx = { token: null, name: 'Guest', bucket: 200, bucketAt: Date.now() };
    ws.alive = true;
    ws.on('pong', () => { ws.alive = true; });
    ws.on('message', (data) => onMessage(ws, data));
    ws.on('close', () => onClose(ws));
    ws.on('error', () => {});
  });

  const send = (ws, msg) => { if (ws.readyState === 1) ws.send(JSON.stringify(msg)); };
  const fail = (ws, message, code = 'error') => send(ws, { t: 'error', code, message });

  function onClose(ws) {
    const { token } = ws.ctx;
    if (!token) return;
    quick.remove(token);
    const ms = rooms.membership(token);
    if (ms && ms.member.ws === ws) ms.room.disconnect(ms.member);
  }

  function onMessage(ws, data) {
    // rate limit: token bucket, ~300 msgs/s sustained
    const c = ws.ctx;
    const now = Date.now();
    c.bucket = Math.min(300, c.bucket + ((now - c.bucketAt) / 1000) * 300);
    c.bucketAt = now;
    if (--c.bucket < 0) { ws.close(1008, 'rate limit'); return; }

    let msg;
    try { msg = JSON.parse(data.toString()); } catch { return; }
    if (!msg || typeof msg.t !== 'string') return;

    if (msg.t === 'hello') {
      let token = typeof msg.token === 'string' && store.hasProfile(msg.token) ? msg.token : store.newToken();
      const prof = store.profile(token, String(msg.name || '').slice(0, 16) || undefined);
      c.token = token;
      c.name = prof.name;
      c.look = msg.look ? sanitizeLook(msg.look) : null;
      send(ws, { t: 'welcome', token, name: prof.name, profile: store.publicProfile(token), online: onlineCount() });
      const ms = rooms.membership(token);
      if (ms) {
        // reconnect: rebind the new socket to the old seat
        if (ms.member.ws && ms.member.ws !== ws) { const old = ms.member.ws; ms.member.ws = null; old.close(4000, 'replaced'); }
        ms.member.name = prof.name;
        ms.room.reconnect(ms.member, ws);
      }
      return;
    }
    if (!c.token) return fail(ws, 'Say hello first', 'no-session');

    if (msg.t === 'ping') {
      send(ws, { t: 'pong', ts: msg.ts });
      const ms = rooms.membership(c.token);
      if (ms && Number.isFinite(msg.rtt)) ms.member.rtt = Math.min(2000, Math.max(0, Math.round(msg.rtt)));
      return;
    }
    if (msg.t === 'profile') return send(ws, { t: 'profile', profile: store.publicProfile(c.token) });
    if (msg.t === 'rename') {
      const p = store.profile(c.token, String(msg.name || '').slice(0, 16));
      c.name = p.name;
      return send(ws, { t: 'profile', profile: store.publicProfile(c.token) });
    }

    if (msg.t === 'look') {
      c.look = sanitizeLook(msg.look);
      const lm = rooms.membership(c.token);
      if (lm) { lm.member.look = c.look; lm.room.broadcastRoom(); }
      return;
    }
    if (msg.t === 'quick') {
      quick.add({ token: c.token, name: msg.name || c.name, ws, look: c.look });
      return send(ws, { t: 'queue', ...quick.status(c.token), online: onlineCount() });
    }
    if (msg.t === 'quickCancel') { quick.remove(c.token); return send(ws, { t: 'queue', cancelled: true, online: onlineCount() }); }
    if (msg.t === 'rooms') return send(ws, { t: 'rooms', rooms: rooms.listPublic() });
    if (msg.t === 'career') return send(ws, { t: 'career', career: store.careerView(c.token) });
    if (msg.t === 'careerStart') {
      const stage = CAREER.find((s) => s.id === Number(msg.stage));
      const view = store.careerView(c.token);
      if (!stage || !view.stages.find((s) => s.id === stage.id)?.unlocked) return fail(ws, 'That stage is locked', 'rejected');
      if (rooms.membership(c.token)) rooms.leave(c.token);
      const { room, member } = rooms.create({ token: c.token, name: msg.name || c.name, ws, look: c.look });
      member.name = String(msg.name || c.name).slice(0, 16) || 'Guest';
      room.setupCareer(stage, member);
      return;
    }

    if (msg.t === 'create') {
      if (rooms.membership(c.token)) rooms.leave(c.token);
      const { room, member } = rooms.create({ token: c.token, name: msg.name || c.name, ws, look: c.look });
      member.name = String(msg.name || c.name).slice(0, 16) || 'Guest';
      room.broadcastRoom();
      return;
    }
    if (msg.t === 'join') {
      const cur = rooms.membership(c.token);
      if (cur && cur.room.code === String(msg.code || '').toUpperCase().trim()) { cur.room.reconnect(cur.member, ws); return; }
      if (cur) rooms.leave(c.token);
      const r = rooms.join(msg.code, { token: c.token, name: msg.name || c.name, ws, look: c.look });
      if (r.error) return fail(ws, r.error, 'join-failed');
      r.room.broadcastRoom();
      return;
    }

    const ms = rooms.membership(c.token);
    if (!ms) return fail(ws, 'You are not in a room', 'no-room');
    const { room, member } = ms;
    let err = null;
    switch (msg.t) {
      case 'leave': rooms.leave(c.token); send(ws, { t: 'left' }); return;
      case 'in': room.pushInputs(member, msg.inputs); return;
      case 'sit': err = room.sit(member, msg.slot); break;
      case 'assign': err = room.assign(member, msg.id, msg.slot); break;
      case 'ready':
        if (room.state === 'lobby') member.ready = !!msg.ready;
        break;
      case 'settings': err = room.setSettings(member, msg.settings || {}); break;
      case 'start': err = room.start(member); break;
      case 'rematch': err = room.rematch(member); return err ? fail(ws, err) : undefined;
      case 'lobby': err = room.toLobby(member); break;
      case 'pauseReq': case 'pauseAccept': case 'pauseDeny': case 'pauseResume':
        err = room.pauseCtl(member, msg.t.slice(5).toLowerCase()); if (err) return fail(ws, err, 'pause'); return;
      case 'chat': room.chat(member, msg.text); return;
      case 'emote': room.emote(member, msg.id); return;
      default: return; // unknown messages (including any attempt to submit scores) are ignored
    }
    if (err) fail(ws, err, 'rejected');
    room.broadcastRoom();
  }

  // fixed-step simulation loop shared by all rooms
  const STEP = 1 / SERVER_TICK;
  let last = performance.now();
  let acc = 0;
  const loop = setInterval(() => {
    const now = performance.now();
    acc += Math.min(0.25, (now - last) / 1000);
    last = now;
    while (acc >= STEP) { rooms.step(STEP); acc -= STEP; }
  }, 8);
  const sweeper = setInterval(() => rooms.sweep(), 30_000);
  // matchmaking and the live player count
  const matchmaker = setInterval(() => {
    quick.tick();
    const online = onlineCount();
    for (const ws of wss.clients) {
      if (ws.readyState !== 1 || !ws.ctx?.token) continue;
      const st = quick.status(ws.ctx.token);
      send(ws, st ? { t: 'queue', ...st, online } : { t: 'online', n: online });
    }
  }, 1000);
  const heartbeat = setInterval(() => {
    for (const ws of wss.clients) {
      if (!ws.alive) { ws.terminate(); continue; }
      ws.alive = false;
      ws.ping();
    }
  }, 15_000);

  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    server.listen(port, host, () => {
      const actual = server.address().port;
      resolve({
        port: actual, rooms, store, server, wss,
        async close() {
          clearInterval(loop); clearInterval(sweeper); clearInterval(matchmaker); clearInterval(heartbeat);
          for (const ws of wss.clients) ws.terminate();
          store.flush();
          await new Promise((r) => wss.close(() => server.close(() => r())));
        },
      });
    });
  });
}

if (process.argv[1] && path.resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
  const port = Number(process.env.PORT) || 3000;
  const s = await createGameServer({ port });
  console.log(`PADEL CLUB server listening on http://localhost:${s.port}`);
  // a public server must survive stray errors from any single connection
  process.on('uncaughtException', (err) => console.error('[uncaught]', err));
  process.on('unhandledRejection', (err) => console.error('[unhandled]', err));
  const stop = () => { s.close().then(() => process.exit(0)); };
  process.on('SIGINT', stop);
  process.on('SIGTERM', stop);
}
===== END FILE: server/index.js =====

===== FILE: server/quick.js =====
// Quick match: players queue up and are put in a doubles match as soon as four are waiting. Nobody waits long: after
// WAIT_MS the people in the queue play together with bots filling the empty seats.

export const WAIT_MS = 10_000;

export class QuickQueue {
  /** @param rooms RoomManager  @param now () => ms (injectable for tests) */
  constructor(rooms, now = Date.now) {
    this.rooms = rooms;
    this.now = now;
    this.list = []; // {token, name, ws, look, at}
  }

  has(token) { return this.list.some((e) => e.token === token); }
  size() { return this.list.length; }

  add(client) {
    if (this.has(client.token)) return this.list.findIndex((e) => e.token === client.token) + 1;
    this.rooms.leave(client.token); // a quick match replaces whatever lobby you were in
    this.list.push({ ...client, at: this.now() });
    return this.list.length;
  }

  remove(token) {
    const n = this.list.length;
    this.list = this.list.filter((e) => e.token !== token);
    return this.list.length !== n;
  }

  /** Seconds the player has waited and seconds until bots fill the match. */
  status(token) {
    const e = this.list.find((x) => x.token === token);
    if (!e) return null;
    const waited = (this.now() - e.at) / 1000;
    return { waited: Math.floor(waited), botsIn: Math.max(0, Math.ceil(WAIT_MS / 1000 - waited)), waiting: this.list.length };
  }

  /** Form matches. Returns the rooms created. */
  tick() {
    this.list = this.list.filter((e) => e.ws && e.ws.readyState === 1);
    const made = [];
    while (this.list.length >= 4) made.push(this.make(this.list.splice(0, 4)));
    if (this.list.length && this.now() - this.list[0].at >= WAIT_MS) made.push(this.make(this.list.splice(0, this.list.length)));
    return made;
  }

  make(players) {
    const [first, ...rest] = players;
    const { room } = this.rooms.create(first);
    room.settings = { ...room.settings, mode: 'doubles', fillBots: true, public: false, difficulty: 'normal' };
    for (const p of rest) {
      const m = room.addMember(p);
      this.rooms.byToken.set(p.token, { room, id: m.id });
    }
    room.beginMatch();
    return room;
  }
}
===== END FILE: server/quick.js =====

===== FILE: server/rooms.js =====
// Room lifecycle: Lobby → Team selection → Ready check → Match → Results → Rematch.

import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import { PadelGame } from '../shared/game.js';
import { botInput, makeBotMemory } from '../shared/ai.js';
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS, SNAPSHOT_EVERY, SERVER_TICK } from '../shared/constants.js';
import { scoreView } from '../shared/scoring.js';
import { CAREER, starsFor } from '../shared/career.js';
import { sanitizeLook, COURT_IDS } from '../shared/looks.js';

const CODE_CHARS = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789';
const MAX_QUEUE = 10;
const RESULTS_DELAY = 4; // seconds of celebration before the results screen
const LOBBY_GRACE_MS = 60_000;
const EMPTY_ROOM_TTL_MS = 10 * 60_000;
export const PAUSE_REQUEST_MS = 10_000; // how long the others have to accept
export const PAUSE_MAX_MS = 30_000; // a pause never lasts longer than this
const PAUSES_PER_PLAYER = 2;

const cleanName = (n) => String(n ?? '').replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N} _.-]/gu, '').trim().slice(0, 16) || 'Guest';
const cleanText = (t) => String(t ?? '').replace(/[\u0000-\u001f<>]/g, '').trim().slice(0, 140);

export class Room {
  constructor(code, store, onEmpty) {
    this.code = code;
    this.store = store;
    this.onEmpty = onEmpty;
    this.members = new Map(); // id -> member
    this.slots = [null, null, null, null]; // {kind:'human', id} | {kind:'bot'} | null
    this.hostId = null;
    this.state = 'lobby';
    this.settings = { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, fillBots: true, public: false };
    this.career = null; // stage id when this room is a career match
    this.game = null;
    this.queues = [[], [], [], []];
    this.lastInput = [null, null, null, null];
    this.starved = [0, 0, 0, 0];
    this.botMem = [0, 1, 2, 3].map(makeBotMemory);
    this.resultAt = 0;
    this.persisted = false;
    this.emptySince = null;
    this.chatTimes = new Map();
    this.pause = null; // {phase:'request'|'active', by, accepts:Set, resumes:Set, expires|until}
    this.pausesUsed = new Map(); // member id -> pauses used this match
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ membership

  humanCount() {
    return [...this.members.values()].length;
  }

  addMember({ token, name, ws, look }) {
    const id = crypto.randomBytes(3).toString('hex');
    const m = { id, token, name: cleanName(name), ws, connected: true, ready: false, rtt: 0, dropTimer: null, look: look ? sanitizeLook(look) : null };
    this.members.set(id, m);
    if (!this.hostId) this.hostId = id;
    // take the first free slot, otherwise replace a bot
    const act = this.activeSlots();
    let slot = act.find((i) => this.slots[i] === null);
    if (slot === undefined) slot = act.find((i) => this.slots[i]?.kind === 'bot');
    this.slots[slot] = { kind: 'human', id };
    this.emptySince = null;
    return m;
  }

  activeSlots(mode = this.settings.mode) { return mode === 'singles' ? [0, 2] : [0, 1, 2, 3]; }

  slotOf(id) {
    return this.slots.findIndex((s) => s?.kind === 'human' && s.id === id);
  }

  canJoin() {
    if (this.state !== 'lobby') return 'Match already in progress';
    const free = this.activeSlots().some((i) => this.slots[i] === null || this.slots[i].kind === 'bot');
    if (!free) return 'Room is full';
    return null;
  }

  removeMember(id) {
    const m = this.members.get(id);
    if (!m) return;
    clearTimeout(m.dropTimer);
    this.members.delete(id);
    const slot = this.slotOf(id);
    if (slot >= 0) this.slots[slot] = this.state === 'match' ? { kind: 'bot' } : null;
    if (this.hostId === id) {
      const next = [...this.members.values()].find((x) => x.connected) || [...this.members.values()][0];
      this.hostId = next ? next.id : null;
    }
    if (this.members.size === 0) this.emptySince = Date.now();
    this.broadcastRoom();
  }

  disconnect(member) {
    member.connected = false;
    member.ws = null;
    member.ready = this.state === 'lobby' ? false : member.ready;
    if (this.hostId === member.id) {
      const next = [...this.members.values()].find((x) => x.connected);
      if (next) this.hostId = next.id;
    }
    // In a match a bot covers the slot immediately (see step()); in the lobby the seat is held briefly.
    if (this.state !== 'match') {
      member.dropTimer = setTimeout(() => this.removeMember(member.id), LOBBY_GRACE_MS);
      member.dropTimer.unref?.();
    }
    if (![...this.members.values()].some((x) => x.connected)) this.emptySince = Date.now();
    this.broadcastRoom();
  }

  reconnect(member, ws) {
    clearTimeout(member.dropTimer);
    member.ws = ws;
    member.connected = true;
    this.emptySince = null;
    this.queues[this.slotOf(member.id)] = [];
    this.broadcastRoom();
    if (this.state === 'match' && this.game) { this.sendStart(member); if (this.pause) this.send(member, this.pauseView()); }
    if (this.state === 'results' && this.lastResultsMsg) this.send(member, this.lastResultsMsg);
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ messaging

  send(member, msg) {
    if (member.ws && member.ws.readyState === 1) member.ws.send(typeof msg === 'string' ? msg : JSON.stringify(msg));
  }

  broadcast(msg) {
    const s = typeof msg === 'string' ? msg : JSON.stringify(msg);
    for (const m of this.members.values()) this.send(m, s);
  }

  roomView() {
    return {
      t: 'room',
      code: this.code,
      hostId: this.hostId,
      state: this.state,
      settings: this.settings,
      slots: this.slots.map((s) => {
        if (!s) return null;
        if (s.kind === 'bot') return { kind: 'bot', name: s.name || 'AI' };
        const m = this.members.get(s.id);
        return { kind: 'human', id: m.id, name: m.name, ready: m.ready || m.id === this.hostId, connected: m.connected, rtt: m.rtt, look: m.look };
      }),
    };
  }

  broadcastRoom() {
    const v = this.roomView();
    for (const m of this.members.values()) this.send(m, { ...v, you: m.id, youSlot: this.slotOf(m.id) });
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ lobby actions

  isHost(m) { return m.id === this.hostId; }

  sit(m, slot) {
    if (this.state !== 'lobby') return 'Teams are locked during a match';
    if (!this.activeSlots().includes(slot)) return 'Bad slot';
    const cur = this.slotOf(m.id);
    if (cur === slot) return null;
    const target = this.slots[slot];
    if (target?.kind === 'human') return 'That seat is taken';
    this.slots[cur] = null;
    this.slots[slot] = { kind: 'human', id: m.id };
    m.ready = false;
    return null;
  }

  assign(host, id, slot) {
    if (!this.isHost(host)) return 'Only the host can move players';
    if (this.state !== 'lobby') return 'Teams are locked during a match';
    const m = this.members.get(id);
    if (!m || !this.activeSlots().includes(slot)) return 'Bad request';
    const cur = this.slotOf(id);
    const target = this.slots[slot];
    this.slots[cur] = target?.kind === 'human' ? target : null; // swap with whoever sits there
    this.slots[slot] = { kind: 'human', id };
    for (const x of this.members.values()) x.ready = false;
    return null;
  }

  setSettings(host, s) {
    if (!this.isHost(host)) return 'Only the host can change settings';
    if (this.state !== 'lobby') return 'Settings are locked during a match';
    const n = { ...this.settings };
    if ([1, 3, 5].includes(s.sets)) n.sets = s.sets;
    if ([2, 4, 6].includes(s.gamesPerSet)) n.gamesPerSet = s.gamesPerSet;
    if (typeof s.goldenPoint === 'boolean') n.goldenPoint = s.goldenPoint;
    if (['easy', 'normal', 'hard', 'expert'].includes(s.difficulty)) n.difficulty = s.difficulty;
    if (typeof s.fillBots === 'boolean') n.fillBots = s.fillBots;
    if (typeof s.public === 'boolean') n.public = s.public;
    if (COURT_IDS.includes(s.court)) n.court = s.court;
    if ((s.mode === 'singles' || s.mode === 'doubles') && s.mode !== n.mode) {
      const err = this.applyMode(s.mode);
      if (err) return err;
      n.mode = s.mode;
    }
    this.settings = n;
    return null;
  }

  /** Switching to 1v1 moves everybody into the two singles seats (or refuses if there are too many humans). */
  applyMode(mode) {
    if (mode === 'singles') {
      const humans = [1, 3].map((i) => this.slots[i]).filter((s) => s?.kind === 'human');
      const free = [0, 2].filter((i) => this.slots[i]?.kind !== 'human');
      if (humans.length > free.length) return 'Too many players for 1v1 (max 2)';
      for (const i of [1, 3]) {
        const s = this.slots[i];
        if (s?.kind === 'human') this.slots[free.shift()] = s;
        this.slots[i] = null;
      }
      for (const i of [0, 2]) if (this.slots[i]?.kind === 'bot') this.slots[i] = null;
    }
    for (const m of this.members.values()) m.ready = false;
    return null;
  }

  canStart() {
    if (this.state !== 'lobby') return 'Not in the lobby';
    const act = this.activeSlots();
    const filled = act.filter((i) => this.slots[i]).length;
    if (filled < act.length && !this.settings.fillBots) return 'Need four players (or enable AI fill)';
    const notReady = [...this.members.values()].filter((m) => !this.isHost(m) && !m.ready && m.connected);
    if (notReady.length) return `${notReady.map((m) => m.name).join(', ')} not ready`;
    // team balance: each team needs at least one human or a bot to fill
    return null;
  }

  start(host) {
    if (!this.isHost(host)) return 'Only the host can start';
    const err = this.canStart();
    if (err) return err;
    this.beginMatch();
    return null;
  }

  beginMatch() {
    const act = this.activeSlots();
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) this.slots[i] = act.includes(i) ? this.slots[i] || { kind: 'bot' } : null;
    this.state = 'match';
    this.game = new PadelGame({ settings: this.settings });
    this.queues = [[], [], [], []];
    this.lastInput = [null, null, null, null];
    this.starved = [0, 0, 0, 0];
    this.botMem = [0, 1, 2, 3].map(makeBotMemory);
    this.persisted = false;
    this.pause = null; this.pausesUsed = new Map();
    this.resultAt = 0;
    this.lastResultsMsg = null;
    for (const m of this.members.values()) { m.ready = false; m.rematch = false; }
    this.broadcastRoom();
    for (const m of this.members.values()) this.sendStart(m);
  }

  sendStart(m) {
    const names = this.slots.map((s) => (s?.kind === 'human' ? this.members.get(s.id).name : s?.name || 'AI'));
    this.send(m, {
      t: 'start', slot: this.slotOf(m.id), names, settings: this.settings,
      looks: this.slots.map((s, i) => sanitizeLook(s?.kind === 'human' ? this.members.get(s.id)?.look : s?.look, i)),
      score: scoreView(this.game.match), tickRate: SERVER_TICK,
    });
  }

  rematch(m) {
    if (this.state !== 'results') return 'No finished match';
    m.rematch = true;
    const waiting = [...this.members.values()].filter((x) => x.connected && !x.rematch);
    this.broadcast({ t: 'rematchVotes', ready: [...this.members.values()].filter((x) => x.rematch).map((x) => x.id) });
    if (waiting.length === 0) this.beginMatch();
    return null;
  }

  /** Career: configure the room from a stage (named bots with their own levels) and start at once. */
  setupCareer(stage, member) {
    this.career = stage.id;
    this.settings = {
      ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, fillBots: true, public: false, mode: stage.mode, difficulty: stage.difficulty, court: stage.court || 'indoor',
      sets: stage.sets, gamesPerSet: stage.gamesPerSet, goldenPoint: !!stage.golden,
    };
    const bot = (name, diff, look) => ({ kind: 'bot', name, diff, look });
    this.slots = [{ kind: 'human', id: member.id }, null, null, null];
    if (stage.mode === 'doubles') {
      this.slots[1] = bot(stage.partner, stage.partnerDiff || stage.difficulty, stage.looks?.[0]);
      this.slots[2] = bot(stage.opponents[0], stage.difficulty, stage.looks?.[1]);
      this.slots[3] = bot(stage.opponents[1], stage.difficulty, stage.looks?.[2]);
    } else {
      this.slots[2] = bot(stage.opponents[0], stage.difficulty, stage.looks?.[0]);
    }
    this.beginMatch();
  }

  toLobby(host) {
    if (this.career) return 'Career matches have no lobby';
    if (!this.isHost(host)) return 'Only the host can return to the lobby';
    if (this.state === 'match') return 'Match in progress';
    this.state = 'lobby';
    this.game = null;
    for (const m of this.members.values()) { m.ready = false; m.rematch = false; }
    this.broadcastRoom();
    return null;
  }

  chat(m, text) {
    const now = Date.now();
    const last = this.chatTimes.get(m.id) || 0;
    if (now - last < 400) return;
    this.chatTimes.set(m.id, now);
    const t = cleanText(text);
    if (t) this.broadcast({ t: 'chat', id: m.id, name: m.name, slot: this.slotOf(m.id), text: t });
  }

  emote(m, id) {
    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id < 0 || id > 7) return;
    this.broadcast({ t: 'emote', slot: this.slotOf(m.id), id });
  }

  pushInputs(m, inputs) {
    if (this.state !== 'match' || !Array.isArray(inputs)) return;
    const slot = this.slotOf(m.id);
    if (slot < 0) return;
    const q = this.queues[slot];
    for (const inp of inputs.slice(0, 8)) {
      if (!inp || typeof inp !== 'object') continue;
      q.push(inp);
    }
    // never let lag build an unbounded backlog; keep swing/jump intents when trimming
    while (q.length > MAX_QUEUE) {
      const d = q.shift();
      if (d.swing) q[0].swing = true;
      if (d.jump) q[0].jump = true;
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ pause (online)
  // One player asks, the other humans accept. The game then stops for at most 30 s, or until every human presses Resume.

  connectedHumans() {
    return [...this.members.values()].filter((m) => m.connected && this.slotOf(m.id) >= 0);
  }

  pauseView(extra = {}) {
    const p = this.pause;
    if (!p) return { t: 'pause', phase: 'off', ...extra };
    const name = (id) => this.members.get(id)?.name || '?';
    return {
      t: 'pause', phase: p.phase, by: name(p.by), byId: p.by, expires: p.expires, until: p.until, now: Date.now(),
      accepted: [...p.accepts].map(name), resumed: [...(p.resumes || [])].map(name), resumedIds: [...(p.resumes || [])], humans: this.connectedHumans().map((m) => m.id), ...extra,
    };
  }

  /** kind: 'req' | 'accept' | 'deny' | 'resume'. Returns an error message or null. */
  pauseCtl(m, kind, now = Date.now()) {
    if (this.state !== 'match' || !this.game || this.game.phase === 'ended') return 'There is no match to pause';
    if (this.slotOf(m.id) < 0) return 'Only players can pause';
    const p = this.pause;
    if (kind === 'req') {
      if (p) return p.phase === 'active' ? 'Already paused' : 'A pause was already requested';
      if ((this.pausesUsed.get(m.id) || 0) >= PAUSES_PER_PLAYER) return 'You have used your pauses for this match';
      this.pause = { phase: 'request', by: m.id, accepts: new Set([m.id]), resumes: new Set(), expires: now + PAUSE_REQUEST_MS };
      this.pauseEvaluate(now);
      if (this.pause?.phase === 'request') this.broadcast(this.pauseView());
      return null;
    }
    if (!p) return null;
    if (kind === 'accept' && p.phase === 'request' && m.id !== p.by) { p.accepts.add(m.id); this.pauseEvaluate(now); if (this.pause?.phase === 'request') this.broadcast(this.pauseView()); return null; }
    if (kind === 'deny' && p.phase === 'request' && m.id !== p.by) { this.pause = null; this.broadcast({ t: 'pause', phase: 'denied', by: m.name }); return null; }
    if (kind === 'resume' && p.phase === 'active') { p.resumes.add(m.id); this.pauseEvaluate(now); if (this.pause) this.broadcast(this.pauseView()); return null; }
    return null;
  }

  /** Move the pause forward: accepted by everybody, resumed by everybody, or timed out. */
  pauseEvaluate(now = Date.now()) {
    const p = this.pause;
    if (!p) return;
    const humans = this.connectedHumans();
    if (p.phase === 'request') {
      if (now >= p.expires) { this.pause = null; this.broadcast({ t: 'pause', phase: 'denied', by: '', expired: true }); return; }
      if (humans.every((h) => p.accepts.has(h.id))) {
        p.phase = 'active'; p.until = now + PAUSE_MAX_MS; p.resumes = new Set();
        this.pausesUsed.set(p.by, (this.pausesUsed.get(p.by) || 0) + 1);
        this.broadcast(this.pauseView());
      }
      return;
    }
    if (now >= p.until || humans.every((h) => p.resumes.has(h.id))) {
      this.pause = null;
      for (let s = 0; s < 4; s++) { this.queues[s] = []; this.lastInput[s] = null; this.starved[s] = 0; } // nothing stale carries over
      this.broadcast({ t: 'pause', phase: 'off' });
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ simulation

  step(dt) {
    const g = this.game;
    if (!g) return;
    if (this.pause) { this.pauseEvaluate(); if (this.pause?.phase === 'active') return; } // the match is frozen while paused
    for (let s = 0; s < 4; s++) {
      if (!g.players[s].active) continue;
      const slot = this.slots[s];
      const m = slot?.kind === 'human' ? this.members.get(slot.id) : null;
      g.players[s].human = !!(m && m.connected); // bots read this to play sensibly with a person as partner
      if (m && m.connected) {
        const q = this.queues[s];
        let inp = q.shift();
        if (inp) {
          this.lastInput[s] = inp;
          this.starved[s] = 0;
          g.players[s].ack = Number.isInteger(inp.seq) ? inp.seq : g.players[s].ack;
        } else if (this.lastInput[s]) {
          // no fresh input (lag spike or hidden tab): repeat briefly, then stand still
          const stale = ++this.starved[s] > 15;
          inp = { ...this.lastInput[s], swing: false, jump: false, ...(stale ? { mx: 0, mz: 0, sprint: false } : {}) };
        }
        if (inp) g.applyInput(s, inp);
      } else {
        g.applyInput(s, botInput(g, s, this.botMem[s], slot?.diff || this.settings.difficulty, dt));
      }
    }
    g.tick(dt);
    const evs = g.drainEvents();
    if (evs.length) {
      this.broadcast({ t: 'ev', ev: evs });
      const done = evs.find((e) => e.e === 'match');
      if (done) this.finishMatch(done);
    }
    if (g.tickNo % SNAPSHOT_EVERY === 0) this.broadcast(g.snapshot());
    if (this.resultAt && g.time >= this.resultAt && this.state === 'match') {
      this.state = 'results';
      this.broadcastRoom();
      this.broadcast(this.lastResultsMsg);
    }
  }

  finishMatch(ev) {
    if (this.persisted) return;
    this.persisted = true;
    const humans = [];
    for (let s = 0; s < 4; s++) {
      const slot = this.slots[s];
      if (slot?.kind === 'human') {
        const m = this.members.get(slot.id);
        if (m) humans.push({ token: m.token, id: m.id, name: m.name, slot: s, team: s >> 1 });
      }
    }
    const gains = this.store.recordMatch({ code: this.code, humans, results: ev.results, settings: this.settings });
const names = this.slots.map((s) => (s?.kind === 'human' ? this.members.get(s.id)?.name : s?.name || 'AI'));
    const progress = {};
    for (const h of humans) progress[h.id] = gains[h.token];
    let career = null;
    const stage = this.career && CAREER.find((c) => c.id === this.career);
    if (stage && humans[0]) {
      const stars = starsFor(ev.results);
      const c = this.store.careerComplete(humans[0].token, stage.id, stars);
      career = { stage: stage.id, name: stage.name, won: stars > 0, stars, bonusXp: c.bonusXp, unlocked: c.unlocked, hasNext: stage.id < CAREER.length };
    }
    this.lastResultsMsg = JSON.stringify({ t: 'results', results: ev.results, names, progress, career });
    this.resultAt = this.game.time + RESULTS_DELAY;
  }

  expired(now) {
    return this.emptySince !== null && now - this.emptySince > EMPTY_ROOM_TTL_MS;
  }
}

export class RoomManager {
  constructor(store) {
    this.store = store;
    this.rooms = new Map();
    this.byToken = new Map(); // token -> {room, id}
  }

  newCode() {
    for (;;) {
      let c = '';
      for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) c += CODE_CHARS[crypto.randomInt(CODE_CHARS.length)];
      if (!this.rooms.has(c)) return c;
    }
  }

  create({ token, name, ws, look }) {
    const room = new Room(this.newCode(), this.store);
    this.rooms.set(room.code, room);
    const m = room.addMember({ token, name, ws, look });
    this.byToken.set(token, { room, id: m.id });
    return { room, member: m };
  }

  join(code, { token, name, ws, look }) {
    const room = this.rooms.get(String(code || '').toUpperCase().trim());
    if (!room) return { error: 'Room not found. Check the code and try again.' };
    const err = room.canJoin();
    if (err) return { error: err };
    const m = room.addMember({ token, name, ws, look });
    this.byToken.set(token, { room, id: m.id });
    return { room, member: m };
  }

  /** Open lobbies that chose to be public and still have a free seat. */
  listPublic() {
    const out = [];
    for (const r of this.rooms.values()) {
      if (r.state !== 'lobby' || !r.settings.public || r.career || r.canJoin()) continue;
      const host = r.members.get(r.hostId);
      const act = r.activeSlots();
      out.push({
        code: r.code, host: host ? host.name : '?', mode: r.settings.mode, difficulty: r.settings.difficulty,
        players: act.filter((i) => r.slots[i]?.kind === 'human').length, max: act.length,
      });
    }
    return out.slice(0, 30);
  }

  /** The room/member this guest identity currently belongs to, if still alive. */
  membership(token) {
    const e = this.byToken.get(token);
    if (!e) return null;
    const member = e.room.members.get(e.id);
    if (!member || !this.rooms.has(e.room.code)) { this.byToken.delete(token); return null; }
    return { room: e.room, member };
  }

  leave(token) {
    const ms = this.membership(token);
    if (!ms) return;
    this.byToken.delete(token);
    ms.room.removeMember(ms.member.id);
  }

  step(dt) {
    for (const r of this.rooms.values()) if (r.state === 'match') r.step(dt);
  }

  sweep(now = Date.now()) {
    for (const [code, r] of this.rooms) {
      if (r.expired(now) || (r.members.size === 0 && r.emptySince !== null && now - r.emptySince > 30_000)) {
        for (const m of r.members.values()) this.byToken.delete(m.token);
        this.rooms.delete(code);
      }
    }
  }
}
===== END FILE: server/rooms.js =====

===== FILE: server/store.js =====
// File-backed persistence (guest profiles + finished matches).
// This is a deliberately small stand-in for PostgreSQL: the Store interface
// (profile / recordMatch) is all the rest of the server touches, so a SQL
// implementation can replace this file without changing anything else.

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import { CAREER } from '../shared/career.js';

const MAX_MATCHES = 500;
const MAX_HISTORY = 20;

export const levelFor = (xp) => Math.floor(Math.sqrt(xp / 100)) + 1;

export class Store {
  constructor(dir) {
    this.dir = dir;
    this.file = path.join(dir, 'store.json');
    this.data = { profiles: {}, matches: [] };
    this.timer = null;
    fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
    try {
      this.data = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(this.file, 'utf8'));
    } catch {
      /* first run or unreadable file: start empty */
    }
  }

  newToken() {
    return crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex');
  }

  /** Create the profile if needed and return it. */
  profile(token, name) {
    let p = this.data.profiles[token];
    if (!p) {
      p = { name: name || 'Guest', xp: 0, matches: 0, wins: 0, losses: 0, createdAt: Date.now(), history: [] };
      this.data.profiles[token] = p;
      this.scheduleSave();
    } else if (name && name !== p.name) {
      p.name = name;
      this.scheduleSave();
    }
    return p;
  }

  hasProfile(token) {
    return !!this.data.profiles[token];
  }

  publicProfile(token) {
    const p = this.data.profiles[token];
    if (!p) return null;
    return { name: p.name, xp: p.xp, level: levelFor(p.xp), matches: p.matches, wins: p.wins, losses: p.losses, history: p.history };
  }

  /** Career ladder for a profile: every stage with its stars and whether it is unlocked. */
  careerView(token) {
    const p = this.profile(token);
    const done = p.career?.done || {};
    const stages = CAREER.map((s) => ({ ...s, stars: done[s.id] || 0, unlocked: s.id === 1 || (done[s.id - 1] || 0) >= 1 }));
    return { stages, stars: stages.reduce((a, s) => a + s.stars, 0), max: CAREER.length * 3 };
  }

  /** Record a career result (stars 0 = lost). Bonus XP is paid only for improving your best stars on a stage. */
  careerComplete(token, stageId, stars) {
    const p = this.profile(token);
    const stage = CAREER.find((s) => s.id === stageId);
    p.career ||= { done: {} };
    const prev = p.career.done[stageId] || 0;
    let bonusXp = 0, unlocked = null;
    if (stage && stars > prev) {
      p.career.done[stageId] = stars;
      bonusXp = stage.xp * (stars - prev);
      p.xp += bonusXp;
      if (prev === 0 && CAREER.some((s) => s.id === stageId + 1)) unlocked = stageId + 1;
    }
    this.scheduleSave();
    return { stars, prev, bonusXp, unlocked, level: levelFor(p.xp) };
  }

  /**
   * Persist a finished match. `humans` = [{token, name, slot, team}].
   * Only the server calls this, from its own simulation's result.
   */
  recordMatch({ code, humans, results, settings }) {
    const entry = {
      at: Date.now(), code, settings,
      winner: results.winner, score: results.score.sets, duration: results.duration,
      players: humans.map((h) => ({ name: h.name, team: h.team })),
    };
    this.data.matches.push(entry);
    if (this.data.matches.length > MAX_MATCHES) this.data.matches.splice(0, this.data.matches.length - MAX_MATCHES);
    const gains = {};
    for (const h of humans) {
      const p = this.profile(h.token, h.name);
      const won = h.team === results.winner;
      const gain = 40 + (won ? 60 : 0);
      p.xp += gain;
      p.matches++;
      if (won) p.wins++; else p.losses++;
      p.history.unshift({
        at: entry.at, won, score: results.score.sets.map((s) => `${s[0]}-${s[1]}`).join(' '),
        team: h.team, duration: results.duration,
      });
      if (p.history.length > MAX_HISTORY) p.history.length = MAX_HISTORY;
      gains[h.token] = { xp: gain, level: levelFor(p.xp), totalXp: p.xp };
    }
    this.scheduleSave();
    return gains;
  }

  scheduleSave() {
    if (this.timer) return;
    this.timer = setTimeout(() => { this.timer = null; this.flush(); }, 400);
    this.timer.unref?.();
  }

  flush() {
    if (this.timer) { clearTimeout(this.timer); this.timer = null; }
    const tmp = `${this.file}.tmp`;
    try {
      fs.writeFileSync(tmp, JSON.stringify(this.data));
      fs.renameSync(tmp, this.file);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[store] write failed:', err.message);
    }
  }
}
===== END FILE: server/store.js =====

===== FILE: client/js/audio.js =====
// Audio engine. Every sound is synthesised with the Web Audio API (no sample files are shipped), but it is layered and
// contextual: each shot type has its own body, contact quality changes the character, glass and mesh sound different,
// every court has its own room (reverb) and ambience, and repeated sounds are varied and rate-limited.
// The context is only created after a user gesture (unlock()).
import { settings } from './settings.js';

// Per shot: bp = bright "pop" band (Hz, Q), body = low thump (Hz), len = pop length (s), dull = low-passed (bandeja/block)
const SHOTS = {
  forehand: { bp: 2000, q: 1.2, body: 300, len: 0.07, vol: 0.5 },
  backhand: { bp: 1800, q: 1.2, body: 280, len: 0.07, vol: 0.5 },
  volley: { bp: 2600, q: 1.5, body: 340, len: 0.045, vol: 0.45 },
  smash: { bp: 1400, q: 0.9, body: 170, len: 0.13, vol: 0.85, boom: true },
  bandeja: { bp: 1700, q: 1.0, body: 260, len: 0.08, vol: 0.45, dull: true },
  vibora: { bp: 2400, q: 1.1, body: 300, len: 0.08, vol: 0.62, whoosh: true },
  slice: { bp: 2200, q: 1.3, body: 330, len: 0.06, vol: 0.4, brush: true },
  serve: { bp: 1900, q: 1.1, body: 290, len: 0.07, vol: 0.5 },
  lob: { bp: 1500, q: 1.0, body: 250, len: 0.09, vol: 0.4, dull: true },
  chiquita: { bp: 1300, q: 0.9, body: 230, len: 0.06, vol: 0.32, dull: true },
  drop: { bp: 1200, q: 0.9, body: 220, len: 0.05, vol: 0.28, dull: true },
  block: { bp: 900, q: 0.8, body: 200, len: 0.05, vol: 0.38, dull: true },
};

// How each court sounds: reverb mix and the ambience layers
const ROOMS = {
  indoor: { wet: 0.22, decay: 1.8, amb: 'hall' },
  outdoor: { wet: 0.04, decay: 0.5, amb: 'outdoor' },
  night: { wet: 0.3, decay: 2.4, amb: 'crowd' },
  rooftop: { wet: 0.08, decay: 0.7, amb: 'city' },
};

const rr = (a, b) => a + Math.random() * (b - a);

export class Sound {
  constructor() {
    this.ctx = null;
    this.ambNodes = [];
    this.ambTimers = [];
    this.last = new Map();
    this.court = 'indoor';
    this.stepFlip = false;
  }

  /** Must be called from a user gesture before anything is audible. */
  unlock() {
    if (!this.ctx) {
      const AC = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
      if (!AC) return;
      this.ctx = new AC();
      const c = this.ctx;
      this.master = c.createGain();
      const comp = c.createDynamicsCompressor();
      this.master.connect(comp).connect(c.destination);
      this.sfx = c.createGain(); this.sfx.connect(this.master);
      this.amb = c.createGain(); this.amb.connect(this.master);
      this.musicGain = c.createGain(); this.musicGain.connect(this.master);
      this.musicSend = c.createGain(); this.musicSend.gain.value = 0.55; // music goes through the room reverb as well: softer, less synthetic
      this.musicGain.connect(this.musicSend);
      this.reverb = c.createConvolver();
      this.wet = c.createGain();
      this.sfx.connect(this.reverb); this.reverb.connect(this.wet); this.wet.connect(this.master);
      this.musicSend.connect(this.reverb);
      this.noiseBuf = this.makeNoise();
      this.setCourt(this.court);
    }
    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume();
    this.applyVolume();
  }

  applyVolume() {
    if (!this.ctx) return;
    this.master.gain.value = settings.master;
    this.sfx.gain.value = settings.sfx;
    this.amb.gain.value = settings.ambience;
    this.musicGain.gain.value = (settings.music ?? 0.35) * 0.6;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ music
  // A small generative loop (chord pad, bass, plucked arpeggio and, in matches, a light beat). Everything is synthesised
  // with Web Audio, so there are no audio files; it is quiet by default and has its own volume.

  startMusic(kind = 'menu') {
    if (!this.ctx) return;
    if (this.musicKind === kind && this.musicTimer) return;
    this.stopMusic();
    this.musicKind = kind;
    const bpm = kind === 'match' ? 92 : 72;
    this.m = { step: 0, next: this.ctx.currentTime + 0.1, spb: 60 / bpm / 4 };
    this.musicTimer = setInterval(() => this.musicTick(), 60);
  }

  stopMusic() {
    clearInterval(this.musicTimer); this.musicTimer = null; this.musicKind = null;
  }

  musicTick() {
    const c = this.ctx;
    if (!c || c.state !== 'running') { if (this.m) this.m.next = c ? c.currentTime + 0.1 : 0; return; }
    const m = this.m;
    while (m.next < c.currentTime + 0.25) {
      this.musicStep(m.step, m.next, m.spb);
      m.step++; m.next += m.spb;
    }
  }

  musicStep(step, t, spb) {
    const match = this.musicKind === 'match';
    const c = this.ctx;
    const bar = Math.floor(step / 16) % 8, s = step % 16;
    // I – vi – ii – V (Cmaj7, Am7, Dm7, G7), repeated: smooth voice leading, nothing harsh
    const chords = [[48, 52, 55, 59], [45, 52, 55, 60], [50, 53, 57, 60], [43, 53, 55, 59]];
    const ch = chords[bar % 4];
    const hz = (n) => 440 * 2 ** ((n - 69) / 12);
    const swing = s % 2 === 1 ? spb * 0.18 : 0; // a little lazy swing on the off-beats
    const env = (g, start, atk, dur, vol) => {
      g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, start);
      g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(vol, start + atk);
      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, start + dur);
    };
    const voice = (type, f, start, atk, dur, vol, lp) => {
      const o = c.createOscillator(); o.type = type; o.frequency.value = f;
      const g = c.createGain(); env(g, start, atk, dur, vol);
      let n = o;
      if (lp) { const fl = c.createBiquadFilter(); fl.type = 'lowpass'; fl.frequency.value = lp; fl.Q.value = 0.3; o.connect(fl); n = fl; }
      n.connect(g).connect(this.musicGain);
      o.start(start); o.stop(start + dur + 0.1);
    };
    if (s === 0) { // warm pad: slow attack, long release, two slightly detuned sines per note
      for (const n of ch) {
        voice('sine', hz(n + 12) * 0.997, t, 0.9, spb * 17, 0.045, 1400);
        voice('triangle', hz(n + 12) * 1.003, t, 0.9, spb * 17, 0.02, 900);
      }
    }
    // bass: root on 1 and the fifth on 3
    if (s === 0 || s === 8 || (match && s === 11)) voice('sine', hz(ch[0] - 12 + (s === 8 ? 7 : 0)), t + swing, 0.01, spb * 3.2, 0.2, 300);
    // electric-piano style melody (C major pentatonic), sparse, with rests, changing every two bars
    const penta = [60, 62, 64, 67, 69, 72, 74, 76];
    const phrases = [
      [-1, -1, 4, -1, -1, 3, -1, 2, -1, -1, 3, -1, 1, -1, -1, -1],
      [2, -1, -1, 3, -1, -1, 4, -1, 5, -1, -1, 4, -1, -1, 2, -1],
      [-1, 0, -1, 2, -1, -1, 3, -1, -1, 4, -1, 3, -1, -1, -1, -1],
      [4, -1, -1, 5, -1, 4, -1, -1, 3, -1, 2, -1, -1, -1, 1, -1],
    ];
    const ph = phrases[Math.floor(bar / 2) % 4][s];
    if (ph >= 0 && (match || (bar % 2 === 0))) {
      const n = penta[ph];
      voice('sine', hz(n), t + swing, 0.005, spb * 3, 0.07, 2600);
      voice('sine', hz(n) * 2, t + swing, 0.005, spb * 1.2, 0.02, 4000); // bell-like upper partial that fades fast
    }
    if (match) {
      if (s % 8 === 0) { // soft kick
        const o = c.createOscillator(); o.type = 'sine';
        o.frequency.setValueAtTime(105, t); o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(44, t + 0.1);
        const g = c.createGain(); g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.32, t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.2);
        o.connect(g).connect(this.musicGain); o.start(t); o.stop(t + 0.22);
      }
      if (s === 4 || s === 12) { // rim-like snap instead of a snare
        const src = c.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = this.noiseBuf;
        const f = c.createBiquadFilter(); f.type = 'bandpass'; f.frequency.value = 1800; f.Q.value = 1.4;
        const g = c.createGain(); g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.08, t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.07);
        src.connect(f).connect(g).connect(this.musicGain); src.start(t, Math.random()); src.stop(t + 0.08);
      }
      if (s % 2 === 0) { // quiet shaker
        const src = c.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = this.noiseBuf;
        const f = c.createBiquadFilter(); f.type = 'highpass'; f.frequency.value = 6500;
        const g = c.createGain(); g.gain.setValueAtTime(s % 4 === 0 ? 0.028 : 0.016, t + swing); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + swing + 0.05);
        src.connect(f).connect(g).connect(this.musicGain); src.start(t + swing, Math.random()); src.stop(t + swing + 0.06);
      }
    }
  }

  makeNoise() {
    const len = this.ctx.sampleRate * 2;
    const b = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, len, this.ctx.sampleRate);
    const d = b.getChannelData(0);
    for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) d[i] = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
    return b;
  }

  /** The room: a generated impulse response (decaying noise) whose length and mix depend on the court. */
  setCourt(id) {
    this.court = ROOMS[id] ? id : 'indoor';
    if (!this.ctx) return;
    const room = ROOMS[this.court];
    const len = Math.floor(this.ctx.sampleRate * room.decay);
    const ir = this.ctx.createBuffer(2, len, this.ctx.sampleRate);
    for (let ch = 0; ch < 2; ch++) {
      const d = ir.getChannelData(ch);
      for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) d[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.pow(1 - i / len, 3);
    }
    this.reverb.buffer = ir;
    this.wet.gain.value = room.wet;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ ambience

  startAmbience(courtId) {
    if (!this.ctx) return;
    this.stopAmbience();
    this.setCourt(courtId || this.court);
    const c = this.ctx;
    const layer = (freq, q, type, gain, lfoHz = 0, lfoDepth = 0) => {
      const src = c.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = this.noiseBuf; src.loop = true;
      const f = c.createBiquadFilter(); f.type = type; f.frequency.value = freq; f.Q.value = q;
      const g = c.createGain(); g.gain.value = gain;
      src.connect(f).connect(g).connect(this.amb);
      src.start();
      this.ambNodes.push(src);
      if (lfoHz) {
        const lfo = c.createOscillator(); lfo.frequency.value = lfoHz;
        const d = c.createGain(); d.gain.value = lfoDepth;
        lfo.connect(d).connect(g.gain); lfo.start(); this.ambNodes.push(lfo);
      }
    };
    const kind = ROOMS[this.court].amb;
    if (kind === 'hall') { layer(200, 0.5, 'lowpass', 0.07); layer(520, 0.7, 'bandpass', 0.05, 0.13, 0.02); }
    if (kind === 'outdoor') {
      layer(420, 0.6, 'bandpass', 0.07, 0.11, 0.05); // wind swelling
      layer(1600, 0.4, 'highpass', 0.012);
      this.ambTimers.push(setInterval(() => this.bird(), 4500)); // birdsong now and then
    }
    if (kind === 'city') {
      layer(160, 0.6, 'lowpass', 0.09, 0.08, 0.03); // distant traffic
      layer(900, 0.5, 'bandpass', 0.02, 0.2, 0.012); // city murmur
      layer(2400, 0.4, 'highpass', 0.008);
    }
    if (kind === 'crowd') {
      layer(700, 0.6, 'bandpass', 0.13, 0.17, 0.05); // crowd murmur with slow swells
      layer(300, 0.5, 'lowpass', 0.06);
      layer(5200, 4, 'bandpass', 0.008, 7, 0.006); // night insects
    }
  }

  stopAmbience() {
    for (const n of this.ambNodes) { try { n.stop(); } catch { /* already stopped */ } }
    this.ambNodes = [];
    this.ambTimers.forEach(clearInterval);
    this.ambTimers = [];
  }

  bird() {
    if (!this.ctx || Math.random() < 0.4) return;
    const n = 2 + (Math.random() * 3 | 0), base = rr(2800, 4200);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) this.tone(base * rr(0.9, 1.2), 0.07, { vol: 0.03, slide: rr(300, 900), delay: i * 0.1, bus: this.amb });
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ building blocks

  out(pan, bus) {
    const dest = bus || this.sfx;
    if (!this.ctx.createStereoPanner || !pan) return dest;
    const p = this.ctx.createStereoPanner(); p.pan.value = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, pan)); p.connect(dest);
    return p;
  }

  tone(freq, dur, { type = 'sine', vol = 0.3, slide = 0, delay = 0, pan = 0, bus = null } = {}) {
    const c = this.ctx, t = c.currentTime + delay;
    const o = c.createOscillator(); o.type = type;
    o.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, t);
    if (slide) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(20, freq + slide), t + dur);
    const g = c.createGain();
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, t);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
    o.connect(g).connect(this.out(pan, bus));
    o.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
  }

  burst(freq, q, dur, { vol = 0.4, type = 'bandpass', delay = 0, pan = 0, sweep = 0 } = {}) {
    const c = this.ctx, t = c.currentTime + delay;
    const s = c.createBufferSource(); s.buffer = this.noiseBuf;
    const f = c.createBiquadFilter(); f.type = type; f.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, t); f.Q.value = q;
    if (sweep) f.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(60, freq + sweep), t + dur);
    const g = c.createGain();
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, t);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
    s.connect(f).connect(g).connect(this.out(pan));
    s.start(t, Math.random()); s.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
  }

  gate(name, ms) {
    const now = performance.now();
    if (now - (this.last.get(name) || 0) < ms) return false;
    this.last.set(name, now);
    return true;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ gameplay sounds

  /** A racket hit. o: {kind, label, power, pan}. Shot type picks the body, contact quality the character. */
  hit(o = {}) {
    const S = SHOTS[o.kind] || SHOTS.forehand;
    const pan = o.pan || 0;
    const v = S.vol * (o.power ?? 1) * rr(0.9, 1.1);
    const k = rr(0.94, 1.06); // pitch variation so rallies do not sound like a loop
    const bad = o.label === 'off-centre' || o.label === 'poor';
    const bp = S.bp * k * (bad ? 0.55 : 1);
    this.burst(bp, S.q, S.len * (bad ? 1.3 : 1), { vol: v, pan, type: S.dull ? 'lowpass' : 'bandpass' });
    this.tone(S.body * k, 0.1, { vol: v * 0.7, slide: -S.body * 0.4, pan });
    if (S.boom) this.tone(90, 0.22, { vol: v * 0.5, slide: -40, pan });
    if (S.whoosh) this.burst(1800, 0.7, 0.16, { vol: v * 0.3, pan, sweep: -1000, delay: -0.0 });
    if (S.brush) this.burst(4200, 1.5, 0.05, { vol: v * 0.3, pan, type: 'highpass' });
    if (bad) this.tone(700 * k, 0.09, { vol: v * 0.35, type: 'triangle', pan }); // hollow frame sound
    else if (o.label === 'perfect') this.tone(3400 * k, 0.05, { vol: v * 0.12, pan }); // clean, bright ring
  }

  play(name, o = {}) {
    if (!this.ctx) return;
    const { pan = 0, power = 1 } = o;
    switch (name) {
      case 'hit': if (this.gate('hit', 25)) this.hit(o); break;
      case 'smash': if (this.gate('hit', 25)) this.hit({ ...o, kind: 'smash' }); break;
      case 'floor':
        if (!this.gate('floor', 30)) break;
        this.tone(rr(135, 175) + 55 * power, 0.09, { vol: 0.35 * power, slide: -60, pan });
        this.burst(500, 0.6, 0.05, { vol: 0.15, pan });
        break;
      case 'wall': // glass rings, the mesh above 3 m rattles
        if (!this.gate('wall', 40)) break;
        if (o.mesh) { this.burst(2800, 0.8, 0.18, { vol: 0.28 * power, pan, type: 'highpass' }); this.tone(420, 0.1, { vol: 0.08, type: 'triangle', pan }); } else {
          this.burst(3600, 2, 0.09, { vol: 0.35 * power, pan, type: 'highpass' });
          this.tone(rr(900, 1050), 0.14, { vol: 0.12 * power, type: 'triangle', pan });
          this.tone(1800, 0.1, { vol: 0.05 * power, pan, delay: 0.01 });
        }
        break;
      case 'net': this.burst(260, 0.7, 0.14, { vol: 0.4, pan }); this.burst(3000, 1, 0.05, { vol: 0.08, pan, type: 'highpass' }); break;
      case 'step':
        if (!this.gate('step' + (o.i ?? 0), 90)) break;
        this.stepFlip = !this.stepFlip;
        this.burst((this.stepFlip ? 850 : 1000) * rr(0.9, 1.1), 0.7, 0.04, { vol: 0.07 * (o.power ?? 1) * Math.min(1.4, (o.speed || 4) / 5), pan, type: 'lowpass' });
        break;
      case 'skid': if (this.gate('skid' + (o.i ?? 0), 400)) this.burst(2600, 1.1, 0.12, { vol: 0.07 * (o.power ?? 1), pan, type: 'bandpass' }); break;
      case 'point': this.tone(660, 0.16, { type: 'triangle', vol: 0.25 }); this.tone(880, 0.26, { type: 'triangle', vol: 0.25, delay: 0.12 }); break;
      case 'lose': this.tone(330, 0.22, { type: 'triangle', vol: 0.18 }); this.tone(262, 0.3, { type: 'triangle', vol: 0.16, delay: 0.14 }); break;
      case 'fault': this.tone(190, 0.25, { type: 'sawtooth', vol: 0.18 }); break;
      case 'game': [523, 659, 784].forEach((f, i) => this.tone(f, 0.28, { type: 'triangle', vol: 0.22, delay: i * 0.1 })); break;
      case 'set': [523, 659, 784, 988].forEach((f, i) => this.tone(f, 0.3, { type: 'triangle', vol: 0.22, delay: i * 0.09 })); break;
      case 'matchpoint': this.tone(784, 0.12, { type: 'square', vol: 0.08 }); this.tone(988, 0.2, { type: 'square', vol: 0.08, delay: 0.14 }); break;
      case 'match': [523, 659, 784, 1047].forEach((f, i) => this.tone(f, 0.45, { type: 'triangle', vol: 0.25, delay: i * 0.16 })); break;
      case 'start': this.tone(440, 0.1, { type: 'triangle', vol: 0.2 }); this.tone(660, 0.18, { type: 'triangle', vol: 0.2, delay: 0.1 }); break;
      case 'ready': this.tone(600, 0.07, { type: 'triangle', vol: 0.15 }); this.tone(900, 0.1, { type: 'triangle', vol: 0.15, delay: 0.07 }); break;
      case 'progress': [392, 523, 659, 784, 1047].forEach((f, i) => this.tone(f, 0.3, { type: 'triangle', vol: 0.2, delay: i * 0.08 })); break;
      case 'ui': this.tone(520, 0.05, { vol: 0.15, type: 'triangle' }); break;
      default:
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ announcer (optional speech)

  announce(text) {
    if (!settings.announcer || !('speechSynthesis' in window) || !text) return;
    try {
      const u = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
      u.volume = Math.min(1, settings.voice * settings.master * 1.4);
      u.lang = 'en-GB'; u.rate = 1.05; u.pitch = 0.95;
      speechSynthesis.cancel();
      speechSynthesis.speak(u);
    } catch { /* speech not available */ }
  }
}

/** "Fifteen, love", "Deuce", "Advantage": the score as a referee would call it, server first. */
export function scoreCallout(score, serverTeam) {
  const word = { 0: 'love', 15: 'fifteen', 30: 'thirty', 40: 'forty', AD: 'advantage' };
  if (score.label === 'Deuce') return 'Deuce';
  const [a, b] = score.points;
  const first = serverTeam === 1 ? b : a, second = serverTeam === 1 ? a : b;
  if (score.tiebreak) return `${first}, ${second}`;
  if (first === 'AD' || second === 'AD') return `Advantage ${first === 'AD' ? 'server' : 'receiver'}`;
  if (first === second) return `${word[first]} all`;
  return `${word[first]}, ${word[second]}`;
}

// HTML for the career screens: hub, calendar, event bracket, training, development and result panels.
// Everything shown here is read from the save and the real tournament state: no placeholder numbers.
import {
  EVENTS, EVENT_BY_ID, ATTRS, ATTR_LABELS, ATTR_MAX, attrCost, calendar, rankOf, levelFor, DRILLS, OBJECTIVES,
  RESULT_LABELS, nextPlayerMatch, describeOpponents, standings, finishRewards,
} from '/shared/career2.js';
import { ROSTER_BY_ID } from '/shared/roster.js';
import { sceneFor } from '/shared/story.js';
import { BOT_LABELS } from '/shared/ai.js';
import { COURTS } from '/shared/looks.js';

const esc = (s) => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
const STYLE_NAMES = { net: 'Net player', retriever: 'Retriever', power: 'Power', allround: 'All-rounder', wild: 'Streaky attacker', counter: 'Counter-puncher' };
const stars = (n) => `<span class="stars">${'★'.repeat(n)}<i>${'★'.repeat(3 - n)}</i></span>`;
const nameOf = (id) => (id === 'you' ? 'You' : ROSTER_BY_ID[id]?.name || id);
const teamName = (ids) => ids.map(nameOf).join(' &amp; ');
const formatLabel = (e) => `${e.format === 'group' ? 'Group + final' : e.size + '-draw knockout'} · ${e.mode === 'doubles' ? 'doubles' : 'singles'}`;

const speaker = (who) => (who === 'narrator' ? 'Narrator' : ROSTER_BY_ID[who]?.name || who);
const sceneHtml = (s) => (s ? `<div class="scene"><b>${esc(speaker(s.who))}</b><p>${esc(s.text)}</p></div>` : '');

const TABS = [['hub', 'Hub'], ['calendar', 'Calendar'], ['training', 'Training'], ['development', 'Development']];

function header(save, tab) {
  return `<div class="row" style="flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px"><h2 style="margin:0">Career</h2><span class="grow"></span>
    <button class="btn small" data-act="equipment">Equipment</button><button class="btn small ghost" data-act="careerBack">Back</button></div>
    <div class="statrow">
      <div><span class="label">Season</span><b>${save.season}</b></div>
      <div><span class="label">World rank</span><b>#${rankOf(save.points)}</b></div>
      <div><span class="label">Ranking points</span><b>${save.points}</b></div>
      <div><span class="label">Level</span><b>${levelFor(save.xp)}</b></div>
      <div><span class="label">Coins</span><b>${save.coins}</b></div>
      <div><span class="label">Dev. points</span><b>${save.devPoints}</b></div>
    </div>
    <div class="seg" style="margin:10px 0">${TABS.map(([k, l]) => `<button data-act="careerTab" data-val="${k}" class="${tab === k ? 'on' : ''}">${l}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

const wrap = (inner) => `<div class="screen"><div class="panel career">${inner}</div></div>`;

function eventCard(c, save) {
  const e = c.event;
  const status = c.done ? `<span class="tag ready">${esc(RESULT_LABELS[c.done.result] || c.done.result)}</span>` : c.active ? '<span class="tag host">In progress</span>' : c.locked ? '<span class="tag off">Locked</span>' : '<span class="tag">Open</span>';
  const btn = c.locked ? `<button class="btn" disabled>${esc(c.locked)}</button>`
    : c.active ? `<button class="btn primary" data-act="eventEnter" data-event="${e.id}">Continue</button>`
      : `<button class="btn ${c.done ? '' : 'primary'}" data-act="eventEnter" data-event="${e.id}">${c.done ? 'Play again' : 'Enter'}</button>`;
  return `<div class="stage ${c.locked ? 'locked' : ''}">
    <div class="row"><span class="label">${esc(e.tier)}</span><span class="grow"></span>${status}</div>
    <h3>${esc(e.name)}</h3><p class="mute">${esc(e.blurb)}</p>
    <div class="row" style="flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px"><span class="tag">${esc(formatLabel(e))}</span><span class="tag">${esc(COURTS[e.court].name.split(' ').slice(0, 2).join(' '))}</span><span class="tag">${e.sets === 1 ? '1 set' : 'best of ' + e.sets} · ${e.gamesPerSet} games${e.golden ? ' · golden' : ''}</span></div>
    <p style="font-size:12px" class="mute">Up to +${e.points} ranking points · ${e.coins} coins</p>${btn}</div>`;
}

/** The upgrade list: used by the Career > Development tab and by the Upgrades button on the home screen. */
export function upgradesHtml(save) {
  return `<div class="upg"><p class="mute" style="margin-bottom:10px">You have <b style="color:var(--accent)">${save.devPoints}</b> upgrade points. Win matches against Club, Pro or Legend bots, finish career events, earn drill stars and complete objectives to get more. Upgrades work in every solo match (practice, 1v1, career, training) and never online, where everybody is equal.</p>
    ${ATTRS.map((a) => { const lv = save.attrs[a]; const cost = attrCost(lv); const can = lv < ATTR_MAX && save.devPoints >= cost; return `<div class="attr"><span class="attrname">${esc(ATTR_LABELS[a])}</span><span class="pips">${Array.from({ length: ATTR_MAX }, (_, i) => `<i class="${i < lv ? 'on' : ''}"></i>`).join('')}</span><button class="btn small" data-act="attrBuy" data-attr="${a}" ${can ? '' : 'disabled'}>${lv >= ATTR_MAX ? 'Max' : '+1 (' + cost + ')'}</button></div>`; }).join('')}</div>`;
}

export function hubHtml(save, tab) {
  const cal = calendar(save);
  let body = '';
  if (tab === 'hub') {
    const next = cal.find((c) => c.active) || cal.find((c) => !c.done && !c.locked);
    const nextCard = next
      ? `<div class="label">${next.active ? 'Event in progress' : 'Next event'}</div><div class="stagegrid">${eventCard(next, save)}</div>`
      : '<p class="mute">You have played every event on the calendar. Replay any of them from the Calendar tab to climb the ranking.</p>';
    const hist = save.history.length
      ? `<table class="table"><tr><th>Event</th><th>Round</th><th>Opponent</th><th>Score</th></tr>${save.history.slice(0, 6).map((h) => `<tr><td>${esc(EVENT_BY_ID[h.event]?.name || h.event)}</td><td>${esc(h.round)}</td><td>${esc(h.opp)}</td><td style="color:${h.won ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--bad)'}">${h.won ? 'W' : 'L'} ${esc(h.score)}</td></tr>`).join('')}</table>`
      : '<p class="mute">No matches yet. Enter the Club Open to begin.</p>';
    const obj = OBJECTIVES.map((o) => `<div class="obj ${save.objectives[o.id] ? 'done' : ''}"><span>${save.objectives[o.id] ? '✓' : '○'}</span><span class="grow">${esc(o.text)}</span><span class="mute">${esc(o.rewardText || [o.reward.coins && o.reward.coins + ' coins', o.reward.devPoints && o.reward.devPoints + ' dev. pts'].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</span></div>`).join('');
    body = `${nextCard}<div class="label" style="margin:14px 0 6px">Recent results</div>${hist}<div class="label" style="margin:14px 0 6px">Objectives</div>${obj}`;
  } else if (tab === 'calendar') {
    body = [1, 2, 3].map((s) => `<div class="label" style="margin:12px 0 8px">Season ${s}</div><div class="stagegrid">${cal.filter((c) => c.event.season === s).map((c) => eventCard(c, save)).join('')}</div>`).join('');
  } else if (tab === 'training') {
    body = `<p class="mute" style="margin-bottom:10px">Drills use the same ball physics and shot mechanics as a match. Earn stars to gain development points; only improvements pay.</p><div class="stagegrid">${DRILLS.map((d) => `<div class="stage"><h3>${esc(d.name)}</h3><p class="mute">${esc(d.blurb)}</p><div class="row">${stars(save.drills[d.id] || 0)}<span class="grow"></span><span class="mute" style="font-size:12px">${d.stars.join(' / ')} for 1-3★</span></div><button class="btn primary" data-act="drillStart" data-drill="${d.id}">Start drill</button></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
  } else if (tab === 'development') {
    body = upgradesHtml(save);
  }
  return wrap(header(save, tab) + body);
}

/** The draw / group table for the active tournament plus the next match. */
export function eventHtml(save) {
  const t = save.active;
  const ev = EVENT_BY_ID[t.eventId];
  const n = nextPlayerMatch(t);
  const teamLabel = (i) => `<span class="${i === 0 ? 'you' : ''}">${teamName(t.teams[i].ids)}</span>`;
  const matchBox = (m) => `<div class="bmatch">${[m.a, m.b].map((i) => `<div class="${m.winner === i ? 'win' : m.winner !== undefined ? 'lose' : ''}">${teamLabel(i)}</div>`).join('')}${m.score ? `<div class="mute" style="font-size:11px">${esc(m.score)}</div>` : ''}</div>`;
  let bracket;
  if (t.format === 'knockout') {
    const rounds = [...t.rounds];
    const names = t.koNames;
    bracket = `<div class="bracket">${names.map((nm, i) => `<div class="bround"><div class="label">${esc(nm)}</div>${rounds[i] ? rounds[i].matches.map(matchBox).join('') : '<div class="bmatch tbd">To be decided</div>'}</div>`).join('')}</div>`;
  } else {
    const order = standings(t);
    bracket = `<table class="table"><tr><th>#</th><th>Team</th><th>W</th><th>L</th><th>Games</th></tr>${order.map((i, k) => `<tr><td>${k + 1}</td><td>${teamLabel(i)}</td><td>${t.games[i].w}</td><td>${t.games[i].l}</td><td>${t.games[i].gf}-${t.games[i].ga}</td></tr>`).join('')}</table>
      <div class="label" style="margin:10px 0 6px">Matches</div><div class="bracket">${t.rounds.map((r) => `<div class="bround"><div class="label">${esc(r.name)}</div>${r.matches.map(matchBox).join('')}</div>`).join('')}</div>`;
  }
  const opps = n ? describeOpponents(n.opponents) : [];
  const scout = opps.map((o) => `<div class="scout"><b>${esc(o.name)}</b> <span class="tag">${esc(BOT_LABELS[o.level])}</span> <span class="tag">${esc(STYLE_NAMES[o.style])}</span><br><span class="mute">${esc(o.note)}</span><br><span class="mute" style="font-size:12px">World ranking points: ${o.points}</span></div>`).join('');
  const nextCard = n ? `<div class="panel" style="background:var(--panel-2);margin-top:14px"><div class="label">Your next match · ${esc(n.name)}</div><div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:700;margin:4px 0">${teamName(t.teams[0].ids)} vs ${teamName(n.opponents)}</div>${scout}
      <p class="mute" style="margin:8px 0">${ev.sets === 1 ? '1 set' : 'Best of ' + ev.sets} to ${ev.gamesPerSet} games${ev.golden ? ', golden point' : ''} · ${esc(COURTS[ev.court].name)}. Objective: win it and keep the errors down.</p>
      <button class="btn primary" data-act="eventPlay">Play match</button></div>` : '';
  return wrap(`<div class="row"><h2 style="margin:0">${esc(ev.name)}</h2><span class="tag">${esc(ev.tier)}</span><span class="grow"></span><button class="btn small ghost" data-act="eventAbandon">Withdraw</button><button class="btn small" data-act="careerTab" data-val="calendar">Calendar</button></div>
    <p class="mute" style="margin:6px 0 12px">${esc(ev.blurb)}</p>${t.round === 0 && t.rounds.length === 1 && !t.rounds[0].matches.some((m) => m.winner !== undefined) ? sceneHtml(sceneFor(ev.id, 'intro')) : ''}${bracket}${nextCard}`);
}

/** Panel shown on the results screen after an event match. */
export function eventResultHtml(info) {
  const lines = [];
  if (info.finished) {
    const r = info.finished;
    lines.push(`<div style="font-size:24px;font-weight:800;color:var(--accent)">${esc(RESULT_LABELS[r.result] || r.result)}</div>`);
    lines.push(`<p class="mute">+${r.points} ranking points · +${r.coins} coins · +${r.xp} XP${r.devPoints ? ' · +' + r.devPoints + ' development points' : ''}</p>`);
    if (info.summary) lines.push(`<p>${esc(info.summary)}</p>`);
    lines.push(sceneHtml(sceneFor(info.eventId, r.result === 'champion' ? 'champion' : 'other')));
  } else {
    lines.push(`<p class="mute">Next: ${esc(info.next || '')}</p>`);
  }
  for (const o of info.objectives || []) lines.push(`<div class="obj done"><span>✓</span><span class="grow">Objective complete: ${esc(o.text)}</span><span class="mute">${esc(o.rewardText || '')}</span></div>`);
  return `<div class="panel" style="margin-top:14px;background:var(--panel-2)"><div class="label">${esc(info.event)} · ${esc(info.round)}</div><p style="margin:6px 0">${esc(info.commentary)}</p>${lines.join('')}</div>`;
}

export function drillResultHtml(drill, score, attempts, r) {
  return `<h2>${esc(drill.name)}</h2><div style="font-size:34px;letter-spacing:.2em;color:var(--accent)">${'★'.repeat(r.stars)}<span class="mute">${'★'.repeat(3 - r.stars)}</span></div>
    <p>Score <b>${score}</b> from ${attempts} balls. Stars at ${drill.stars.join(' / ')}.</p>
    <p class="mute">${r.devPoints ? `+${r.devPoints} development points.` : r.stars === 0 ? 'Not enough for a star yet. Try again.' : 'No new points: you already have these stars or better.'}</p>
    <div class="row" style="margin-top:12px"><button class="btn primary" data-act="drillStart" data-drill="${drill.id}">Try again</button><button class="btn" data-act="careerTab" data-val="training">Back to training</button></div>`;
}
