/** Practice wins against stronger bots pay upgrade points (nothing against Rookies, so they cannot be farmed). */
function practiceReward(cfg, results) {
  if (results.winner !== 0) return null;
  const pts = { easy: 0, normal: 1, hard: 2, expert: 3 }[cfg.difficulty] || 0;
  if (!pts) return null;
  const save = loadCareer();
  save.devPoints += pts; save.xp += 10 * pts;
  saveCareer(save);
  setTimeout(() => toast(`+${pts} upgrade point${pts > 1 ? 's' : ''} (Upgrades on the home screen)`), 4600);
  return null;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- career v2

function showCareer(tab) {
  stopLocal();
  if (match) { match.stop(); match = null; }
  if (tab) careerTab = tab;
  hud = null;
  world.setZone(null);
  world.setSingles(false);
  screen = 'career';
  const save = loadCareer();
  ui.root.innerHTML = save.active && careerTab === 'event' ? eventHtml(save) : hubHtml(save, careerTab === 'event' ? 'hub' : careerTab);
}

function enterEvent(id) {
  const save = loadCareer();
  const ev = EVENT_BY_ID[id];
  if (!ev) return;
  if (save.active && save.active.eventId !== id) { toast('Finish or withdraw from your current event first', true); return; }
  if (!save.active) {
    const locked = (ev.requires?.points && save.points < ev.requires.points) || (ev.season > save.season);
    if (locked) { toast('That event is still locked', true); return; }
    save.active = createTournament(ev, save.seed++);
    saveCareer(save);
  }
  showCareer('event');
}

function playEventMatch() {
  const save = loadCareer();
  const t = save.active;
  if (!t) return showCareer('hub');
  const ev = EVENT_BY_ID[t.eventId];
  const n = nextPlayerMatch(t);
  if (!n) return showCareer('hub');
  const me = (net.name || 'You').slice(0, 16);
  const mates = ev.mode === 'doubles' ? [t.teams[0].ids[1]] : [];
  const roster = (id) => ROSTER_BY_ID[id];
  const opp = n.opponents.map(roster);
  const mate = mates.map(roster);
  const names = ev.mode === 'singles' ? [me, 'AI', opp[0].name, 'AI'] : [me, mate[0].name, opp[0].name, opp[1].name];
  const lv = (c) => ({ level: c.level, style: c.style });
  const diffs = ev.mode === 'singles' ? ['', 'easy', lv(opp[0]), 'easy'] : ['', lv(mate[0]), lv(opp[0]), lv(opp[1])];
  const looks = ev.mode === 'singles' ? [myLook, DEFAULT_LOOKS[1], opp[0].look, DEFAULT_LOOKS[3]] : [myLook, mate[0].look, opp[0].look, opp[1].look];
  const myPts = save.points;
  startLocalMatch({
    mode: ev.mode, difficulty: 'normal', sets: ev.sets, gamesPerSet: ev.gamesPerSet, golden: ev.golden, court: ev.court,
    attrMods: attrMods(save.attrs),
    custom: { names, diffs, looks },
    onFinish: (results) => finishEventMatch(ev, n, opp, myPts, results),
  });
}

function finishEventMatch(ev, n, opp, myPts, results) {
  const save = loadCareer();
  const t = save.active;
  if (!t) return null;
  let gf = 0, ga = 0;
  for (const [a, b] of results.score.sets) { gf += a; ga += b; }
  const won = results.winner === 0;
  const upset = won && opp.some((o) => o.points > myPts);
  const me = results.players[0] || {};
  const applied = applyMatch(save, ev, n.name, won, {
    gamesFor: gf, gamesAgainst: ga, opp: opp.map((o) => o.name).join(' & '), upset, smashes: me.smashes, aces: me.aces, at: Date.now(),
  });
  const seed = save.seed++;
  let k = 0;
  reportPlayerMatch(t, won, gf, ga, (a, b) => simulateMatch(a, b, ev, seed * 31 + k++));
  const info = { event: ev.name, eventId: ev.id, round: n.name, commentary: commentary(won, gf, ga, opp[0]?.note), objectives: [], next: '' };
  let finished = null;
  if (t.status === 'done') {
    finished = completeEvent(save, ev, t.result);
    info.finished = { ...finished, result: t.result };
    info.summary = t.result === 'champion' ? `${ev.name} is yours.` : `You finished as ${RESULT_LABELS[t.result].toLowerCase()}.`;
  } else {
    const nx = nextPlayerMatch(t);
    info.next = nx ? `${nx.name} against ${nx.opponents.map((id) => ROSTER_BY_ID[id].name).join(' & ')}` : '';
  }
  info.objectives = claimObjectives(save);
  saveCareer(save);
  return { kind: 'event', won, over: !!finished, html: eventResultHtml(info) };
}

function startDrill(id) {
  const save = loadCareer();
  const drill = DRILLS.find((d) => d.id === id);
  if (!drill) return;
  drillRun = { id };
  startLocalMatch({
    mode: 'singles', difficulty: 'easy', sets: 1, gamesPerSet: 6, court: court === 'outdoor' ? 'outdoor' : 'indoor',
    attrMods: attrMods(save.attrs), passive: true, controller: new DrillController(id),
  });
}

function onDrillResults(m) {
  const { id, score, attempts } = m.result;
  const drill = DRILLS.find((d) => d.id === id);
  const save = loadCareer();
  const r = applyDrill(save, drill, score);
  const gained = claimObjectives(save);
  saveCareer(save);
  stopLocal();
  if (match) { match.stop(); match = null; }
  hud = null;
  world.setZone(null);
  screen = 'results';
  ui.root.innerHTML = `<div class="screen"><div class="panel modal" style="width:min(520px,100%)">${drillResultHtml(drill, score, attempts, r)}${gained.map((o) => `<div class="obj done"><span>✓</span><span class="grow">Objective complete: ${o.text}</span></div>`).join('')}</div></div>`;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- controls screen

const ctrl = { device: 'keyboard', capture: null, raf: 0 };
function openControls() {
  const el = ui.controlsModal(input.settings, { device: ctrl.device, capture: ctrl.capture, padName: input.padName });
  el.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
    const k = e.target.dataset.ctrlRange;
    if (k) { input.settings[k] = +e.target.value; input.save(); }
  });
  // live test of every action while the screen is open
  cancelAnimationFrame(ctrl.raf);
  const tick = () => {
    const modal = document.getElementById('modal');
    if (!modal || !modal.querySelector('[data-test]')) return;
    modal.querySelectorAll('[data-test]').forEach((c) => c.classList.toggle('on', input.actionDown(c.dataset.test)));
    ctrl.raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  };
  ctrl.raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  ui.onModalClose = () => { input.cancelCapture(); ctrl.capture = null; cancelAnimationFrame(ctrl.raf); };
}
input.on('padchange', () => { if (document.querySelector('#modal [data-test]')) openControls(); });

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- actions

function act(name, d, el) {
  sound.unlock(); // a click is the user gesture that lets audio (and music) start
  if (!sound.musicKind && screen !== 'game') sound.startMusic('menu');
  switch (name) {
    case 'rename': net.send({ t: 'rename', name: val('nameInput') }); break;
    case 'create': net.send({ t: 'create', name: myName() }); break;
    case 'quick':
      queued = true;
      net.send({ t: 'quick', name: myName() });
      ui.quickModal({ waiting: 1, online: onlineN, botsIn: 10 });
      ui.onModalClose = () => { if (queued) { queued = false; net.send({ t: 'quickCancel' }); } };
      break;
    case 'level':
      level = d.val;
      try { localStorage.setItem('padel.level', level); } catch { /* optional */ }
      showHome();
      break;
    case 'court': court = d.val; try { localStorage.setItem('padel.court', court); } catch { /* optional */ } showHome(); break;
    case 'equipment': {
      const canvas = ui.equipmentModal(myLook, loadCareer().unlockedRackets);
      preview?.dispose();
      preview = new Preview(canvas);
      preview.setCharacter(myLook, previewTeam);
      ui.onModalClose = () => { preview?.dispose(); preview = null; };
      break;
    }
    case 'lookArch': case 'lookHair': case 'lookSkin': case 'lookRacket': {
      const key = { lookArch: 'arch', lookHair: 'hair', lookSkin: 'skin', lookRacket: 'racket' }[name];
      myLook = sanitizeLook({ ...myLook, [key]: +d.val }, 0);
      try { localStorage.setItem('padel.look', JSON.stringify(myLook)); } catch { /* optional */ }
      net.look = myLook;
      net.send({ t: 'look', look: myLook });
      ui.markEquipment(myLook);
      if (preview) { if (preview.mode === 'racket') preview.setRacket(myLook.racket); else preview.setCharacter(myLook, previewTeam); }
      break;
    }
    case 'previewMode': if (preview) { if (d.val === 'racket') preview.setRacket(myLook.racket); else preview.setCharacter(myLook, previewTeam); } break;
    case 'previewTeam': previewTeam ^= 1; if (preview && preview.mode === 'character') preview.setCharacter(myLook, previewTeam); break;
    case 'mouseAim': setMouseAim(d.val === '1', d.pause === '1'); break;
    case 'camera': setSetting('camera', d.val); ui.settingsModal(() => {}); break;
    case 'controlsSettings': openControls(); break;
    case 'scheme': applyScheme(input.settings, d.val); input.save(); openControls(); break;
    case 'ctrlDevice': ctrl.device = d.val; ctrl.capture = null; input.cancelCapture(); openControls(); break;
    case 'ctrlOpt': input.settings[d.key] = d.val === 'true'; if (d.key === 'mouseAim' && input.settings.scheme !== 'custom') input.settings.scheme = 'custom'; input.save(); openControls(); break;
    case 'ctrlReset': resetBindings(input.settings, ctrl.device); input.settings.scheme = 'custom'; input.save(); openControls(); toast('Defaults restored'); break;
    case 'rebind': {
      ctrl.capture = d.action;
      openControls();
      input.capture((device, code) => {
        const r = rebind(input.settings, device, d.action, code);
        ctrl.capture = null;
        input.save();
        openControls();
        toast(r.swappedWith ? `Swapped with "${r.swappedWith}"` : 'Binding saved');
      }, ctrl.device);
      break;
    }
    case 'career': showCareer(); break;
    case 'careerTab': showCareer(d.val); break;
    case 'eventEnter': enterEvent(d.event); break;
    case 'eventPlay': playEventMatch(); break;
    case 'eventContinue': case 'careerMenu': {
      const s = loadCareer();
      showCareer(s.active ? 'event' : 'hub');
      break;
    }
    case 'eventAbandon': {
      const s = loadCareer();
      s.active = null;
      saveCareer(s);
      toast('You withdrew from the event');
      showCareer('calendar');
      break;
    }
    case 'drillStart': startDrill(d.drill); break;
    case 'attrBuy': {
      const s = loadCareer();
      const r = buyAttr(s, d.attr);
      if (!r.ok) toast(r.reason, true); else { claimObjectives(s); saveCareer(s); }
      if (document.querySelector('#modal .upg')) showUpgrades(); else showCareer('development');
      break;
    }
    case 'careerBack': showHome(); break;
    case 'rooms': ui.roomsModal(null); net.send({ t: 'rooms' }); break;
    case 'joinRoom': ui.closeModal(); net.send({ t: 'join', code: d.code, name: myName() }); break;
    case 'upgrades': showUpgrades(); break;
    case 'practice': startLocalMatch({ mode: 'doubles', difficulty: level, reward: true }); break;
    case 'practice1': startLocalMatch({ mode: 'singles', difficulty: level, reward: true }); break;
    case 'join': {
      const code = val('joinCode').trim().toUpperCase();
      if (code.length !== 5) { toast('Enter the 5-character room code', true); break; }
      net.send({ t: 'join', code, name: myName() });
      break;
    }
    case 'copy':
      navigator.clipboard?.writeText(d.text).then(() => toast('Invite link copied'), () => toast(d.text));
      break;
    case 'leave':
      if (local) { stopLocal(); room = null; showHome(); } else net.send({ t: 'leave' });
      break;
    case 'sit': net.send({ t: 'sit', slot: +d.slot }); break;
    case 'select': selected = selected === d.id ? null : d.id; showLobby(); break;
    case 'place':
      if (selected === d.id) { selected = null; showLobby(); break; }
      if (selected) net.send({ t: 'assign', id: selected, slot: +d.slot });
      selected = null;
      break;
    case 'ready': net.send({ t: 'ready', ready: d.val === '1' }); break;
    case 'set': {
      const v = d.val === 'true' ? true : d.val === 'false' ? false : /^\d+$/.test(d.val) ? +d.val : d.val;
      net.send({ t: 'settings', settings: { [d.key]: v } });
      break;
    }
    case 'start': net.send({ t: 'start' }); break;
    case 'quick': net.send({ t: 'chat', text: d.text }); break;
    case 'chat': {
      const i = document.getElementById('chatInput');
      if (i.value.trim()) net.send({ t: 'chat', text: i.value });
      i.value = '';
      break;
    }
    case 'rematch': if (local) startLocalMatch(localCfg); else net.send({ t: 'rematch' }); break;
    case 'leaveLocal': stopLocal(); room = null; showHome(); break;
    case 'tolobby': net.send({ t: 'lobby' }); break;
    case 'profile': {
      const bonus = loadCareer().xp;
      const merged = profile ? { ...profile, xp: profile.xp + bonus, level: Math.floor(Math.sqrt((profile.xp + bonus) / 100)) + 1 } : null;
      ui.profileModal(merged, net.name);
      break;
    }
    case 'history': { const list = loadHistory(localStorage); ui.historyModal(list, summarize(list)); break; }
    case 'historyClear': { saveHistory(localStorage, []); ui.historyModal([], summarize([])); toast('History cleared'); break; }
    case 'controls': ui.controls(); break;
    case 'settings': ui.settingsModal(() => {}); break;
    case 'pauseRequest': net.send({ t: 'pauseReq' }); ui.closeModal(); match?.setPaused(false); toast('Pause requested: waiting for the other players'); break;
    case 'pauseAccept': net.send({ t: 'pauseAccept' }); break;
    case 'pauseDeny': net.send({ t: 'pauseDeny' }); ui.clearPauseOverlay(); break;
    case 'pauseResume': net.send({ t: 'pauseResume' }); break;
    case 'closeModal': closeModalOrBack(); break;
    case 'toggle':
      setSetting(d.key, d.val === '1');
      ui.settingsModal(() => {});
      break;
    case 'quality': setSetting('quality', d.val); world.setQuality(d.val); ui.settingsModal(() => {}); break;
    case 'fullscreen':
      if (document.fullscreenElement) document.exitFullscreen(); else document.documentElement.requestFullscreen?.();
      break;
    default:
  }
  void el;
}

document.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
  if (e.target.id === 'nameInput') net.send({ t: 'rename', name: e.target.value });
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- network events

net.on('status', () => {
  if (screen === 'home' && document.activeElement?.id !== 'nameInput') showHome();
});
net.on('disconnected', () => { if (!local) hud?.connection(false); });
net.on('welcome', (m) => {
  profile = m.profile;
  if (Number.isFinite(m.online)) onlineN = m.online;
  hud?.connection(true);
  if (screen === 'home' && document.activeElement?.id !== 'nameInput') showHome();
  if (autoJoin && !room) {
    net.send({ t: 'join', code: autoJoin, name: net.name });
    history.replaceState(null, '', location.pathname);
    autoJoin = null;
  }
});
net.on('profile', (m) => {
  profile = m.profile;
  if (ui.modal && document.querySelector('#modal h2')) ui.profileModal(profile, net.name);
});
net.on('error', (m) => {
  toast(m.message, true);
  practicePending = false;
  if (m.code === 'join-failed' && screen === 'home') showHome();
});
net.on('left', () => {
  room = null; practicePending = false; selected = null;
  showHome();
});
net.on('drillStatus', (m) => { hud?.setDrill(m.text); world.setZone(m.zone || null); });
net.on('drillResults', onDrillResults);
function setOnline(n) {
  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return;
  onlineN = n;
  const el = document.getElementById('onlineN');
  if (el) el.textContent = `${n} online`;
}
net.on('online', (m) => setOnline(m.n));
net.on('queue', (m) => {
  setOnline(m.online);
  if (m.cancelled || !queued) return;
  ui.quickModal(m);
});
// online pause: a request the others must accept, then a freeze of at most 30 s until everybody presses Resume
let pauseTimer = null;
function renderPause(m, offset) {
  const left = (t) => Math.max(0, Math.ceil((t - (Date.now() - offset)) / 1000));
  const mineRequest = m.byId === room?.you;
  if (m.phase === 'request') {
    ui.pauseOverlay(mineRequest
      ? `<h2>Pause requested</h2><p class="mute">Waiting for the other players to accept… (${left(m.expires)}s)</p>`
      : `<h2>${m.by} wants to pause</h2><p class="mute">Accept? It stops the match for 30 seconds at most. (${left(m.expires)}s)</p><div class="row" style="justify-content:center;margin-top:12px"><button class="btn primary" data-act="pauseAccept">Accept</button><button class="btn" data-act="pauseDeny">Decline</button></div>`);
  } else if (m.phase === 'active') {
    const me = m.resumedIds?.includes(room?.you);
    ui.pauseOverlay(`<h2>Paused</h2><p class="mute">The match resumes when everyone presses Resume, or in ${left(m.until)}s.</p><p class="mute" style="margin-top:6px">Resumed: ${m.resumed?.length || 0} / ${m.humans?.length || 0}</p><div class="row" style="justify-content:center;margin-top:12px"><button class="btn primary" data-act="pauseResume" ${me ? 'disabled' : ''}>${me ? 'Waiting for the others…' : 'Resume'}</button></div>`);
  }
}
net.on('pause', (m) => {
  clearInterval(pauseTimer);
  if (m.phase === 'request' || m.phase === 'active') {
    const offset = Date.now() - (m.now || Date.now());
    renderPause(m, offset);
    match?.setPaused(m.phase === 'active');
    pauseTimer = setInterval(() => renderPause(m, offset), 500);
  } else {
    ui.clearPauseOverlay();
    match?.setPaused(false);
    if (m.phase === 'denied') toast(m.expired ? 'The pause request expired' : `${m.by} declined the pause`, true);
  }
});
net.on('rooms', (m) => { if (document.getElementById('modal')) ui.roomsModal(m.rooms); });

net.on('room', (m) => {
  room = m;
  if (m.state === 'lobby') {
    if (practicePending && m.hostId === m.you) {
      net.send({ t: 'settings', settings: { difficulty: level, ...(practicePending === 'singles' ? { mode: 'singles' } : {}) } });
      practicePending = false;
      net.send({ t: 'start' });
    }
    if (screen !== 'lobby' || match) {
      if (match) { match.stop(); match = null; }
      ui.clear();
    }
    showLobby();
  } else if (m.state === 'results' && screen === 'results' && lastResults) {
    showResults(lastResults);
  }
});

net.on('start', (m) => {
  queued = false; inPause = false;
  clearInterval(pauseTimer); ui.clearPauseOverlay();
  if (match) match.stop();
  ui.clear();
  world.setCourt(m.settings.court || 'indoor');
  world.setLooks(m.looks);
  rematchVotes = [];
  hud = ui.showHud(m.names, m.settings);
  world.setSingles(m.settings.mode === 'singles');
  match = new MatchClient({
    net: m.local ? localProxy : net, world, hud, sound, start: m, input,
    onReplay: (on) => local?.setPaused(on),
    onPause: () => { if (ui.modal) closeModalOrBack(); else openPauseMenu(); },
  });
  screen = 'game';
  sound.unlock();
  sound.startMusic('match');
});

net.on('snap', (m) => match?.onSnap(m));
net.on('ev', (m) => match?.onEvents(m.ev));
net.on('chat', (m) => ui.addChat(m));
net.on('rematchVotes', (m) => {
  rematchVotes = m.ready;
  if (screen === 'results' && lastResults) showResults(lastResults);
});
function recordMatch(m) {
  try {
    const slot = local ? 0 : Math.max(0, room?.slots?.findIndex((s) => s && s.id === room.you) ?? 0);
    const entry = entryFrom(m.results, { slot, names: m.names, mode: room?.settings?.mode || 'doubles', court: room?.settings?.court || 'indoor', kind: m.career ? 'career' : local ? 'practice' : 'online' });
    saveHistory(localStorage, addEntry(loadHistory(localStorage), entry));
  } catch { /* history is a convenience: never break the results screen */ }
}

net.on('results', (m) => {
  lastResults = m;
  recordMatch(m);
  if (match) { match.stop(); }
  setTimeout(() => { if (screen !== 'lobby') showResults(m); }, 0);
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- main loop

const idleCam = { a: 0 };
function idle(dt) {
  idleCam.a += dt * 0.12;
  const a = idleCam.a;
  world.camera.position.set(Math.sin(a) * 9, 5.5 + Math.sin(a * 0.7) * 1.2, Math.cos(a) * 14);
  world.camera.lookAt(0, 1, 0);
  const spots = [[-2.2, -6.5], [2.4, -2.5], [2.2, 6.5], [-2.4, 2.5]];
  spots.forEach(([x, z], i) => world.updatePlayer(i, { x, z, vx: 0, vz: 0, swingT: -1, kindName: 'forehand', jumpT: 0 }, dt, false));
  world.setBall(0, 1.4 + Math.sin(a * 6) * 0.3, 0);
  world.reticle.position.y = -5; world.reticleDot.position.y = -5;
  world.reachRing.visible = false; world.meRing.position.y = -5;
}

// automatic graphics quality: if the game cannot hold ~40 fps, step the preset down
let last = performance.now();
let fpsT = 0, fpsN = 0, slowWindows = 0;
function loop(now) {
  const dt = Math.min(0.1, (now - last) / 1000);
  last = now;
  fpsT += dt; fpsN++;
  if (fpsT >= 2) {
    const fps = fpsN / fpsT;
    fpsT = 0; fpsN = 0;
    slowWindows = screen === 'game' && fps < 38 && !document.hidden ? slowWindows + 1 : 0;
    if (slowWindows >= 2 && settings.quality !== 'low') {
      const next = settings.quality === 'high' ? 'medium' : 'low';
      setSetting('quality', next);
      world.setQuality(next);
      toast(`Low frame rate: graphics set to ${next}`);
      slowWindows = 0;
    }
  }
  input.gameActive = screen === 'game';
  input.poll();
  if (match && screen === 'game') match.frame(dt, now);
  else if (screen !== 'results' && !window.__freeze) idle(dt);
  world.render(dt);
  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
}

net.connect();
showHome();
requestAnimationFrame(loop);

// handy for debugging / automated checks
window.__padel = { world, net, input, get local() { return local; }, get match() { return match; }, get room() { return room; } };
===== END FILE: client/js/main.js =====

===== FILE: client/js/net.js =====
// WebSocket client with automatic reconnect. Guest identity = a random token kept in localStorage;
// presenting it again after a drop returns the player to their seat.

const store = {
  get(k, d = null) { try { return localStorage.getItem(k) ?? d; } catch { return d; } },
  set(k, v) { try { localStorage.setItem(k, v); } catch { /* storage unavailable */ } },
};
export { store };

export class Net {
  constructor() {
    this.handlers = new Map();
    this.status = 'connecting';
    this.rtt = 0;
    this.attempt = 0;
    this.token = store.get('padel.token');
    this.name = store.get('padel.name', '');
    try { this.look = JSON.parse(store.get('padel.look', 'null')); } catch { this.look = null; }
    this.ws = null;
    this.closedByUs = false;
  }

  on(type, fn) {
    if (!this.handlers.has(type)) this.handlers.set(type, []);
    this.handlers.get(type).push(fn);
  }

  emit(type, data) { for (const fn of this.handlers.get(type) || []) fn(data); }

  setStatus(s) { if (this.status !== s) { this.status = s; this.emit('status', s); } }

  connect() {
    const proto = location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss' : 'ws';
    this.setStatus(this.attempt > 0 ? 'reconnecting' : 'connecting');
    const ws = new WebSocket(`${proto}://${location.host}/ws`);
    this.ws = ws;
    ws.onopen = () => {
      this.attempt = 0;
      this.send({ t: 'hello', token: this.token, name: this.name || undefined, look: this.look || undefined });
    };
    ws.onmessage = (e) => {
      let m;
      try { m = JSON.parse(e.data); } catch { return; }
      if (m.t === 'welcome') {
        this.token = m.token;
        this.name = m.name;
        store.set('padel.token', m.token);
        store.set('padel.name', m.name);
        this.setStatus('online');
        this.startPing();
      } else if (m.t === 'pong') {
        this.rtt = Math.round(performance.now() - m.ts);
      }
      this.emit(m.t, m);
    };
    ws.onclose = () => {
      this.stopPing();
      if (this.ws !== ws) return;
      this.setStatus('reconnecting');
      const delay = Math.min(5000, 400 * 2 ** this.attempt++);
      setTimeout(() => this.connect(), delay);
      this.emit('disconnected');
    };
    ws.onerror = () => {};
  }

  startPing() {
    this.stopPing();
    const ping = () => this.send({ t: 'ping', ts: performance.now(), rtt: this.rtt });
    ping();
    this.pingTimer = setInterval(ping, 2000);
  }

  stopPing() { clearInterval(this.pingTimer); }

  get online() { return this.ws && this.ws.readyState === 1; }

  send(o) { if (this.online) this.ws.send(JSON.stringify(o)); }
}
===== END FILE: client/js/net.js =====

===== FILE: client/js/preview.js =====
// A small stand-alone 3D viewer used by the equipment screen: rotates a character or a single racket.
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { buildCharacter, buildRacket, disposeCharacter } from './characters.js';
import { RACKETS } from '/shared/looks.js';

export class Preview {
  constructor(canvas) {
    this.canvas = canvas;
    this.renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas, antialias: true, alpha: true });
    this.renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2));
    this.renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
    this.renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
    this.scene = new THREE.Scene();
    this.scene.add(new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xe8f0ff, 0x30384a, 1.15));
    const key = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xfff3e0, 2.2); key.position.set(2, 4, 3); this.scene.add(key);
    const rim = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x8fb4ff, 1.2); rim.position.set(-3, 2, -3); this.scene.add(rim);
    const floor = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(0.9, 40), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x1b2433, roughness: 0.8 }));
    floor.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; this.scene.add(floor);
    this.camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(32, canvas.width / canvas.height, 0.1, 30);
    this.mode = 'character';
    this.item = null;
    this.t = 0;
    this.last = performance.now();
    this.stopped = false;
    const loop = (now) => {
      if (this.stopped) return;
      const dt = Math.min(0.05, (now - this.last) / 1000);
      this.last = now; this.t += dt;
      if (this.item) this.item.rotation.y += dt * 0.7;
      if (this.rig) this.breathe();
      this.renderer.render(this.scene, this.camera);
      this.raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    };
    this.raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  }

  clearItem() {
    if (this.item) { this.scene.remove(this.item); if (this.rig) disposeCharacter(this.rig); this.item = null; this.rig = null; }
  }

  /** Show a character in the ready stance. */
  setCharacter(look, team) {
    this.clearItem();
    const rig = buildCharacter(look, team);
    const a = 0.32;
    rig.hips.position.y = 0.88 * Math.cos(a) + 0.1;
    for (const L of [rig.legL, rig.legR]) { L.thigh.rotation.x = -a; L.knee.rotation.x = 2 * a; L.foot.rotation.x = -a * 0.9; }
    rig.spine.rotation.x = 0.12;
    rig.armR.sh.rotation.set(-0.7, -0.45, 0); rig.armR.elbow.rotation.set(-1.7, 0, 0);
    rig.armL.sh.rotation.set(-0.5, 0.35, 0.12); rig.armL.elbow.rotation.set(-0.9, 0, 0);
    rig.racket.rotation.set(0, 0, 0);
    this.rig = rig; this.item = rig.root; this.mode = 'character';
    this.scene.add(rig.root);
    this.camera.position.set(0, 1.15, 4.1); this.camera.lookAt(0, 0.95, 0);
  }

  /** Show a single racket, large, so its design can be inspected. */
  setRacket(index) {
    this.clearItem();
    const r = buildRacket(RACKETS[index]);
    r.scale.setScalar(3.2);
    r.rotation.z = Math.PI; // head up
    const holder = new THREE.Group(); holder.add(r); holder.position.y = 0.95;
    this.item = holder; this.mode = 'racket';
    this.scene.add(holder);
    this.camera.position.set(0, 1.0, 2.9); this.camera.lookAt(0, 0.95, 0);
  }

  breathe() {
    const t = this.t;
    this.rig.hips.position.y += (Math.sin(t * 2.4) * 0.004);
    this.rig.armR.elbow.rotation.x = -1.7 + Math.sin(t * 2.1) * 0.04;
  }

  dispose() {
    this.stopped = true;
    cancelAnimationFrame(this.raf);
    this.clearItem();
    this.renderer.dispose();
  }
}
===== END FILE: client/js/preview.js =====

// Rendering only: court, players, ball. No game rules live here.
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { RoomEnvironment } from '/vendor/three/examples/jsm/environments/RoomEnvironment.js';
import { EffectComposer } from '/vendor/three/examples/jsm/postprocessing/EffectComposer.js';
import { RenderPass } from '/vendor/three/examples/jsm/postprocessing/RenderPass.js';
import { UnrealBloomPass } from '/vendor/three/examples/jsm/postprocessing/UnrealBloomPass.js';
import { OutputPass } from '/vendor/three/examples/jsm/postprocessing/OutputPass.js';
import { COURT } from '/shared/constants.js';
import { DEFAULT_LOOKS } from '/shared/looks.js';
import { buildCharacter, disposeCharacter } from './characters.js';
import { buildEnvironment } from './envs.js';
import { netHeight } from '/shared/physics.js';

const TEAM_COLORS = [0xf2f5f8, 0x3d78c4]; // team A white, team B blue
const ACCENT = 0xd4f23c;
const clampSpin = (w) => Math.max(-70, Math.min(70, w)); // rad/s: faster than this just aliases on screen

function canvasTex(w, h, draw, { repeat, aniso = 4 } = {}) {
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = w; c.height = h;
  draw(c.getContext('2d'), w, h);
  const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
  t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  t.anisotropy = aniso;
  if (repeat) { t.wrapS = t.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping; t.repeat.set(...repeat); }
  return t;
}

function turfTexture(base = '#2b5f94') {
  const W = 1024, H = 2048; // 102.4 px per metre
  return canvasTex(W, H, (g) => {
    g.fillStyle = base; g.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    for (let i = 0; i < 60000; i++) { // fibre speckle
      const x = Math.random() * W, y = Math.random() * H;
      g.fillStyle = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'rgba(255,255,255,.035)' : 'rgba(0,0,0,.06)';
      g.fillRect(x, y, 2, 2);
    }
    for (let y = 0; y < H; y += 128) { // mowing stripes
      g.fillStyle = (y / 128) % 2 ? 'rgba(255,255,255,.018)' : 'rgba(0,0,0,.02)';
      g.fillRect(0, y, W, 128);
    }
    g.strokeStyle = '#f4f6f8'; g.lineWidth = 6; g.lineCap = 'square';
    const px = (x) => (x + 5) * 102.4;
    const pz = (z) => (z + 10) * 102.4;
    g.strokeRect(3, 3, W - 6, H - 6);
    for (const z of [-COURT.SERVICE_LINE, COURT.SERVICE_LINE]) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, pz(z)); g.lineTo(W, pz(z)); g.stroke(); }
    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(px(0), pz(-COURT.SERVICE_LINE)); g.lineTo(px(0), pz(COURT.SERVICE_LINE)); g.stroke();
  }, { aniso: 8 });
}

function meshTexture() {
  return canvasTex(128, 128, (g, w, h) => {
    g.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
    g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(200,210,225,.9)'; g.lineWidth = 6;
    g.strokeRect(0, 0, w, h);
  }, { repeat: [1, 1] });
}

/** A horizontal advertising board / sign texture (1024x128). */
function bannerTexture(text = 'PADEL CLUB', bg = '#0d141d', fg = '#eef3f8', sub = 'INDOOR · DOUBLES') {
  return canvasTex(1024, 128, (g, w, h) => {
    g.fillStyle = bg; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
    g.fillStyle = '#d4f23c'; g.beginPath(); g.arc(70, 64, 26, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill();
    g.fillStyle = fg; g.font = '800 62px Segoe UI, Arial'; g.textBaseline = 'middle';
    g.fillText(text, 120, 68);
    const x = 120 + g.measureText(text).width + 40;
    g.fillStyle = '#3d6492'; g.fillRect(x, 40, 4, 48);
    g.fillStyle = fg; g.globalAlpha = 0.6; g.font = '600 34px Segoe UI, Arial'; g.fillText(sub, x + 30, 66); g.globalAlpha = 1;
  });
}

// Pose vector: [pelvisYaw, spineYaw, lean, kneeBend, rShYaw, rShPitch, rElbow, lShYaw, lShPitch, lElbow, step]

export class World {
  constructor(canvas) {
    this.canvas = canvas;
    this.renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas, antialias: true, powerPreference: 'high-performance' });
    this.renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
    this.renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
    this.renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.05;
    this.scene = new THREE.Scene();
    this.scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x0b1119);
    this.scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(0x0b1119, 38, 90);
    this.camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(52, 1, 0.1, 200);
    this.players = [];
    this.buildLights();
    this.buildCourt();
    // the back wall nearest the camera is hidden (like TV coverage) so it never blocks the view
    this.backWall = this.courtGroup.children.filter((c) => c.geometry && Math.abs(Math.abs(c.position.z) - 10) < 0.02 && Math.abs(c.rotation.y) < 0.01 && c.position.y < 5);
    this.buildBall();
    this.buildMarkers();
    this.crowds = [];
    this.buildFx();
    this.buildEnv();
    this.setLooks(DEFAULT_LOOKS);
    this.setCourt('indoor');
    this.setQuality('medium');
    this.resize();
    this.clock = 0;
    this.fovKick = 0;
    this.baseFov = 52;
    window.addEventListener('resize', () => this.resize());
  }

  setQuality(q) {
    const pr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, q === 'high' ? 2 : q === 'medium' ? 1.5 : 1);
    this.renderer.setPixelRatio(pr);
    this.renderer.shadowMap.enabled = q !== 'low';
    this.renderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;
    this.sun.shadow.mapSize.set(q === 'high' ? 2048 : 1024, q === 'high' ? 2048 : 1024);
    if (this.sun.shadow.map) { this.sun.shadow.map.dispose(); this.sun.shadow.map = null; }
    this.sun.castShadow = q !== 'low';
    this.bloom = q === 'high';
    if (this.bloom && !this.composer) {
      const rt = new THREE.WebGLRenderTarget(4, 4, { type: THREE.HalfFloatType, samples: 4 });
      this.composer = new EffectComposer(this.renderer, rt);
      this.composer.addPass(new RenderPass(this.scene, this.camera));
      this.composer.addPass(new UnrealBloomPass(new THREE.Vector2(256, 256), 0.32, 0.55, 0.92));
      this.composer.addPass(new OutputPass());
    }
    this.resize();
  }

  resize() {
    const w = window.innerWidth, h = window.innerHeight;
    this.renderer.setSize(w, h, false);
    this.camera.aspect = w / h;
    this.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    this.composer?.setSize(w, h);
    this.composer?.setPixelRatio(this.renderer.getPixelRatio());
  }

  buildLights() {
    this.hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xcfe0ff, 0x1a2230, 0.95);
    this.scene.add(this.hemi);
    const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xfff4e0, 1.7);
    sun.position.set(7, 20, 5);
    sun.target.position.set(0, 0, 0);
    const c = sun.shadow.camera;
    c.left = -13; c.right = 13; c.top = 13; c.bottom = -13; c.near = 5; c.far = 45;
    sun.shadow.bias = -0.0004;
    sun.shadow.normalBias = 0.02;
    this.sun = sun;
    this.scene.add(sun, sun.target);
  }

  buildCourt() {
    // everything that makes up the court lives in one group so the 1v1 court can be narrowed as a whole
    const s = new THREE.Group();
    this.scene.add(s);
    this.courtGroup = s;
    // surround floor
    const out = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(90, 90), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x151d29, roughness: 0.95 }));
    out.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; out.position.y = -0.01; out.receiveShadow = true;
    s.add(out);
    this.outFloor = out;
    // playing surface
    const turf = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(10, 20), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: turfTexture(), roughness: 0.92 }));
turf.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; turf.receiveShadow = true;
    const bump = canvasTex(128, 128, (g, w, h) => { for (let i = 0; i < 2600; i++) { g.fillStyle = `rgba(${Math.random() * 255 | 0},${Math.random() * 255 | 0},${Math.random() * 255 | 0},.5)`; g.fillRect(Math.random() * w, Math.random() * h, 2, 2); } }, { repeat: [10, 20] });
    turf.material.bumpMap = bump; turf.material.bumpScale = 0.6;
    s.add(turf);
    this.turfMesh = turf;
    const glassMat = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({ color: 0xaecde6, transparent: true, opacity: 0.13, roughness: 0.05, metalness: 0, side: THREE.DoubleSide, depthWrite: false });
    const steel = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x9aa7b8, metalness: 0.7, roughness: 0.4 });
    const mt = meshTexture();
    const meshMat = (w, h) => {
      const t = mt.clone(); t.needsUpdate = true; t.repeat.set(w / 0.12, h / 0.12); t.wrapS = t.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
      return new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: t, transparent: true, alphaTest: 0.2, side: THREE.DoubleSide, opacity: 0.55 });
    };
    const glass = (w, h, x, y, z, ry) => {
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w, h), glassMat);
      m.position.set(x, y, z); m.rotation.y = ry; s.add(m);
    };
    const fence = (w, h, x, y, z, ry) => {
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w, h), meshMat(w, h));
      m.position.set(x, y, z); m.rotation.y = ry; s.add(m);
    };
    const post = (x, z, h) => {
      const p = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.08, h, 0.08), steel);
      p.position.set(x, h / 2, z); p.castShadow = true; s.add(p);
    };
    const rail = (w, x, y, z, ry) => {
      const r = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, 0.06, 0.06), steel);
      r.position.set(x, y, z); r.rotation.y = ry; s.add(r);
    };
    // back walls: 3 m glass + 1 m mesh; side walls: glass in the back 4 m, mesh through the middle
    for (const sz of [-1, 1]) {
      const z = sz * 10;
      glass(10, 3, 0, 1.5, z, 0);
      fence(10, 1, 0, 3.5, z, 0);
      rail(10, 0, 4, z, 0); rail(10, 0, 3, z, 0);
      post(sz * 5, z, 4); post(-sz * 5, z, 4);
      for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
        glass(4, 3, sx * 5, 1.5, sz * 8, Math.PI / 2);
        fence(4, 1, sx * 5, 3.5, sz * 8, Math.PI / 2);
        post(sx * 5, sz * 6, 4);
      }
    }
    // side walls through the middle: mesh with an access door on each half (opening 1.4 .. 3.0 m from the net, 2 m high)
    const { z0: dz0, z1: dz1, h: dh } = COURT.DOOR;
    this.pitGroup = new THREE.Group();
    s.add(this.pitGroup);
    for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
      const x = sx * 5;
      const segs = [[-6, -dz1], [-dz0, dz0], [dz1, 6]];
      for (const [a, b] of segs) fence(b - a, 3, x, 1.5, (a + b) / 2, Math.PI / 2);
      for (const sz of [-1, 1]) { // lintel above each door and a steel frame
        fence(dz1 - dz0, 3 - dh, x, dh + (3 - dh) / 2, sz * (dz0 + dz1) / 2, Math.PI / 2);
        rail(dz1 - dz0, x, dh, sz * (dz0 + dz1) / 2, Math.PI / 2);
        post(x, sz * dz0, 4); post(x, sz * dz1, 4);
        // make the door impossible to miss: glowing frame, a lit lintel bar and a floor mat inside the court
        const glow = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: ACCENT });
        for (const zz of [dz0, dz1]) { const f = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.1, dh, 0.1), glow); f.position.set(x, dh / 2, sz * zz); this.pitGroup.add(f); }
        const lin = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.1, 0.1, dz1 - dz0 + 0.1), glow); lin.position.set(x, dh, sz * (dz0 + dz1) / 2); this.pitGroup.add(lin);
        const mat = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.1, dz1 - dz0), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: ACCENT, transparent: true, opacity: 0.22, depthWrite: false }));
        mat.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; mat.position.set(sx * (COURT.HALF_W - 0.55), 0.03, sz * (dz0 + dz1) / 2); this.pitGroup.add(mat);
      }
      fence(12, 1, x, 3.5, 0, Math.PI / 2);
      rail(20, x, 4, 0, Math.PI / 2); rail(20, x, 3, 0, Math.PI / 2);
      post(x, 0, 4);
      // the strip outside the wall: concrete floor, outer fence, end fences
      const xo = sx * (COURT.HALF_W + COURT.PIT.w);
      const L = COURT.PIT.z1 * 2;
      const slab = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(COURT.PIT.w, L), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x2b3340, roughness: 0.9 }));
      slab.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; slab.position.set(sx * (COURT.HALF_W + COURT.PIT.w / 2), 0.004, 0); slab.receiveShadow = true;
      this.pitGroup.add(slab);
      const pf = (w, h, px, py, pz, ry) => { const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w, h), meshMat(w, h)); m.position.set(px, py, pz); m.rotation.y = ry; this.pitGroup.add(m); };
      pf(L, 3, xo, 1.5, 0, Math.PI / 2);
      for (const sz of [-1, 1]) pf(COURT.PIT.w, 3, sx * (COURT.HALF_W + COURT.PIT.w / 2), 1.5, sz * COURT.PIT.z1, 0);
      for (let zz = -COURT.PIT.z1; zz <= COURT.PIT.z1 + 0.01; zz += 3.07) {
        const pp = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.07, 3, 0.07), steel); pp.position.set(xo, 1.5, zz); this.pitGroup.add(pp);
      }
      const topRail = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.06, 0.06, L), steel); topRail.position.set(xo, 3, 0); this.pitGroup.add(topRail);
    }
    // glass panel seams and base rails
    const seamMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x20293a, roughness: 0.5, metalness: 0.5 });
    const seam = (x, z, h = 3) => { const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.04, h, 0.04), seamMat); m.position.set(x, h / 2, z); s.add(m); };
    for (const sz of [-1, 1]) {
      for (const x of [-4, -2, 0, 2, 4]) seam(x, sz * 9.99);
      for (const sx of [-1, 1]) for (const zz of [6, 8, 10]) seam(sx * 4.99, sz * zz);
      rail(10, 0, 0.04, sz * 10, 0);
    }

    // 1v1: the side walls become solid glass all along
    this.singlesGlass = new THREE.Group();
    for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
      const g = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(12, 3), glassMat); g.position.set(sx * 4.98, 1.5, 0); g.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2; this.singlesGlass.add(g);
      for (const zz of [-4, -2, 0, 2, 4]) { const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.04, 3, 0.04), seamMat); m.position.set(sx * 4.97, 1.5, zz); this.singlesGlass.add(m); }
    }
    this.singlesGlass.visible = false; s.add(this.singlesGlass);

    // net: sagging mesh, white top band, centre strap, side bands, posts with caps
    const cell = canvasTex(32, 32, (g, w, h) => {
      g.clearRect(0, 0, w, h); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(30,36,48,.95)'; g.lineWidth = 3;
      g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, 0); g.lineTo(w, h); g.moveTo(w, 0); g.lineTo(0, h); g.stroke();
    }, { repeat: [10 / 0.07, 1 / 0.07] });
    const netGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(10, 1, 60, 8);
    const np = netGeo.attributes.position;
    for (let i = 0; i < np.count; i++) np.setY(i, (np.getY(i) + 0.5) * netHeight(np.getX(i)));
    const net = new THREE.Mesh(netGeo, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: cell, transparent: true, alphaTest: 0.1, side: THREE.DoubleSide }));
    s.add(net);
    const strip = (w, h, segs, yFn, mat, castS = false) => {
      const g = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w, h, segs, 1);
      const p = g.attributes.position;
      for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i++) p.setY(i, yFn(p.getX(i)) + p.getY(i));
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(g, mat); m.castShadow = castS; return m;
    };
    const white = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xffffff, roughness: 0.6, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
    s.add(strip(10, 0.075, 60, (x) => netHeight(x) - 0.037, white, true));
    s.add(strip(10, 0.03, 60, () => 0.02, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x20242c, side: THREE.DoubleSide })));
    const strap = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.06, COURT.NET_H), white);
    strap.position.set(0, COURT.NET_H / 2, 0.003); s.add(strap);
    const strap2 = strap.clone(); strap2.position.z = -0.003; s.add(strap2);
    const anchor = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.06, 0.06, 0.012, 14), steel); anchor.position.set(0, 0.006, 0); s.add(anchor);
    for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
      const band = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.05, netHeight(5)), white); band.position.set(sx * 4.975, netHeight(5) / 2, 0); band.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2; s.add(band);
      const post = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.042, 0.05, 1.1, 14), steel);
      post.position.set(sx * 5.06, 0.55, 0); post.castShadow = true; s.add(post);
      const capM = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.05, 12, 8), steel); capM.position.set(sx * 5.06, 1.1, 0); s.add(capM);
      const knob = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.022, 0.022, 0.07, 10), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: ACCENT, roughness: 0.5 }));
      knob.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2; knob.position.set(sx * 5.1, 0.55, 0); s.add(knob);
    }
  }

  buildBall() {
    // Felt-covered padel ball: fine fuzz (colour + bump) and the curved seam of a real ball
    const W = 512, H = 256;
    const tex = canvasTex(W, H, (g, w, h) => {
      g.fillStyle = '#d6ee38'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
      for (let i = 0; i < 9000; i++) { // felt fibres
        g.fillStyle = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'rgba(255,255,170,.10)' : 'rgba(70,90,0,.10)';
        g.fillRect(Math.random() * w, Math.random() * h, 1 + Math.random() * 1.5, 1 + Math.random() * 1.5);
      }
      g.strokeStyle = '#f8ffe0'; g.lineWidth = 7; g.lineCap = 'round';
      for (const ox of [0, w / 2]) { // two interlocking curves make the continuous seam
        g.beginPath();
        g.moveTo(ox + w * 0.02, h * 0.18);
        g.bezierCurveTo(ox + w * 0.18, h * 0.02, ox + w * 0.32, h * 0.45, ox + w * 0.25, h * 0.5);
        g.bezierCurveTo(ox + w * 0.18, h * 0.55, ox + w * 0.32, h * 0.98, ox + w * 0.48, h * 0.82);
        g.stroke();
      }
      g.fillStyle = 'rgba(40,60,0,.55)'; g.font = '700 30px Arial'; g.textAlign = 'center'; // a small printed mark
      g.fillText('PADEL', w * 0.75, h * 0.52);
    });
    const bump = canvasTex(256, 128, (g, w, h) => {
      g.fillStyle = '#808080'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
      for (let i = 0; i < 6000; i++) { const v = (Math.random() * 255) | 0; g.fillStyle = 'rgb(' + v + ',' + v + ',' + v + ')'; g.fillRect(Math.random() * w, Math.random() * h, 1, 1); }
    });
    this.ballMesh = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.0335, 32, 24),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: tex, bumpMap: bump, bumpScale: 1.4, emissive: 0x3a4a00, emissiveIntensity: 0.4, roughness: 0.92 }),
    );
    this.ballMesh.castShadow = true;
    this.ball = new THREE.Group(); // the group carries position and scale (and the squash on impact); the mesh inside spins
    this.ball.add(this.ballMesh);
    this.scene.add(this.ball);
    this.ballBase = 1;
    this.squash = null;
    this.ballShadow = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.CircleGeometry(0.12, 24),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x000000, transparent: true, opacity: 0.4, depthWrite: false }),
    );
    this.ballShadow.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
    this.scene.add(this.ballShadow);
    // a faint plumb line from the ball down to its shadow when it is high: it makes the height readable from any camera
    const lg = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints([new THREE.Vector3(), new THREE.Vector3(0, -1, 0)]);
    this.ballDrop = new THREE.Line(lg, new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0xf3ff7a, transparent: true, opacity: 0.0, depthWrite: false }));
    this.ballDrop.frustumCulled = false;
    this.scene.add(this.ballDrop);
    this.ballHalo = new THREE.Mesh(
      new THREE.RingGeometry(0.1, 0.12, 24),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: ACCENT, transparent: true, opacity: 0.0, depthWrite: false, depthTest: false }),
    );
    this.scene.add(this.ballHalo);
    // speed streak: a tapered tail along the flight path (stronger the faster the ball)
    const tail = new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.03, 1, 10, 1, true);
    tail.translate(0, 0.5, 0); // base at the ball, point trailing away along +y (rotated onto the reverse of the velocity)
    this.streak = new THREE.Mesh(tail, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xeaff6a, transparent: true, opacity: 0, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide }));
    this.streak.visible = false;
    this.scene.add(this.streak);
    // motion trail: fading ghosts of earlier positions
    this.trailPos = [];
    this.trail = [];
    const gm = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.0335, 8, 6);
    for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(gm, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xe6ff5a, transparent: true, opacity: 0, depthWrite: false }));
      m.visible = false; this.scene.add(m); this.trail.push(m);
    }
  }

  buildMarkers() {
    const mk = (r0, r1, color, op) => {
      const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.RingGeometry(r0, r1, 40), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color, transparent: true, opacity: op, depthWrite: false }));
      m.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; m.position.y = 0.03; this.scene.add(m); return m;
    };
    this.reticle = mk(0.22, 0.3, ACCENT, 0.95);
    this.reticleDot = mk(0, 0.06, ACCENT, 0.95);
    this.reachRing = mk(1.53, 1.6, 0xffffff, 0.22);
    this.meRing = mk(0.5, 0.58, ACCENT, 0.9);
  }

  /** 1v1: the whole court (walls, net, turf) narrows to 7 m and the sides become solid glass. */
  setSingles(on) {
    this.courtGroup.scale.x = on ? 0.7 : 1;
    this.singlesGlass.visible = on;
    if (this.pitGroup) this.pitGroup.visible = !on;
  }

  /** Draw (or clear) a translucent target rectangle on the opponents' half, for training drills. */
  setZone(z) {
    if (!this.zoneMesh) {
      this.zoneMesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1, 1), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xd4f23c, transparent: true, opacity: 0.28, depthWrite: false }));
      this.zoneMesh.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; this.zoneMesh.position.y = 0.025; this.scene.add(this.zoneMesh);
    }
    this.zoneMesh.visible = !!z;
    if (z) { this.zoneMesh.scale.set(z.x1 - z.x0, z.z1 - z.z0, 1); this.zoneMesh.position.set((z.x0 + z.x1) / 2, 0.025, (z.z0 + z.z1) / 2); }
  }

  setBallScale(k) { this.ballBase = k; this.ball.scale.setScalar(k); this.ballShadow.scale.setScalar(k * 0.7); this.ballHalo.scale.setScalar(k * 0.7); }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ players

  /**
   * Jointed athlete: pelvis → spine (twist/lean) → shoulders → elbows, and hip → knee → foot per leg.
   * Poses are driven by a small parameter vector (see READY) so every shot is a set of keyframes.
   */
  makePlayer(team, look) {
    const rig = buildCharacter(look, team);
    rig.cur = READY.slice();
    return rig;
  }

  /** (Re)build the four athletes from their looks. Unchanged looks keep their rig. */
  setLooks(looks) {
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      const look = looks?.[i] || DEFAULT_LOOKS[i];
      const key = JSON.stringify(look);
      if (this.players[i] && this.players[i].lookKey === key) continue;
      const old = this.players[i];
      if (old) { this.scene.remove(old.root); disposeCharacter(old); }
      const rig = this.makePlayer(i >> 1, look);
      rig.lookKey = key;
      this.players[i] = rig;
      this.scene.add(rig.root);
    }
  }

  /** Interpolate the keyframes of a swing at time t (seconds since the swing began). */
  swingParams(kind, t, from = READY) {
    const K = SWING[kind] || SWING.forehand;
    const keys = [from, K[0], K[1], K[2]];
    const times = [0, 0.075, 0.17, 0.42];
    let i = 0;
    while (i < 2 && t > times[i + 1]) i++; // the last segment (contact → follow-through) also covers t beyond its end
    const f = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (t - times[i]) / (times[i + 1] - times[i])));
    const e = f * f * (3 - 2 * f);
    return keys[i].map((v, k) => v + (keys[i + 1][k] - v) * e);
  }

  /**
   * Keep the racket face square to the net whatever the arm does: the head continues the forearm, and the
   * face normal is the body's forward direction projected perpendicular to that axis.
   */
  orientRacket(pl) {
    const T = this._rt || (this._rt = { q: new THREE.Quaternion(), qw: new THREE.Quaternion(), a: new THREE.Vector3(), n: new THREE.Vector3(), x: new THREE.Vector3(), y: new THREE.Vector3(), f: new THREE.Vector3(), m: new THREE.Matrix4() });
    pl.root.updateMatrixWorld(true);
    pl.armR.elbow.getWorldQuaternion(T.q);
    T.a.set(0, -1, 0).applyQuaternion(T.q); // direction the racket head points
    const yaw = pl.yaw + pl.hips.rotation.y + pl.spine.rotation.y * 0.6;
    T.f.set(Math.sin(yaw), 0, Math.cos(yaw));
    T.n.copy(T.f).addScaledVector(T.a, -T.f.dot(T.a));
    if (T.n.lengthSq() < 0.05) { T.n.set(0, 1, 0).addScaledVector(T.a, -T.a.y); }
    T.n.normalize();
    T.y.copy(T.a).negate();
    T.x.crossVectors(T.y, T.n);
    T.m.makeBasis(T.x, T.y, T.n);
    T.qw.setFromRotationMatrix(T.m);
    pl.racket.quaternion.copy(T.q.invert()).multiply(T.qw);
  }

  /** type: 'win' | 'lose' */
  emote(slot, type) { this.players[slot].emote = { type, t: 0 }; }

  splitStep(slot) { this.players[slot].split = 0.3; }

  /**
   * @param v {x, z, vx, vz, swingT, kindName, jumpT}
   */
  updatePlayer(i, v, dt, isMe) {
    const pl = this.players[i];
    pl.root.visible = Math.abs(v.x) < 20; // parked (inactive) players are hidden
    const speed = Math.hypot(v.vx, v.vz);
    const base = pl.team === 0 ? 0 : Math.PI;
    const swinging = v.swingT >= 0;
    if (swinging && !pl.wasSwinging) pl.startPose = pl.cur.slice();
    pl.wasSwinging = swinging;
    const wrap = (a) => Math.atan2(Math.sin(a), Math.cos(a));

    // Body yaw: square to the net, turning toward the ball when it is on our side. Frozen while swinging.
    const bp = this.ball.position;
    const ballAng = wrap(Math.atan2(bp.x - v.x, bp.z - v.z) - base);
    const ownSide = pl.team === 0 ? bp.z < 0.5 : bp.z > -0.5;
    if (!swinging) {
      const want = base + THREE.MathUtils.clamp(ballAng, -1.05, 1.05) * (ownSide ? 0.6 : 0.2);
      pl.yaw += wrap(want - pl.yaw) * Math.min(1, dt * 8);
    }
    pl.root.rotation.y = pl.yaw;

    // gait in the body's own frame: forward/back running and side shuffles use different leg motion
    const sy = Math.sin(pl.yaw), cy = Math.cos(pl.yaw);
    const vf = v.vx * sy + v.vz * cy;
    const vl = v.vx * cy - v.vz * sy;
    // smoothed speed (so bot jitter does not flap the legs); cadence follows ground speed so feet do not slide
    pl.speedS = (pl.speedS ?? speed) + (speed - (pl.speedS ?? speed)) * Math.min(1, dt * 9);
    const sp = pl.speedS;
    pl.phase += dt * sp * 2.2;
    const ph = pl.phase;
    { // a foot lands every half stride; a hard stop skids
      const sg = Math.sign(Math.sin(ph));
      if (pl.lastSg !== undefined && sg !== pl.lastSg && sp > 1.2 && !swinging && v.jumpT <= 0) this.onStep?.(i, sp, v.x);
      pl.lastSg = sg;
    }
    const run = Math.min(1, sp / 6);
    const moving = sp > 0.35 ? 1 : 0;
    const pitchAmp = Math.min(1.2, Math.abs(vf) / 6) * (0.35 + 0.5 * run) * moving;
    const rollAmp = Math.min(1, Math.abs(vl) / 5.4) * 0.5 * moving;
    const dirF = Math.sign(vf) || 1, dirL = Math.sign(vl) || 1;

    // target pose
    let target;
if (swinging) {
      const st = pl.startPose || READY;
      target = this.swingParams(v.kindName, v.swingT, st);
      const lead = this.swingParams(v.kindName, v.swingT + 0.04, st); // hips and legs lead the swing
      const lag = this.swingParams(v.kindName, Math.max(0, v.swingT - 0.03), st); // the arm whips through behind the body
      target[0] = lead[0]; target[3] = lead[3]; target[10] = lead[10];
      target[4] = lag[4]; target[5] = lag[5]; target[6] = lag[6];
    } else if (v.serving) {
      target = SERVE_READY.slice();
      // the left hand follows the bouncing ball: arm down when it is low, forearm up when it comes back
      const k = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (bp.y - 0.1) / 0.86));
      target[8] = -0.5 * k; target[9] = 0.1 + 0.65 * k; target[7] = 0.2 - 0.1 * k;
      pl.cur[8] = target[8]; pl.cur[9] = target[9]; pl.cur[7] = target[7]; // stay glued to the ball
    } else {
      target = READY.slice();
      // anticipation: turn the shoulders and take the racket back as the ball closes in
      const bv = this.ballVel;
      if (bv && ownSide) {
        const dx = bp.x - v.x, dz = bp.z - v.z, d = Math.hypot(dx, dz) || 1;
        const closing = -(dx * bv.x + dz * bv.z) / d;
        if (closing > 1.5 && d < 5.5) {
          const prep = Math.min(1, (5.5 - d) / 3.5) * 0.8;
          const right = pl.team === 0 ? bp.x < v.x : bp.x > v.x;
          const kind = bp.y > 2.0 ? 'smash' : right ? 'forehand' : 'backhand';
          const load = SWING[kind][0];
          for (let n = 0; n < target.length; n++) target[n] += (load[n] - target[n]) * prep;
        }
      }
    }
    let emoteHop = 0, emoteHead = null;
    if (pl.emote && !swinging) {
      const e = pl.emote;
      e.t += dt;
      if (e.t > 2.4) pl.emote = null;
      else {
        const fade = Math.min(1, e.t / 0.25, (2.4 - e.t) / 0.5);
        const tgt = e.type === 'win' ? EMOTE_WIN : EMOTE_LOSE;
        for (let n = 0; n < target.length; n++) target[n] += (tgt[n] - target[n]) * fade;
        if (e.type === 'win') emoteHop = Math.abs(Math.sin(e.t * 8)) * 0.1 * fade;
        else emoteHead = { x: 0.4 * fade, y: Math.sin(e.t * 3) * 0.3 * fade };
      }
    }
    const k = swinging ? 1 : 1 - Math.exp(-dt * 13);
    for (let n = 0; n < target.length; n++) pl.cur[n] += (target[n] - pl.cur[n]) * k;
    const P = pl.cur.slice();
    const calm = swinging ? 0.25 : 1;
    // lean into acceleration, sit back when braking
    pl.accS = (pl.accS || 0) + (((sp - (pl.prevSp ?? sp)) / Math.max(dt, 1e-3)) - (pl.accS || 0)) * Math.min(1, dt * 6);
    pl.prevSp = sp;
    const leanA = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(pl.accS * 0.012, -0.12, 0.18) * calm;
    if (pl.accS < -16 && sp > 3 && !swinging) this.onSkid?.(i, v.x);
    const brake = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(-pl.accS * 0.012, 0, 0.18) * calm;
    // landing from a jump: absorb with the knees
    if (pl.wasJump && !(v.jumpT > 0)) pl.land = 0.28;
    pl.wasJump = v.jumpT > 0;
    let landCrouch = 0;
    if (pl.land > 0) { landCrouch = Math.sin((pl.land / 0.28) * Math.PI) * 0.35; pl.land -= dt; }
    // idle life: breathing, weight shift, a light bounce on the toes and a loose racket. Fades out while running.
    const idle = swinging ? 0 : Math.max(0, 1 - speed / 1.5);
    const it = this.clock + pl.idlePh;
    if (idle > 0) {
      const calmer = v.serving ? 0.3 : 1;
      P[3] += Math.sin(it * 3.1) * 0.05 * idle * calmer;
      P[2] += Math.sin(it * 1.7) * 0.02 * idle;
      P[0] += Math.sin(it * 0.8) * 0.06 * idle;
      P[1] += Math.sin(it * 0.8 + 0.5) * 0.05 * idle;
      P[5] += Math.sin(it * 2.2) * 0.07 * idle * calmer;
      P[6] += Math.sin(it * 2.7 + 1) * 0.08 * idle * calmer;
      if (!v.serving) P[8] += Math.sin(it * 2.0 + 2) * 0.06 * idle;
    }
    const sway = Math.sin(it * 0.9) * idle;

    // split-step: quick crouch + hop as the opponent hits
    let hop = 0, splitCrouch = 0;
    if (pl.split > 0) {
      const u = 1 - pl.split / 0.3;
      splitCrouch = Math.sin(Math.min(1, u * 1.6) * Math.PI) * 0.35;
      hop = u > 0.55 ? Math.sin((u - 0.55) / 0.45 * Math.PI) * 0.07 : 0;
      pl.split = Math.max(0, pl.split - dt);
    }
    // jump: coil, extend, tuck
    let crouch = P[3] * (1 - 0.55 * run) + splitCrouch + brake + landCrouch; // upright stride when running
    if (v.jumpT > 0) {
      const u = 1 - v.jumpT / 0.62;
      crouch = u < 0.18 ? 0.5 : u < 0.8 ? 0.08 : 0.45;
    }
    const a = crouch;
    pl.hips.position.y = 0.88 * Math.cos(a) + 0.1 + hop + emoteHop + Math.cos(2 * ph) * 0.025 * run * calm; // gentle two-per-stride bob, no hopping
    pl.hips.rotation.set(0, P[0] - Math.sin(ph) * 0.12 * run * calm * dirF, 0); // hips and shoulders counter-rotate when running
    pl.hips.rotation.x = Math.max(-0.3, Math.min(0.35, vf * 0.03)) * calm;
    pl.hips.rotation.z = -vl * 0.02 * calm + sway * 0.03;
    pl.hips.position.x = sway * 0.025;
    pl.spine.rotation.set(P[2] + (vf > 0 ? Math.min(0.3, vf * 0.04) : Math.max(-0.1, vf * 0.015)) * calm + leanA, P[1] + Math.sin(ph) * 0.18 * run * calm * dirF, 0);

    // legs: base crouch + stride + shuffle + batting step
    const legPose = (L, sgn) => {
      const phL = ph + (sgn > 0 ? 0 : Math.PI);
      const stride = Math.sin(phL) * pitchAmp * dirF * calm;
      const lift = Math.max(0, Math.cos(phL * dirF)) * pitchAmp * 1.4 * calm;
      const step = P[10] * (sgn > 0 ? -0.55 : 0.55); // right leg (sgn<0) steps forward when step>0
      L.thigh.rotation.x = -a - stride - step * 0.8;
      L.knee.rotation.x = 2 * a + lift + Math.abs(step) * 0.35;
      L.foot.rotation.x = -(L.thigh.rotation.x + L.knee.rotation.x) * 0.9 + Math.max(0, -Math.sin(phL) * dirF) * 0.5 * run * calm; // toe-off
      L.thigh.rotation.z = Math.sin(phL) * rollAmp * dirL * sgn * calm + (swinging ? sgn * 0.12 : sgn * 0.06);
    };
    legPose(this.players[i].legL, 1);
    legPose(this.players[i].legR, -1);

    // arms
    const counter = Math.sin(ph) * pitchAmp * 1.1 * calm;
    pl.armR.sh.rotation.set(P[5], P[4], 0);
    pl.armR.elbow.rotation.set(-(P[6] + Math.sin(ph + Math.PI) * 0.15 * run * calm), 0, 0);
    pl.armL.sh.rotation.set(P[8] + counter, P[7], 0.12);
    pl.armL.elbow.rotation.set(-(P[9] + 0.55 * run * calm), 0, 0); // running arms bend
    this.orientRacket(pl);

    // head tracks the ball
    const total = pl.yaw + P[0] + P[1];
    const look = wrap(Math.atan2(bp.x - v.x, bp.z - v.z) - total);
    pl.head.rotation.y += (THREE.MathUtils.clamp(look, -1.0, 1.0) * 0.85 - pl.head.rotation.y) * Math.min(1, dt * 12);
    pl.head.rotation.x = 0.08 - (pl.spine.rotation.x) * 0.6 + Math.min(0.2, Math.max(-0.2, (bp.y - 1.5) * -0.04)); // gaze stays level when the body leans
    if (emoteHead) { pl.head.rotation.x += emoteHead.x; pl.head.rotation.y += emoteHead.y; }

    const lift = v.jumpT > 0 ? Math.sin(Math.PI * (1 - v.jumpT / 0.62)) * 0.7 : 0;
    pl.root.position.set(v.x, lift, v.z);
    pl.blob.position.y = 0.02 - lift;
    pl.blob.scale.setScalar(1 - lift * 0.4);
    if (isMe) {
      this.meRing.position.set(v.x, 0.03, v.z);
      this.reachRing.position.set(v.x, 0.03, v.z);
    }
  }

  setBall(x, y, z) {
    // A jump of more than 1.5 m in one frame is not a flight (a real shot moves at most ~0.7 m per frame): the ball was put back in
    // the server's hand for the next point. Make it appear instead of teleporting in view, and drop the old trail.
    const bp = this.ball.position;
    if (this.ballSeen && Math.hypot(x - bp.x, y - bp.y, z - bp.z) > 1.5) { this.ballAppear = { t: 0 }; this.trailPos.length = 0; }
    this.ballSeen = true;
    this.ball.position.set(x, y, z);
    // the shadow is sharper and darker near the floor, softer and larger when the ball is high
    const k = 1 + y * 0.18;
    this.ballShadow.position.set(x, 0.02, z);
    this.ballShadow.scale.setScalar(k * this.ballBase * 0.7);
    this.ballShadow.material.opacity = Math.max(0.12, 0.5 / (1 + 0.45 * y));
    this.ballHalo.position.set(x, y, z);
    this.ballHalo.quaternion.copy(this.camera.quaternion);
    // plumb line
    const pos = this.ballDrop.geometry.attributes.position;
    pos.setXYZ(0, x, y, z); pos.setXYZ(1, x, 0.02, z); pos.needsUpdate = true;
    this.ballDrop.material.opacity = y > 1.3 ? Math.min(0.28, (y - 1.3) * 0.1) : 0;
  }

  /**
   * The ball spins as it really spins: topspin/backspin about the axis across its path, sidespin about the vertical, and on the
   * floor it rolls. sb = backspin(+)/topspin(-). Also the speed streak and the squash when it hits something.
   */
  animateBall(dt, speed, hx, hz, sb, ss = 0) {
    const L = new THREE.Vector3(hz, 0, -hx);
    if (L.lengthSq() < 1e-6) L.set(1, 0, 0);
    L.normalize();
    const onFloor = this.ball.position.y < 0.06;
    const m = this.ballMesh;
    if (onFloor && speed > 0.3) m.rotateOnWorldAxis(L, (speed / 0.0335) * dt * 0.6); // rolling: w = v / r (eased so it does not alias at 60 fps)
    else {
      m.rotateOnWorldAxis(L, clampSpin(-sb * 5) * dt); // topspin turns the top toward the direction of travel
      if (ss) m.rotateOnWorldAxis(this.upAxis || (this.upAxis = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0)), clampSpin(ss * 5) * dt);
      if (!sb && !ss && speed > 0.5) m.rotateOnWorldAxis(L, 8 * dt); // a gentle default tumble so it never looks frozen
    }
    // squash on impact: flatten along the hit axis for a few frames, then spring back
    const q = this.squash;
    let sx = 1, sy = 1, sz = 1;
    if (q) {
      q.t += dt;
      const u = q.t / 0.09;
      if (u >= 1) this.squash = null;
      else {
        const e = Math.sin(Math.PI * u) * q.amt * 0.32;
        if (q.axis === 'y') { sy = 1 - e; sx = sz = 1 + e * 0.5; }
        else if (q.axis === 'x') { sx = 1 - e; sy = sz = 1 + e * 0.5; }
        else { sz = 1 - e; sx = sy = 1 + e * 0.5; }
      }
    }
    let appear = 1;
    if (this.ballAppear) { // pops in at the server's hand: 0 -> 1 with a small overshoot
      this.ballAppear.t += dt;
      const u = Math.min(1, this.ballAppear.t / 0.28);
      appear = u >= 1 ? 1 : 1 + 2.7 * Math.pow(u - 1, 3) + 1.7 * Math.pow(u - 1, 2);
      if (u >= 1) this.ballAppear = null;
    }
    this.ball.scale.set(this.ballBase * sx * appear, this.ballBase * sy * appear, this.ballBase * sz * appear);
    // speed streak
    const v = this.ballVel || { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 };
    const sp = Math.hypot(v.x, v.y, v.z);
    const showStreak = this.trailOn && sp > 14 && !this.ballAppear;
    this.streak.visible = showStreak;
    if (showStreak) {
      const dir = new THREE.Vector3(v.x, v.y, v.z).normalize().negate(); // the tail points backwards along the flight
      this.streak.position.copy(this.ball.position);
      this.streak.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), dir);
      const len = Math.min(0.9, (sp - 14) * 0.03 + 0.12);
      this.streak.scale.set(this.ballBase, len, this.ballBase);
      this.streak.material.opacity = Math.min(0.32, (sp - 14) / 40);
    }
    const on = this.trailOn && speed > 9;
    this.trailPos.unshift(this.ball.position.clone());
    if (this.trailPos.length > 20) this.trailPos.pop();
    this.trail.forEach((g, i) => {
      const p = this.trailPos[(i + 1) * 2 - 1];
      g.visible = !!(on && p);
      if (!g.visible) return;
      g.position.copy(p);
      g.scale.setScalar(this.ballBase * (1 - i * 0.08));
      g.material.opacity = Math.min(0.5, (speed - 9) / 20) * (1 - i / 9);
    });
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ effects

  buildFx() {
    this.fx = [];
    this.ringGeo = new THREE.RingGeometry(0.08, 0.1, 28);
    this.puffGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.05, 8, 6);
    this.trailOn = true;
  }

  spawnFx(kind, x, y, z, power = 1, axis = 'z') {
    const add = (mesh, life, update) => { this.scene.add(mesh); this.fx.push({ mesh, life, t: 0, update }); };
    if (kind === 'floor' || kind === 'wall') this.squash = { t: 0, amt: Math.min(1, power), axis: kind === 'floor' ? 'y' : axis };
    if (kind === 'floor') {
      // a scuff mark where the ball landed: it stays a moment so the bounce spot can be judged
      const mark = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(0.05 + 0.03 * power, 16), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x0b1a10, transparent: true, opacity: 0.45, depthWrite: false }));
      mark.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; mark.position.set(x, 0.032, z);
      add(mark, 1.4, (f, u) => { f.mesh.material.opacity = 0.45 * (1 - u); });
      const n = 3 + Math.round(power * 3);
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const m = new THREE.Mesh(this.puffGeo, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xcfe0d6, transparent: true, opacity: 0.45, depthWrite: false }));
        m.position.set(x, 0.04, z);
        const ang = Math.random() * 6.28, sp = 0.6 + Math.random() * 1.2 * power;
        const vx = Math.cos(ang) * sp, vz = Math.sin(ang) * sp, vy = 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.6;
        add(m, 0.5, (f, u) => { f.mesh.position.x += vx * 0.016; f.mesh.position.z += vz * 0.016; f.mesh.position.y += vy * 0.016 * (1 - u); f.mesh.scale.setScalar(1 + u * 3); f.mesh.material.opacity = 0.4 * (1 - u); });
      }
      const r = new THREE.Mesh(this.ringGeo, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffffff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.5, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide }));
      r.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; r.position.set(x, 0.035, z);
      add(r, 0.3, (f, u) => { f.mesh.scale.setScalar(1 + u * 5 * power); f.mesh.material.opacity = 0.5 * (1 - u); });
    } else if (kind === 'wall') {
      const r = new THREE.Mesh(this.ringGeo, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xbfe3ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.8, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide }));
      r.position.set(x, y, z);
      r.rotation.y = axis === 'x' ? Math.PI / 2 : 0;
      add(r, 0.35, (f, u) => { f.mesh.scale.setScalar(1 + u * 9 * power); f.mesh.material.opacity = 0.8 * (1 - u); });
    } else if (kind === 'net') {
      for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        const m = new THREE.Mesh(this.puffGeo, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xffffff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.5, depthWrite: false }));
        m.position.set(x, y, 0);
        const vx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.2, vy = (Math.random() - 0.2) * 0.8;
        add(m, 0.35, (f, u) => { f.mesh.position.x += vx * 0.016; f.mesh.position.y += vy * 0.016; f.mesh.material.opacity = 0.5 * (1 - u); });
      }
    } else if (kind === 'hit') {
      const r = new THREE.Mesh(this.ringGeo, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xf3ff7a, transparent: true, opacity: 0.9, depthWrite: false, depthTest: false }));
      r.position.set(x, y, z);
      add(r, 0.22, (f, u) => { f.mesh.quaternion.copy(this.camera.quaternion); f.mesh.scale.setScalar((1 + u * 4) * power); f.mesh.material.opacity = 0.9 * (1 - u); });
    }
  }

  updateFx(dt) {
    for (let i = this.fx.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const f = this.fx[i];
      f.t += dt;
      const u = f.t / f.life;
      if (u >= 1) { this.scene.remove(f.mesh); f.mesh.material.dispose(); this.fx.splice(i, 1); continue; }
      f.update(f, u);
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ crowd + reflections

  /**
   * Stands with instanced spectators. cfg: rows, sides, back, density (0..1), pal (colours), xBase, rowStep, stepH, stepMat.
   * Everything is added to `group` so it is disposed with the environment.
   */
  makeCrowd(group, cfg = {}) {
    const { rows = 5, sides = true, back = false, density = 0.7, xBase = 7.3, rowStep = 1.05, stepH = 0.45 } = cfg;
    const pal = cfg.pal || [0x2f4a6b, 0x6b2f3a, 0x3d6b52, 0x7a6a3a, 0x4b4b5e, 0x8a8f99, 0x253244];
    const stepMat = cfg.stepMat || new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x141c28, roughness: 0.9 });
    const people = [];
    const row = (x, z, along, r, rotY) => {
      const h = stepH * (r + 1);
      const step = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(along ? 24 : rowStep, h, along ? rowStep : 24), stepMat);
      step.position.set(x, h / 2, z); step.receiveShadow = true; group.add(step);
      for (let t = -11; t <= 11; t += 0.62) {
        if (Math.random() > density) continue;
        const j = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.15;
        people.push(along ? [t + j, h + 0.3, z + j, rotY] : [x + j, h + 0.3, t + j, rotY]);
      }
    };
    if (sides) for (const sx of [-1, 1]) for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) row(sx * (xBase + r * rowStep), 0, false, r, sx > 0 ? -Math.PI / 2 : Math.PI / 2);
    if (back) for (const sz of [-1, 1]) for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) row(0, sz * (13.5 + r * rowStep), true, r, sz > 0 ? Math.PI : 0);
    if (!people.length) return;
    const bm = new THREE.InstancedMesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(0.17, 0.25, 3, 8), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ roughness: 0.9 }), people.length);
    const hm = new THREE.InstancedMesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.115, 10, 8), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ roughness: 0.8 }), people.length);
    const skins = [0xc89878, 0x8d5a3c, 0xe0b799, 0xa87450];
    const m4 = new THREE.Matrix4(), col = new THREE.Color();
    const phase = [];
    people.forEach(([x, y, z, ry], i) => {
      m4.makeRotationY(ry).setPosition(x, y, z);
      bm.setMatrixAt(i, m4); bm.setColorAt(i, col.setHex(pal[(Math.random() * pal.length) | 0]));
      m4.setPosition(x, y + 0.36, z); hm.setMatrixAt(i, m4); hm.setColorAt(i, col.setHex(skins[(Math.random() * skins.length) | 0]));
      phase.push(Math.random() * 6);
    });
    group.add(bm, hm);
    this.crowds.push({ bm, hm, people, phase });
  }

  cheerCrowd() { this.cheerT = 1.6; }

  updateCrowd() {
    if (!this.cheerT || this.cheerT <= 0) return;
    this.cheerT -= this.dt;
    const m4 = new THREE.Matrix4();
    const amp = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, this.cheerT)) * 0.18;
    for (const c of this.crowds) {
      c.people.forEach(([x, y, z, ry], i) => {
        const hop = Math.abs(Math.sin(this.clock * 9 + c.phase[i])) * amp;
        m4.makeRotationY(ry).setPosition(x, y + hop, z); c.bm.setMatrixAt(i, m4);
        m4.setPosition(x, y + 0.36 + hop, z); c.hm.setMatrixAt(i, m4);
      });
      c.bm.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true; c.hm.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ courts (environments)

  adTexture(text, bg, fg, sub) { return bannerTexture(text, bg, fg, sub); }

  skylineTexture() {
    return canvasTex(512, 128, (g, w, h) => {
      g.clearRect(0, 0, w, h); g.fillStyle = '#0a0e16';
      let x = 0;
      while (x < w) {
        const bw = 18 + Math.random() * 40, bh = 30 + Math.random() * 90;
        g.fillRect(x, h - bh, bw, bh);
        g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,214,140,.8)';
        for (let yy = h - bh + 6; yy < h - 4; yy += 9) for (let xx = x + 4; xx < x + bw - 4; xx += 8) if (Math.random() < 0.35) g.fillRect(xx, yy, 3, 4);
        g.fillStyle = '#0a0e16'; x += bw + 2;
      }
    });
  }

  /** Switch the whole environment: surroundings, lighting, turf colour and advertising. */
  setCourt(id) {
    if (this.courtId === id) return;
    this.courtId = id;
    if (this.envGroup) {
      this.scene.remove(this.envGroup);
      this.envGroup.traverse((o) => { o.geometry?.dispose(); (Array.isArray(o.material) ? o.material : [o.material]).forEach((m) => { if (m) { m.map?.dispose(); m.dispose(); } }); });
    }
    this.crowds = [];
    const env = buildEnvironment(id, this);
    this.envGroup = env.group;
    this.scene.add(env.group);
    this.applyLight(env.light);
    this.setTurf(env.turf);
    this.buildAds(env.ads);
  }

  applyLight(l) {
    if (l.background2D) this.scene.background = l.bg; else this.scene.background = new THREE.Color(l.bg);
    this.scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(l.fog[0], l.fog[1], l.fog[2]);
    this.hemi.color.set(l.hemi[0]); this.hemi.groundColor.set(l.hemi[1]); this.hemi.intensity = l.hemi[2];
    this.sun.color.set(l.sun.color); this.sun.intensity = l.sun.intensity; this.sun.position.set(...l.sun.pos);
    this.renderer.toneMappingExposure = l.exposure;
    this.scene.environmentIntensity = l.envIntensity;
  }

  setTurf(t) {
    const mat = this.turfMesh.material;
    mat.map?.dispose();
    mat.map = turfTexture(t.base);
    mat.needsUpdate = true;
    const om = this.outFloor.material;
    om.map?.dispose(); om.map = null;
    om.color.set(t.out.color); om.roughness = t.out.rough;
    if (t.out.tiles) {
      om.color.set(0xffffff);
      om.map = canvasTex(256, 256, (g, w, h) => {
        g.fillStyle = '#b86a42'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
        for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 64) for (let x = 0; x < w; x += 64) { g.fillStyle = `rgba(${150 + Math.random() * 50 | 0},${70 + Math.random() * 30 | 0},40,.45)`; g.fillRect(x + 2, y + 2, 60, 60); }
        g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(60,30,18,.55)'; g.lineWidth = 3; for (let i = 0; i <= w; i += 64) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(i, 0); g.lineTo(i, h); g.moveTo(0, i); g.lineTo(w, i); g.stroke(); }
      }, { repeat: [14, 14] });
    }
    om.needsUpdate = true;
  }

  buildAds(a) {
    if (this.adGroup) { this.courtGroup.remove(this.adGroup); this.adGroup.traverse((o) => { o.geometry?.dispose(); o.material?.map?.dispose(); o.material?.dispose(); }); }
    const g = new THREE.Group();
    const tex = bannerTexture(a.text, a.bg, a.fg, a.sub); tex.wrapS = THREE.RepeatWrapping; tex.repeat.set(2.8, 1);
    const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: tex });
    for (const sx of [-1, 1]) { const ad = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(20, 0.9), mat); ad.position.set(sx * 6.05, 0.45, 0); ad.rotation.y = -sx * Math.PI / 2; g.add(ad); }
    for (const sz of [-1, 1]) { const ad = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(12, 0.9), mat); ad.position.set(0, 0.45, sz * 10.9); ad.rotation.y = sz > 0 ? Math.PI : 0; g.add(ad); }
    this.adGroup = g; this.courtGroup.add(g);
  }

  /** Short field-of-view kick on powerful hits. */
  punch(k) { this.fovKick = Math.max(this.fovKick, k); }

  buildEnv() {
    const pm = new THREE.PMREMGenerator(this.renderer);
    this.scene.environment = pm.fromScene(new RoomEnvironment(), 0.04).texture;
    this.scene.environmentIntensity = 0.32;
    pm.dispose();
  }

  render(dt = 0.016) {
    this.dt = dt;
    this.clock += dt;
    this.updateFx(dt);
    this.updateCrowd();
    if (this.backWall) { const cz = this.camera.position.z; for (const o of this.backWall) o.visible = !(Math.abs(cz) > 9.5 && Math.sign(cz) === Math.sign(o.position.z)); }
    if (this.fovKick > 0.01) { this.fovKick *= Math.exp(-dt * 9); this.camera.fov = this.baseFov + this.fovKick; this.camera.updateProjectionMatrix(); } else if (this.camera.fov !== this.baseFov && !window.__freeze) { this.camera.fov = this.baseFov; this.camera.updateProjectionMatrix(); }
    if (this.bloom && this.composer) this.composer.render(dt); else this.renderer.render(this.scene, this.camera);
  }
}
===== END FILE: client/js/scene.js =====

===== FILE: client/js/settings.js =====
import { store } from './net.js';

const DEFAULTS = {
  quality: 'medium', // low | medium | high
  master: 0.7,
  sfx: 0.9,
  ambience: 0.5,
  voice: 0.8,
  music: 0.35,
  announcer: false, // spoken score calls (uses the browser's speech synthesis)
  camera: 'player', // player | broadcast | tactical | custom
  camDist: 7.2,
  camHeight: 4.6,
  camSmooth: 7,
  camFov: 52,
  ballTrack: true, // the view drifts toward the ball (never changes how you move or aim)
  ballScale: 3,
  timing: true,
  replays: true, // instant replay after each point (solo matches)
  trail: true,
  hints: true,
  highContrast: false,
  invertAim: false,
};

let cur = { ...DEFAULTS };
try { cur = { ...DEFAULTS, ...JSON.parse(store.get('padel.settings', '{}')) }; } catch { /* keep defaults */ }
const listeners = [];

export const settings = cur;
export function setSetting(k, v) {
  cur[k] = v;
  store.set('padel.settings', JSON.stringify(cur));
  document.body.classList.toggle('hc', !!cur.highContrast);
  listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(k, v));
}
export function onSetting(fn) { listeners.push(fn); }
document.body.classList.toggle('hc', !!cur.highContrast);
===== END FILE: client/js/settings.js =====

===== FILE: client/js/ui.js =====
// DOM screens: home, lobby, HUD, results, modals. Pure presentation + callbacks into main.js.
import { settings, setSetting } from './settings.js';
import { BOT_LEVELS, BOT_LABELS } from '/shared/ai.js';
import { controlsHtml } from './controls-ui.js';
import { ARCHETYPES, HAIR_STYLES, SKIN_TONES, RACKETS, COURTS, COURT_IDS } from '/shared/looks.js';

const COURT_SHORT = { indoor: 'Indoor', outdoor: 'Outdoor', night: 'Night', rooftop: 'Rooftop' };

export const esc = (s) => String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
const $ = (sel, root = document) => root.querySelector(sel);
const TEAM_NAMES = ['Team A', 'Team B'];
const TEAM_COLORS = ['#f2f5f8', '#3d78c4'];

export function toast(text, err = false) {
  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = `toast${err ? ' err' : ''}`;
  el.textContent = text;
  $('#toasts').appendChild(el);
  setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 3600);
}

const pingClass = (rtt) => (rtt === 0 ? '' : rtt < 80 ? 'ok' : rtt < 160 ? 'warn' : 'bad');

export class UI {
  constructor(root, cb) {
    this.root = root;
    this.cb = cb;
    this.chatLines = [];
    this.modal = null;
    root.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      const el = e.target.closest('[data-act]');
      if (!el || el.disabled) return;
      this.cb.sound.unlock();
      this.cb.sound.play('ui');
      this.cb.act(el.dataset.act, el.dataset, el);
    });
    root.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (e.key !== 'Enter') return;
      const a = e.target.dataset?.enter;
      if (a) this.cb.act(a, e.target.dataset, e.target);
    });
  }

  clear() { this.root.innerHTML = ''; this.hud = null; this.modal = null; }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ home

  home({ name, profile, status, joinCode, level = 'easy', careerStars = null, court = 'indoor', online = null }) {
    const lvl = profile?.level ?? 1;
    const xp = profile?.xp ?? 0;
    const into = xp - (lvl - 1) ** 2 * 100;
    const need = lvl ** 2 * 100 - (lvl - 1) ** 2 * 100;
    this.root.innerHTML = `
      <div class="home"><div class="home-inner">
        <div class="brand"><div class="brand-mark"></div><h1>PADEL CLUB</h1></div>
