        <p class="tagline">Four-player online doubles. Grab three friends, share a room code, and play on a real glass-walled court.</p>
        <div class="profile-chip">
          <div class="avatar">${esc((name || 'G')[0].toUpperCase())}</div>
          <div class="grow">
            <input type="text" id="nameInput" maxlength="16" value="${esc(name)}" placeholder="Your name" data-enter="rename" aria-label="Player name">
            <div class="row" style="margin-top:8px"><span class="label">Level ${lvl}</span><div class="bar grow"><i style="width:${Math.min(100, (into / need) * 100)}%"></i></div></div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="menu">
          <button class="btn primary" data-act="quick" ${status !== 'online' ? 'disabled' : ''}>Quick match <small id="onlineN">${online ? online + ' online' : 'find players now'}</small></button>
          <button class="btn" data-act="create" ${status !== 'online' ? 'disabled' : ''}>Play with friends <small>create private room</small></button>
          <button class="btn" data-act="career">Career <small>${careerStars || 'seasons, rankings, training'}</small></button>
          <button class="btn" data-act="equipment">Equipment <small>player &amp; racket</small></button>
          <button class="btn" data-act="upgrades">Upgrades <small>speed, power, stamina…</small></button>
          <div class="row levelrow"><span class="label">Court</span><div class="seg grow">${COURT_IDS.map((c) => `<button data-act="court" data-val="${c}" class="${court === c ? 'on' : ''}">${COURT_SHORT[c]}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
          <div class="row levelrow"><span class="label">Bots</span><div class="seg grow">${BOT_LEVELS.map((l) => `<button data-act="level" data-val="${l}" class="${level === l ? 'on' : ''}">${BOT_LABELS[l]}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
          <button class="btn" data-act="practice" ${status !== 'online' ? 'disabled' : ''}>Practice 2 vs 2 <small>you + 3 bots</small></button>
          <button class="btn" data-act="practice1" ${status !== 'online' ? 'disabled' : ''}>1 vs 1 <small>you vs AI</small></button>
          <div class="joinrow">
            <input type="text" id="joinCode" maxlength="5" placeholder="CODE" value="${esc(joinCode || '')}" data-enter="join" aria-label="Room code">
            <button class="btn grow" data-act="join" ${status !== 'online' ? 'disabled' : ''}>Join room</button>
          </div>
          <div class="row">
            <button class="btn ghost grow" data-act="profile">Profile</button>
            <button class="btn ghost grow" data-act="history">History</button>
            <button class="btn ghost grow" data-act="rooms">Rooms</button>
            <button class="btn ghost grow" data-act="controls">How to play</button>
            <button class="btn ghost grow" data-act="settings">Settings</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="status"><span class="dot ${status === 'online' ? 'ok' : 'warn'}"></span>${status === 'online' ? 'Connected to server' : status === 'reconnecting' ? 'Connection lost — retrying…' : 'Connecting…'}</div>
      </div></div>`;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ lobby

  lobby(room, { selected, canStartReason }) {
    const me = room.you;
    const isHost = room.hostId === me;
    const s = room.settings;
    const singles = s.mode === 'singles';
    const link = `${location.origin}/?room=${room.code}`;
    const seat = (i) => {
      const sl = room.slots[i];
      if (!sl) {
        return `<div class="seat empty"><span>Empty seat</span><button class="btn small" data-act="${selected ? 'place' : 'sit'}" data-slot="${i}">${selected ? 'Place here' : 'Sit here'}</button></div>`;
      }
      if (sl.kind === 'bot') {
        return `<div class="seat empty"><span class="name"><span class="tag">AI</span> Bot player</span><button class="btn small" data-act="${selected ? 'place' : 'sit'}" data-slot="${i}">${selected ? 'Place here' : 'Take this seat'}</button></div>`;
      }
      const mine = sl.id === me;
      const tags = [
        sl.id === room.hostId ? '<span class="tag host">Host</span>' : '',
        sl.connected ? (sl.ready ? '<span class="tag ready">Ready</span>' : '<span class="tag">Not ready</span>') : '<span class="tag off">Disconnected</span>',
      ].join(' ');
      const swap = isHost ? `<button class="btn small ghost" data-act="${selected ? 'place' : 'select'}" data-slot="${i}" data-id="${sl.id}">${selected === sl.id ? 'Cancel' : selected ? 'Swap here' : 'Move'}</button>` : '';
      const takeSeat = !mine && !isHost && selected ? '' : '';
      return `<div class="seat ${mine ? 'me' : ''} ${selected === sl.id ? 'sel' : ''}">
        <div class="name"><span class="dot ${pingClass(sl.rtt)}"></span>${esc(sl.name)}${mine ? ' <span class="mute">(you)</span>' : ''}</div>
        <div class="row">${tags}<span class="grow"></span><span class="mute" style="font-size:12px">${sl.rtt ? sl.rtt + ' ms' : ''}</span>${swap}${takeSeat}</div>
      </div>`;
    };
    const seg = (key, opts, val, enabled) => `<div class="seg">${opts.map(([v, l]) => `<button data-act="set" data-key="${key}" data-val="${v}" class="${String(val) === String(v) ? 'on' : ''}" ${enabled ? '' : 'disabled'}>${l}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
    const meMember = room.slots.find((x) => x?.id === me);
    this.root.innerHTML = `
      <div class="screen"><div class="lobby">
        <div class="panel lobby-head">
          <div><div class="label">Room code</div><div class="code">${esc(room.code)}</div></div>
          <div class="col grow" style="gap:6px"><span class="label">Invite link</span><input type="text" readonly value="${esc(link)}" onclick="this.select()" aria-label="Invite link"></div>
          <button class="btn" data-act="copy" data-text="${esc(link)}">Copy link</button>
          <button class="btn ghost" data-act="leave">Leave room</button>
        </div>
        <div class="panel col">
          <div class="teams">
            <div class="team"><h3><span class="swatch" style="background:${TEAM_COLORS[0]}"></span>${TEAM_NAMES[0]}</h3>${seat(0)}${singles ? '' : seat(1)}</div>
            <div class="team"><h3><span class="swatch" style="background:${TEAM_COLORS[1]}"></span>${TEAM_NAMES[1]}</h3>${seat(2)}${singles ? '' : seat(3)}</div>
          </div>
          <div class="row" style="margin-top:6px">
            ${isHost
    ? `<button class="btn primary grow" data-act="start" ${canStartReason ? 'disabled' : ''}>Start match</button><span class="mute" style="font-size:13px">${esc(canStartReason || 'Everyone is ready')}</span>`
    : `<button class="btn ${meMember?.ready ? '' : 'primary'} grow" data-act="ready" data-val="${meMember?.ready ? 0 : 1}">${meMember?.ready ? 'Cancel ready' : 'I am ready'}</button><span class="mute" style="font-size:13px">Waiting for the host to start</span>`}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="panel col">
          <div class="label">Match format ${isHost ? '' : '(host controls)'}</div>
          <div class="setting"><span>Mode</span>${seg('mode', [['doubles', '2 vs 2'], ['singles', '1 vs 1']], s.mode || 'doubles', isHost)}</div>
          <div class="setting"><span>Court</span>${seg('court', COURT_IDS.map((c) => [c, COURT_SHORT[c]]), s.court || 'indoor', isHost)}</div>
          <div class="setting"><span>Sets</span>${seg('sets', [[1, 'Single set'], [3, 'Best of 3']], s.sets, isHost)}</div>
          <div class="setting"><span>Games per set</span>${seg('gamesPerSet', [[2, '2'], [4, '4'], [6, '6']], s.gamesPerSet, isHost)}</div>
          <div class="setting"><span>Deuce</span>${seg('goldenPoint', [[false, 'Advantage'], [true, 'Golden point']], s.goldenPoint, isHost)}</div>
          <div class="setting"><span>AI level</span>${seg('difficulty', BOT_LEVELS.map((l) => [l, BOT_LABELS[l]]), s.difficulty, isHost)}</div>
          <div class="setting"><span>Fill empty seats with AI</span>${seg('fillBots', [[true, 'On'], [false, 'Off']], s.fillBots, isHost)}</div>
          <div class="setting"><span>Room visibility</span>${seg('public', [[false, 'Private'], [true, 'Public']], !!s.public, isHost)}</div>
          <div class="chat grow">
            <div class="label" style="margin-top:6px">Chat</div>
            <div class="chat-log" id="chatLog"></div>
            <div class="quick">${['Ready?', 'Nice!', 'Good luck', 'Rematch?', 'One sec'].map((t) => `<button data-act="quick" data-text="${esc(t)}">${esc(t)}</button>`).join('')}</div>
            <input type="text" id="chatInput" maxlength="140" placeholder="Say something…" data-enter="chat" aria-label="Chat message">
          </div>
        </div>
      </div></div>`;
    this.renderChat();
  }

  addChat(m) {
    this.chatLines.push(m);
    if (this.chatLines.length > 60) this.chatLines.shift();
    this.renderChat();
    if (this.hud) this.hud.feedLine(m);
  }

  renderChat() {
    const log = $('#chatLog');
    if (!log) return;
    log.innerHTML = this.chatLines.map((m) => `<div><b style="color:${TEAM_COLORS[(m.slot ?? 0) >> 1] || '#fff'}">${esc(m.name)}</b> ${esc(m.text)}</div>`).join('');
    log.scrollTop = log.scrollHeight;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ HUD

  showHud(names, settingsMatch) {
    this.root.innerHTML = `
      <div class="hud">
        <div class="score" id="score"></div>
        <div class="topright"><span id="clock">0:00</span><span class="status"><span class="dot" id="pingDot"></span><span id="ping">– ms</span></span></div>
        <div class="drillbar hidden" id="drillbar"></div>
        <div class="rallychip hidden" id="rally"></div>
        <div class="chargebar hidden" id="charge"><span>Serve power · full = risk of going long</span><div><i></i></div></div>
        <div class="banner" id="banner"><div class="big"></div><div class="sub"></div></div>
        <div class="feedback" id="feedback"></div>
        <div class="timing hidden" id="timing"><i class="t-out"></i><i class="t-in"></i><span></span></div>
        <div class="chatfeed" id="feed"></div>
        <div class="bottomleft">
          <div class="label" id="stamLabel">Stamina</div><div class="bar" style="margin-top:6px"><i id="stam" style="width:100%"></i></div>
          <div class="keys" id="keys">WASD or arrows move · Shift sprint · X jump<br><b>Space hit</b> (serve: hold to add power, release to hit) · G globo · R slice<br>Q soft / víbora · E bandeja / block · M mouse aim<br>Hold a direction as you hit: that is where the ball goes<br><br>Tab stats · Esc menu · 1–4 quick chat</div>
        </div>
        <div class="statsov hidden" id="statsov"><div class="panel modal" id="statsbody"></div></div>
        <div class="reconnect hidden" id="recon">Connection lost — reconnecting…</div>
      </div>`;
    const ui = this;
    this.hud = {
      names,
      singles: settingsMatch?.mode === 'singles',
      lastScore: null,
      stats: null,
      hideKeys: setTimeout(() => { const k = $('#keys'); if (k) k.style.opacity = 0.0; }, 18000),
      setScore(v, n = this.names) {
        const prev = this.lastScore;
        this.lastScore = v;
        const el = $('#score');
        if (!el) return;
        const ns = v.sets.length;
        const cols = `5px 14px minmax(110px, 1fr) ${'26px '.repeat(ns)}40px 50px`;
        const row = (t) => {
          const nm = this.singles ? esc(n[t * 2]) : `${esc(n[t * 2])} <i>/</i> ${esc(n[t * 2 + 1])}`;
          const serving = v.server && v.server.team === t;
          const done = v.sets.map((s) => `<span class="n ${s[t] > s[1 - t] ? 'w' : ''}">${s[t]}</span>`).join('');
          const bump = (now, was) => (prev && was !== undefined && now !== was ? ' bump' : '');
          const gBump = bump(v.games[t], prev?.games?.[t]);
          const pBump = bump(v.points[t], prev?.points?.[t]);
          return `<div class="tr t${t}" style="grid-template-columns:${cols}"><span class="tb" style="background:${TEAM_COLORS[t]}"></span><span class="sv ${serving ? 'on' : ''}"></span><span class="tn">${nm}</span>${done}<span class="g${gBump}">${v.games[t]}</span><span class="p${pBump}">${v.points[t]}</span></div>`;
        };
        const flag = v.matchPoint ? 'Match point' : v.setPoint ? 'Set point' : v.gamePoint !== null && v.gamePoint !== undefined ? 'Game point' : v.tiebreak ? 'Tiebreak' : v.label;
        const hot = v.matchPoint ? ' hot' : v.setPoint ? ' warm' : '';
        const head = `<div class="hd" style="grid-template-columns:1fr ${'26px '.repeat(ns)}40px 50px"><span></span>${v.sets.map((_, i) => `<span>S${i + 1}</span>`).join('')}<span>Games</span><span>Pts</span></div>`;
        el.innerHTML = head + row(0) + row(1) + (flag ? `<div class="meta${hot}">${esc(flag)}</div>` : '');
      },
      setClock(sec) {
        const c = $('#clock');
        if (c) c.textContent = `${Math.floor(sec / 60)}:${String(Math.floor(sec % 60)).padStart(2, '0')}`;
      },
      setVitals(stam, rtt) {
        const s = $('#stam'); if (s) { s.style.width = `${stam}%`; s.classList.toggle('tired', stam < 50); s.classList.toggle('spent', stam < 25); }
        const sl = $('#stamLabel'); if (sl) sl.textContent = stam < 25 ? 'Exhausted' : stam < 50 ? 'Tired' : 'Stamina';
        const p = $('#ping'); if (p) p.textContent = rtt ? `${rtt} ms` : '– ms';
        const d = $('#pingDot'); if (d) d.className = `dot ${pingClass(rtt)}`;
      },
      banner(big, sub, ms) {
        const b = $('#banner'); if (!b) return;
        b.querySelector('.big').textContent = big;
        b.querySelector('.sub').textContent = sub || '';
        b.classList.add('show');
        clearTimeout(this.bt);
        if (ms) this.bt = setTimeout(() => b.classList.remove('show'), ms);
      },
      feedback(label, kind) {
        const f = $('#feedback'); if (!f || !settings.timing) return;
        const nice = { perfect: 'Perfect', clean: 'Clean', good: 'Good', early: 'Early', late: 'Late', 'off-centre': 'Off-centre', stretched: 'Stretched', poor: 'Poor contact', serve: 'Serve' }[label] || label;
        const shot = { lob: 'globo', vibora: 'víbora', chiquita: 'chiquita', drop: 'dejada', block: 'bloqueo', slice: 'slice', bandeja: 'bandeja', smash: 'remate', serve: 'saque' }[kind] || kind;
        f.textContent = `${nice} · ${shot}`;
        f.className = `feedback show ${label === 'perfect' ? 'perfect' : label === 'good' || label === 'clean' || label === 'serve' ? 'good' : 'bad'}`;
        clearTimeout(this.ft);
        this.ft = setTimeout(() => f.classList.remove('show'), 900);
      },
      feedLine(m) {
        const feed = $('#feed'); if (!feed) return;
        const d = document.createElement('div');
        d.innerHTML = `<b style="color:${TEAM_COLORS[(m.slot ?? 0) >> 1] || '#fff'}">${esc(m.name)}</b> ${esc(m.text)}`;
        feed.appendChild(d);
        setTimeout(() => d.remove(), 5000);
      },
      setStats(st, longest) { this.stats = { st, longest }; if (!$('#statsov')?.classList.contains('hidden')) this.showStats(true); },
      showStats(on) {
        const o = $('#statsov'); if (!o) return;
        o.classList.toggle('hidden', !on);
        if (!on) return;
        const s = this.stats?.st;
        $('#statsbody').innerHTML = `<h2>Match stats</h2>${s ? statsTable(s, this.names, this.singles) + `<p class="mute" style="margin-top:10px">Longest rally: ${this.stats.longest} shots</p>` : '<p class="mute">Stats appear after the first point.</p>'}`;
      },
      timing(x, y, outPx, zone, text) {
        const t = $('#timing'); if (!t) return;
        if (x === null) { t.classList.add('hidden'); return; }
        t.classList.remove('hidden');
        t.style.transform = `translate(${x}px, ${y}px)`;
        t.className = `timing z-${zone}`;
        t.querySelector('.t-out').style.width = t.querySelector('.t-out').style.height = `${outPx * 2}px`;
        t.querySelector('span').textContent = text;
      },
      charge(f) { const c = $('#charge'); if (!c) return; if (f === null) { c.classList.add('hidden'); return; } c.classList.remove('hidden'); const i = c.querySelector('i'); i.style.width = `${f * 100}%`; c.classList.toggle('full', f >= 0.98); },
      rally(n) { const r = $('#rally'); if (!r) return; if (!n) { r.classList.add('hidden'); return; } r.innerHTML = `<small>Rally</small><b>${n}</b><small>${n === 1 ? 'shot' : 'shots'}</small>`; r.classList.remove('hidden'); r.classList.toggle('long', n >= 10); },
      setDrill(text) { const d = $('#drillbar'); if (!d) return; d.classList.toggle('hidden', !text); d.textContent = text || ''; },
      connection(ok) { $('#recon')?.classList.toggle('hidden', ok); },
    };
    void ui; void settingsMatch;
    return this.hud;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ results

  results(m, room, myId, rematchVotes = []) {
    const r = m.results;
    const win = r.winner;
    const names = m.names;
    const sets = r.score.sets.map((s) => `${s[0]}–${s[1]}`).join('  ·  ');
    const isHost = room?.hostId === myId;
    const my = m.progress?.[myId];
    const mins = `${Math.floor(r.duration / 60)}:${String(r.duration % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    this.root.innerHTML = `
      <div class="screen"><div class="panel modal" style="width:min(720px,100%)">
        <div class="label">Final</div>
        <div class="winner" style="color:${TEAM_COLORS[win]}">${room?.settings?.mode === 'singles' ? esc(names[win * 2]) : `${esc(names[win * 2])} / ${esc(names[win * 2 + 1])}`} win</div>
        <p style="font-size:22px;margin:6px 0 16px;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums">${sets}</p>
        <div class="row" style="gap:28px;margin-bottom:14px">
          <div><div class="label">Duration</div>${mins}</div>
          <div><div class="label">Longest rally</div>${r.longestRally} shots</div>
          <div><div class="label">Points</div>${r.teamPoints[0]} – ${r.teamPoints[1]}</div>
          ${my ? `<div><div class="label">XP earned</div>+${my.xp} · Level ${my.level}</div>` : ''}
        </div>
        ${statsTable(r.players, names, room?.settings?.mode === 'singles')}
        ${m.career ? m.career.html : ''}
        <div class="row" style="margin-top:18px">
          ${m.career ? `<button class="btn primary grow" data-act="eventContinue">${m.career.over ? 'Back to career' : 'Continue'}</button>
          <button class="btn ghost" data-act="leaveLocal">Main menu</button>` : `
          <button class="btn primary grow" data-act="rematch">Rematch ${rematchVotes.length ? `(${rematchVotes.length} ready)` : ''}</button>
          ${isHost ? '<button class="btn grow" data-act="tolobby">Back to lobby</button>' : ''}
          <button class="btn ghost" data-act="leave">Leave</button>`}
        </div>
      </div></div>`;
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ modals

  roomsModal(list) {
    const rows = list === null ? '<p class="mute">Loading…</p>' : list.length === 0
      ? '<p class="mute">No public rooms right now. Create one and set <b>Room visibility → Public</b> in the lobby.</p>'
      : `<table class="table"><tr><th>Host</th><th>Mode</th><th>Bots</th><th>Players</th><th></th></tr>${list.map((r) => `<tr><td>${esc(r.host)}</td><td>${r.mode === 'singles' ? '1 vs 1' : '2 vs 2'}</td><td>${BOT_LABELS[r.difficulty] || r.difficulty}</td><td>${r.players}/${r.max}</td><td><button class="btn small primary" data-act="joinRoom" data-code="${esc(r.code)}">Join</button></td></tr>`).join('')}</table>`;
    this.openModal(`<h2>Public rooms</h2>${rows}<div class="row" style="margin-top:12px"><button class="btn small" data-act="rooms">Refresh</button></div>`);
  }

  pauseOverlay(html) {
    let el = document.getElementById('pauseov');
    if (!el) { el = document.createElement('div'); el.className = 'screen'; el.id = 'pauseov'; el.style.zIndex = 30; this.root.appendChild(el); }
    el.innerHTML = `<div class="panel modal" style="text-align:center">${html}</div>`;
  }

  clearPauseOverlay() { document.getElementById('pauseov')?.remove(); }

  quickModal(m) {
    const text = `<div class="qm"><div class="spin"></div><p style="font-size:18px;font-weight:700;margin:10px 0 4px">Looking for players…</p>
      <p class="mute">${m.waiting ?? 1} in the queue · ${m.online ?? '?'} online</p>
      <p class="mute" style="margin-top:6px">${m.botsIn > 0 ? `If nobody else joins, bots fill the empty seats in ${m.botsIn}s.` : 'Starting your match…'}</p></div>`;
    const el = document.getElementById('qmBody');
    if (el) { el.innerHTML = text; return; }
    this.openModal(`<h2>Quick match</h2><div id="qmBody">${text}</div>`);
    this.modal.querySelector('[data-act=closeModal]').textContent = 'Cancel';
  }

  historyModal(list, s) {
    const when = (t) => new Date(t).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' }) + ' ' + new Date(t).toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
    const kind = { practice: 'Practice', career: 'Career', online: 'Online' };
    const rows = list.slice(0, 40).map((e) => `<tr><td>${esc(when(e.at))}</td><td>${kind[e.kind] || ''} · ${e.mode === 'singles' ? '1v1' : '2v2'}</td><td>${e.mates?.length ? esc(e.mates.join(' & ')) + ' vs ' : 'vs '}${esc((e.opponents || []).join(' & '))}</td><td style="color:${e.won ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--bad)'};font-weight:800">${e.won ? 'W' : 'L'} ${e.sets.map((x) => x.join('–')).join(' ')}</td><td>${e.longestRally}</td></tr>`).join('');
    this.openModal(`<h2>Match history</h2>
      <div class="statrow"><div><span class="label">Matches</span><b>${s.matches}</b></div><div><span class="label">Wins</span><b>${s.wins}</b></div><div><span class="label">Win rate</span><b>${s.winRate}%</b></div><div><span class="label">Streak</span><b>${s.streak}</b></div><div><span class="label">Best rally</span><b>${s.longestRally}</b></div><div><span class="label">Aces</span><b>${s.aces}</b></div></div>
      ${list.length ? `<table class="table" style="margin-top:12px"><tr><th>When</th><th>Mode</th><th>Players</th><th>Result</th><th>Rally</th></tr>${rows}</table>${list.length > 40 ? '<p class="mute">Showing the latest 40.</p>' : ''}<div class="row" style="margin-top:10px"><button class="btn small ghost" data-act="historyClear">Clear history</button></div>` : '<p class="mute" style="margin-top:12px">No matches yet. Every finished match (practice, career or online) is saved here, in this browser.</p>'}`);
    this.modal.querySelector('.modal').classList.add('wide');
  }

  openModal(html) {
    this.closeModal();
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'screen';
    el.id = 'modal';
    el.style.zIndex = 20;
    el.innerHTML = `<div class="panel modal">${html}<div class="row" style="margin-top:18px"><span class="grow"></span><button class="btn" data-act="closeModal">Close</button></div></div>`;
    this.root.appendChild(el);
    this.modal = el;
    return el;
  }

  closeModal() { $('#modal')?.remove(); this.modal = null; this.onModalClose?.(); this.onModalClose = null; }

  /** Player + racket customisation with a live 3D preview. Returns the preview canvas. */
  equipmentModal(look, unlocked = null) {
    const arch = ARCHETYPES.map((a) => `<button class="opt" data-act="lookArch" data-val="${a.id}"><b>${esc(a.name)}</b><span class="mute">${esc(a.blurb)}</span></button>`).join('');
    const hair = HAIR_STYLES.map((h, i) => `<button data-act="lookHair" data-val="${i}">${esc(h)}</button>`).join('');
    const skin = SKIN_TONES.map((c, i) => `<button class="swatch-btn" data-act="lookSkin" data-val="${i}" style="background:#${c.toString(16).padStart(6, '0')}" aria-label="Skin tone ${i + 1}"></button>`).join('');
    const rk = RACKETS.map((r) => `<button class="opt racket" data-act="lookRacket" data-val="${r.id}" ${!unlocked || unlocked.includes(r.id) ? '' : 'disabled title="Unlock it in Career objectives"'}><i style="background:#${r.face.toString(16).padStart(6, '0')};border-color:#${r.accent.toString(16).padStart(6, '0')}"></i><span><b>${esc(r.name)}</b> <span class="mute">${esc(r.category)}</span><br><span class="mute">${esc(r.note)}</span></span></button>`).join('');
    const el = this.openModal(`<h2>Equipment</h2><div class="equip">
      <div class="equip-view"><canvas id="previewCanvas" width="320" height="380"></canvas>
        <div class="row" style="flex-wrap:wrap"><button class="btn small" data-act="previewMode" data-val="character">Player</button><button class="btn small" data-act="previewMode" data-val="racket">Inspect racket</button><button class="btn small ghost" data-act="previewTeam">Team colour</button></div></div>
      <div class="equip-opts">
        <div class="label">Player</div><div class="optgrid">${arch}</div>
        <div class="label">Hair</div><div class="seg wrap">${hair}</div>
        <div class="label">Skin tone</div><div class="row">${skin}</div>
        <div class="label">Racket <span class="mute">(cosmetic: no gameplay advantage)</span></div><div class="optgrid">${rk}</div>
      </div></div>`);
    el.querySelector('.modal').classList.add('wide');
    this.markEquipment(look);
    return el.querySelector('#previewCanvas');
  }

  markEquipment(look) {
    const want = { lookArch: look.arch, lookHair: look.hair, lookSkin: look.skin, lookRacket: look.racket };
    document.querySelectorAll('#modal [data-act^=look]').forEach((b) => b.classList.toggle('on', want[b.dataset.act] === +b.dataset.val));
  }

  controlsModal(s, st) {
    const el = $('#modal');
    if (el) { this.reopenScroll = el.querySelector('.modal').scrollTop; el.remove(); this.modal = null; } // keep the scroll position when the screen refreshes
    const m = this.openModal(controlsHtml(s, st));
    m.querySelector('.modal').classList.add('wide');
    if (this.reopenScroll) { m.querySelector('.modal').scrollTop = this.reopenScroll; this.reopenScroll = 0; }
    return m;
  }

  pauseMenu(local = false, mouseAimOn = false) {
    this.openModal(`<h2>Paused</h2><p class="mute" style="margin-bottom:14px">${local ? 'The match is stopped.' : 'Online, the match only stops if the other players accept a pause (30 s at most, two per player).'}</p>
      <div class="col"><button class="btn primary" data-act="closeModal">Resume</button>${local ? '' : '<button class="btn" data-act="pauseRequest">Request a pause (the others must accept)</button>'}<button class="btn" data-act="mouseAim" data-val="${mouseAimOn ? 0 : 1}" data-pause="1">Mouse aim: ${mouseAimOn ? 'ON (click to switch off)' : 'OFF (click to switch on)'}</button><button class="btn" data-act="settings">Settings</button><button class="btn" data-act="controls">Controls</button><button class="btn ghost" data-act="leave">Leave match</button></div>`);
  }

  controls() {
    this.openModal(`<h2>How to play</h2>
      <div class="kv">
        <span class="k">W A S D / Arrows</span><span>Move (both work). The camera follows you, so W always runs toward the net.</span>
        <span class="k">Aim</span><span>While you swing you are planted, and the direction you hold at the moment of contact is where the ball goes: left/right = that side, up = deep, down = short. With no direction it picks the open side. With mouse aim on (Settings, the pause menu or the M key) the target ring follows the cursor and left click hits (right click = globo).</span>
        <span class="k">Space</span><span><b>Hit</b>. To serve, the ball bounces in your hand (slowly): HOLD Space to charge power and release to hit (a tap is a normal serve, a full charge is +22% pace but less accurate); hit it as it rises to waist height (the lower and higher you hit it, the less accurate). R = slice serve, Q = kick serve; hold a direction to aim it. A circle closes in on the ball: press Space when it touches the small inner ring. Green = PERFECT, white = GOOD, red = LATE. Mouse left-click does the same.</span>
        <span class="k">R</span><span>Hits a slice (press it instead of Space): backspin that skids low and slow off the floor and glass.</span>
        <span class="k">Q</span><span>Hits a soft shot: chiquita near the net, dejada (drop shot) from deep, <b>víbora</b> (sidespin) on overheads.</span>
        <span class="k">E</span><span>Hits a controlled shot: block on low balls, <b>bandeja</b> (backspin, placed deep) on overheads.</span>
        <span class="k">G / Enter</span><span>Globo (lob), a high ball over opponents at the net. Mouse right-click does the same. Overheads with no key: remate (smash). Smash while holding down (short) with a clean contact: a kill that bounces out of the court, "por 4".</span>
        <span class="k">X</span><span>Jump for high balls.</span>
        <span class="k">Shift</span><span>Sprint (uses stamina).</span>
        <span class="k">Tab / Esc</span><span>Match stats / menu. Keys 1–4 send quick chat.</span>
      </div>
      <p class="mute" style="margin-top:14px;line-height:1.5">Rules: the ball must bounce once on the opponents' side before touching glass. After that you may play it off the walls. Two bounces, hitting the glass first, the net or out = point lost. Serve underhand into the diagonal box; two faults lose the point. Your partner and you cannot hit twice in a row.</p>`);
  }

  settingsModal(onChange) {
    const mouseAimOn = !!this.getMouseAim?.();
    const range = (key, min, max, step, label) => `<div class="setting"><span>${label}</span><input type="range" min="${min}" max="${max}" step="${step}" value="${settings[key]}" data-range="${key}" style="max-width:200px"></div>`;
    const tog = (key, label) => `<div class="setting"><span>${label}</span><div class="seg"><button data-act="toggle" data-key="${key}" data-val="1" class="${settings[key] ? 'on' : ''}">On</button><button data-act="toggle" data-key="${key}" data-val="0" class="${settings[key] ? '' : 'on'}">Off</button></div></div>`;
    const el = this.openModal(`<h2>Settings</h2>
      <div class="setting"><span>Graphics quality</span><div class="seg">${['low', 'medium', 'high'].map((q) => `<button data-act="quality" data-val="${q}" class="${settings.quality === q ? 'on' : ''}">${q}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
      ${range('master', 0, 1, 0.05, 'Master volume')}
      ${range('sfx', 0, 1, 0.05, 'Effects volume')}
      ${range('ambience', 0, 1, 0.05, 'Environment volume')}
      ${tog('announcer', 'Announcer (spoken score)')}
      ${range('voice', 0, 1, 0.05, 'Announcer volume')}
      ${range('music', 0, 1, 0.05, 'Music volume')}
<div class="setting"><span>Camera</span><div class="seg">${['player', 'first', 'wall', 'corner', 'side', 'broadcast', 'tactical', 'custom'].map((c) => `<button data-act="camera" data-val="${c}" class="${settings.camera === c ? 'on' : ''}">${c}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
      ${tog('ballTrack', 'Camera drifts toward the ball')}
      ${settings.camera === 'custom' ? range('camDist', 4, 16, 0.1, 'Custom: distance') + range('camHeight', 2.5, 16, 0.1, 'Custom: height') + range('camSmooth', 2, 12, 0.5, 'Custom: smoothing') + range('camFov', 36, 70, 1, 'Custom: field of view') : '<p class="mute" style="font-size:12px;margin:4px 0">Player: behind you · First: over your shoulder · Wall: low behind the glass, great for wall play · Corner: high from a back corner · Side: television side-line · Broadcast: whole court, high · Tactical: overhead · Custom: your own. Press C in a match to cycle.</p>'}
      ${range('ballScale', 1, 4, 0.1, 'Ball size (visibility)')}
      <div class="setting"><span>Aim with the mouse (click to hit)</span><div class="seg"><button data-act="mouseAim" data-val="1" class="${mouseAimOn ? 'on' : ''}">On</button><button data-act="mouseAim" data-val="0" class="${mouseAimOn ? '' : 'on'}">Off</button></div></div>
      ${tog('timing', 'Timing indicator')}
      ${tog('replays', 'Instant replays after points (solo matches)')}
      ${tog('trail', 'Ball trail')}
      ${tog('highContrast', 'High contrast UI')}
      <div class="setting"><span>Controls (keyboard, mouse, gamepad)</span><button class="btn small primary" data-act="controlsSettings">Configure…</button></div>
      <div class="setting"><span>Fullscreen</span><button class="btn small" data-act="fullscreen">Toggle</button></div>`);
    el.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
      const k = e.target.dataset.range;
      if (k) { setSetting(k, +e.target.value); onChange(k); }
    });
  }

  profileModal(profile, name) {
    if (!profile) { this.openModal('<h2>Profile</h2><p class="mute">Loading…</p>'); return; }
    const hist = profile.history.length
      ? `<table class="table"><tr><th>Result</th><th>Score</th><th>Length</th></tr>${profile.history.slice(0, 8).map((h) => `<tr><td style="color:${h.won ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--bad)'}">${h.won ? 'Win' : 'Loss'}</td><td>${esc(h.score)}</td><td>${Math.floor(h.duration / 60)} min</td></tr>`).join('')}</table>`
      : '<p class="mute">No matches yet. Play one!</p>';
    this.openModal(`<h2>${esc(name)}</h2>
      <div class="row" style="gap:28px;margin-bottom:14px"><div><div class="label">Level</div><div style="font-size:26px;font-weight:800">${profile.level}</div></div><div><div class="label">XP</div>${profile.xp}</div><div><div class="label">Record</div>${profile.wins}W – ${profile.losses}L</div></div>
      <div class="label" style="margin-bottom:6px">Recent matches</div>${hist}`);
  }
}

function statsTable(players, names, singles = false) {
  const rows = players.map((p, i) => singles && i % 2 ? '' : `<tr><td><span class="swatch" style="background:${TEAM_COLORS[i >> 1]};margin-right:8px"></span>${esc(names[i])}</td><td>${p.winners + p.aces}</td><td>${p.errors}</td><td>${p.aces}</td><td>${p.smashes}</td><td>${p.volleys}</td><td>${p.bandejas + p.viboras}</td><td>${p.hits}</td></tr>`).join('');
  return `<table class="table"><tr><th>Player</th><th>Winners</th><th>Errors</th><th>Aces</th><th>Smashes</th><th>Volleys</th><th>Bandeja/Víbora</th><th>Shots</th></tr>${rows}</table>`;
}

===== FILE: test/career.test.js =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { Store } from '../server/store.js';
import { Room, RoomManager } from '../server/rooms.js';
import { CAREER, starsFor } from '../shared/career.js';
import { BOT_LEVELS } from '../shared/ai.js';

const tmp = () => fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'padel-career-'));

test('career ladder: only stage 1 is open, beating a stage unlocks the next, stars only pay when improved', () => {
  const dir = tmp();
  const store = new Store(dir);
  const v0 = store.careerView('tok');
  assert.deepEqual(v0.stages.map((s) => s.unlocked), [true, ...CAREER.slice(1).map(() => false)]);
  const lost = store.careerComplete('tok', 1, 0);
  assert.equal(lost.unlocked, null);
  const win = store.careerComplete('tok', 1, 2);
  assert.equal(win.unlocked, 2);
  assert.equal(win.bonusXp, CAREER[0].xp * 2);
  const again = store.careerComplete('tok', 1, 2);
  assert.equal(again.bonusXp, 0, 'no bonus for repeating the same result');
  const better = store.careerComplete('tok', 1, 3);
  assert.equal(better.bonusXp, CAREER[0].xp);
  assert.equal(better.unlocked, null, 'already unlocked');
  const v1 = store.careerView('tok');
  assert.equal(v1.stages[1].unlocked, true);
  assert.equal(v1.stages[2].unlocked, false);
  assert.equal(v1.stars, 3);
  store.flush();
  assert.equal(new Store(dir).careerView('tok').stars, 3, 'progress persists');
  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('stars depend on how many games you concede', () => {
  const r = (sets, winner = 0) => ({ winner, score: { sets } });
  assert.equal(starsFor(r([[6, 0]])), 3);
  assert.equal(starsFor(r([[6, 3]])), 2);
  assert.equal(starsFor(r([[6, 5]])), 1);
  assert.equal(starsFor(r([[6, 0]], 1)), 0);
});

test('every career stage uses a valid bot level and enough names for its mode', () => {
  for (const s of CAREER) {
    assert.ok(BOT_LEVELS.includes(s.difficulty));
    assert.equal(s.opponents.length, s.mode === 'doubles' ? 2 : 1);
    if (s.mode === 'doubles') { assert.ok(s.partner); assert.ok(BOT_LEVELS.includes(s.partnerDiff)); }
  }
  assert.ok(CAREER.map((s) => BOT_LEVELS.indexOf(s.difficulty)).every((v, i, a) => i === 0 || v >= a[i - 1]), 'difficulty never goes down');
});

test('career room: human plus named bots at the stage level, a result earns stars and unlocks the next stage', () => {
  const dir = tmp();
  const store = new Store(dir);
  const mgr = new RoomManager(store);
  const { room, member } = mgr.create({ token: 'tok', name: 'Gon', ws: null });
  const stage = CAREER[1]; // doubles
  room.setupCareer(stage, member);
  assert.equal(room.state, 'match');
  assert.deepEqual(room.slots.map((s) => s.name || 'human'), ['human', 'Sofi', 'Marta', 'Luis']);
  assert.equal(room.slots[2].diff, stage.difficulty);
  assert.equal(room.settings.sets, stage.sets);
  // the human's team wins 3-0
  room.finishMatch({ results: { winner: 0, score: { sets: [[3, 0]] }, duration: 90, players: [], teamPoints: [12, 2], longestRally: 5, rallies: 14 } });
  const msg = JSON.parse(room.lastResultsMsg);
  assert.equal(msg.career.stars, 3);
  assert.equal(msg.career.unlocked, 3);
  assert.equal(room.toLobby(member), 'Career matches have no lobby');
  assert.equal(store.careerView('tok').stages[2].unlocked, true);
  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('public rooms are listed only while open and public', () => {
  const dir = tmp();
  const mgr = new RoomManager(new Store(dir));
  const a = mgr.create({ token: 'a', name: 'Ana', ws: null });
  const b = mgr.create({ token: 'b', name: 'Ben', ws: null });
  assert.equal(mgr.listPublic().length, 0, 'rooms are private by default');
  assert.equal(a.room.setSettings(a.member, { public: true }), null);
  const list = mgr.listPublic();
  assert.equal(list.length, 1);
  assert.deepEqual([list[0].code, list[0].host, list[0].players, list[0].max], [a.room.code, 'Ana', 1, 4]);
  a.room.beginMatch();
  assert.equal(mgr.listPublic().length, 0, 'started matches are not listed');
  void b;
  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('bot levels are really different: stronger levels beat weaker ones', async () => {
  const { PadelGame } = await import('../shared/game.js');
  const { botInput, makeBotMemory } = await import('../shared/ai.js');
  const play = (a, b, seed) => {
    let s = seed * 104729;
    const rng = () => { s = (s * 1664525 + 1013904223) % 4294967296; return s / 4294967296; };
    const g = new PadelGame({ rng, settings: { sets: 1, gamesPerSet: 3 } });
    const mem = [0, 1, 2, 3].map(makeBotMemory);
    for (let i = 0; i < 60 * 60 * 12; i++) {
      for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) g.applyInput(k, botInput(g, k, mem[k], k < 2 ? a : b));
      g.tick(1 / 60);
      for (const e of g.drainEvents()) if (e.e === 'match') return e.winner;
    }
    return -1;
  };
  for (const [hi, lo] of [['expert', 'easy'], ['hard', 'normal']]) {
    let wins = 0;
    for (let seed = 1; seed <= 4; seed++) { if (play(hi, lo, seed) === 0) wins++; if (play(lo, hi, seed + 50) === 1) wins++; }
    assert.ok(wins >= 7, hi + ' should beat ' + lo + ' (' + wins + '/8)');
  }
});

test('career helpers (used by the in-browser career) match the server rules', async () => {
  const { careerViewFrom, applyCareerResult } = await import('../shared/career.js');
  let done = {};
  assert.deepEqual(careerViewFrom(done).stages.map((s) => s.unlocked).slice(0, 3), [true, false, false]);
  let r = applyCareerResult(done, 1, 2);
  assert.equal(r.unlocked, 2); assert.equal(r.bonusXp, CAREER[0].xp * 2);
  done = r.done;
  r = applyCareerResult(done, 1, 2);
  assert.equal(r.bonusXp, 0); assert.equal(r.unlocked, null);
  assert.equal(applyCareerResult(done, 1, 3).bonusXp, CAREER[0].xp);
  assert.equal(careerViewFrom(done).stages[1].unlocked, true);
  assert.equal(done[1], 2, 'input map is not mutated');
});
===== END FILE: test/career.test.js =====

===== FILE: test/career2.test.js =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {
  EVENTS, EVENT_BY_ID, ATTRS, defaultSave, migrateCareer, loadCareer, saveCareer, calendar, rankOf, seasonOf,
  createTournament, nextPlayerMatch, reportPlayerMatch, standings, finishRewards, completeEvent, applyMatch,
  buyAttr, attrMods, DRILLS, drillStars, applyDrill, OBJECTIVES, claimObjectives, simulateMatch, commentary,
} from '../shared/career2.js';
import { ROSTER, ROSTER_BY_ID } from '../shared/roster.js';
import { PadelGame } from '../shared/game.js';
import { skillFor } from '../shared/ai.js';

const memStore = () => { const m = new Map(); return { getItem: (k) => m.get(k) ?? null, setItem: (k, v) => m.set(k, v), raw: m }; };
const alwaysWin = (a, b) => ({ winner: 0, gamesA: 3, gamesB: 1 });

test('events are well formed: unique ids, known roster ids, partners for doubles, increasing seasons', () => {
  assert.equal(new Set(EVENTS.map((e) => e.id)).size, EVENTS.length);
  for (const e of EVENTS) {
    for (const id of e.pool) assert.ok(ROSTER_BY_ID[id], `${e.id}: unknown ${id}`);
    if (e.mode === 'doubles') assert.ok(ROSTER_BY_ID[e.partner], `${e.id} needs a partner`);
    assert.ok([4, 8].includes(e.size));
    assert.ok(['indoor', 'outdoor', 'night', 'rooftop'].includes(e.court));
  }
  assert.ok(EVENTS.every((e, i) => i === 0 || e.season >= EVENTS[i - 1].season));
  assert.ok(ROSTER.length >= 12);
});

test('save migration: v1 progress survives, v2 is validated, garbage falls back', () => {
  const v1 = { done: { 1: 3, 2: 2, 3: 0, 5: 1 }, xp: 500 };
  const m = migrateCareer(v1);
  assert.equal(m.v, 2);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(m.done).sort(), ['club-doubles', 'club-open', 'regional-qualifier']);
  assert.equal(m.xp, 500);
  assert.ok(m.points > 0 && m.stats.titles === 3);
  assert.deepEqual(migrateCareer(null), defaultSave());
  assert.deepEqual(migrateCareer('nonsense'), defaultSave());
  const hostile = migrateCareer({ v: 2, coins: -5, points: 'x', attrs: { smash: 99, volley: -2 }, done: { 'club-open': { result: 'champion', points: 30 }, nope: { result: 'champion' } }, drills: { serve: 7, lob: 2 }, objectives: { 'first-win': true, bogus: true }, unlockedRackets: [6, 99, 'x'] });
  assert.equal(hostile.coins, 0); assert.equal(hostile.attrs.smash, 10); assert.equal(hostile.attrs.volley, 0);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(hostile.done), ['club-open']);
  assert.deepEqual(hostile.drills, { lob: 2 });
  assert.deepEqual(hostile.objectives, { 'first-win': true });
  assert.ok(hostile.unlockedRackets.includes(6) && !hostile.unlockedRackets.includes(99));
});

test('persistence round trip, and the old v1 key is read but never deleted', () => {
  const st = memStore();
  st.setItem('padel.career', JSON.stringify({ done: { 1: 2 }, xp: 120 }));
  const s = loadCareer(st);
  assert.ok(s.done['club-open']);
  s.coins = 77; saveCareer(st, s);
  assert.equal(loadCareer(st).coins, 77);
  assert.ok(st.raw.has('padel.career'), 'backup of the old save kept');
  st.setItem('padel.career2', '{broken');
  assert.deepEqual(loadCareer(st), defaultSave());
});

test('calendar: season gating, points requirements and sequential unlocks', () => {
  const s = defaultSave();
  let cal = calendar(s);
  assert.equal(cal[0].locked, null);
  assert.match(cal[1].locked, /Play Club Open first/);
  assert.match(cal.find((c) => c.event.id === 'regional-qualifier').locked, /season 1/);
  for (const e of EVENTS.filter((x) => x.season === 1)) s.done[e.id] = { result: 'champion', points: 10 };
  assert.equal(seasonOf(s), 2);
  s.points = 50;
  cal = calendar(s);
  assert.match(cal.find((c) => c.event.id === 'regional-qualifier').locked, /100 ranking points/);
  s.points = 120;
  assert.equal(calendar(s).find((c) => c.event.id === 'regional-qualifier').locked, null);
  assert.ok(rankOf(0) > rankOf(500) && rankOf(10000) === 1);
});

test('knockout bracket: the player is in the draw, rounds advance, a loss ends the run at the right stage', () => {
  const ev = EVENT_BY_ID['league-final']; // 8 draw doubles
  const t = createTournament(ev, 42);
  assert.equal(t.teams.length, 8);
  assert.deepEqual(t.teams[0].ids, ['you', 'ana']);
  assert.equal(new Set(t.teams.flatMap((x) => x.ids)).size, 16, 'everybody is a different person');
  assert.equal(t.rounds[0].name, 'Quarter-final');
  let n = nextPlayerMatch(t);
  assert.ok(n && n.opponents.length === 2);
  reportPlayerMatch(t, true, 4, 1, alwaysWin);
  assert.equal(t.rounds.length, 2); assert.equal(t.rounds[1].name, 'Semi-final');
  assert.ok(t.rounds[0].matches.every((m) => m.winner !== undefined), 'the other quarter-finals were played');
  n = nextPlayerMatch(t); assert.equal(n.name, 'Semi-final');
  reportPlayerMatch(t, true, 4, 2, alwaysWin);
  n = nextPlayerMatch(t); assert.equal(n.name, 'Final');
  reportPlayerMatch(t, true, 4, 0, alwaysWin);
  assert.equal(t.status, 'done'); assert.equal(t.result, 'champion'); assert.equal(nextPlayerMatch(t), null);

  const t2 = createTournament(ev, 42);
  reportPlayerMatch(t2, true, 4, 1, alwaysWin);
  reportPlayerMatch(t2, false, 1, 4, alwaysWin);
  assert.equal(t2.status, 'done'); assert.equal(t2.result, 'semi');
  const t3 = createTournament(EVENT_BY_ID['club-open'], 7); // 4 draw: semi, final
  reportPlayerMatch(t3, true, 3, 0, alwaysWin);
  reportPlayerMatch(t3, false, 1, 3, alwaysWin);
  assert.equal(t3.result, 'finalist');
});

test('group stage: three matches, table, final for the top two', () => {
  const t = createTournament(EVENT_BY_ID['sunday-league'], 9);
  assert.equal(t.rounds.length, 3);
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { assert.equal(nextPlayerMatch(t).name, `Group match ${i + 1}`); reportPlayerMatch(t, true, 4, 1, alwaysWin); }
  assert.equal(standings(t)[0], 0, 'three wins tops the table');
  assert.equal(t.status, 'playing');
  assert.equal(nextPlayerMatch(t).name, 'Final');
  reportPlayerMatch(t, true, 4, 2, alwaysWin);
  assert.equal(t.result, 'champion');

  const u = createTournament(EVENT_BY_ID['sunday-league'], 9);
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) reportPlayerMatch(u, false, 1, 4, (a, b) => ({ winner: 0, gamesA: 4, gamesB: 2 }));
  assert.equal(u.status, 'done');
  assert.ok(['third', 'fourth'].includes(u.result));
});

test('rewards, XP, objectives (granted once), unlocks and development', () => {
  const s = defaultSave();
  const ev = EVENT_BY_ID['club-open'];
  assert.ok(finishRewards(ev, 'champion').points > finishRewards(ev, 'semi').points);
  applyMatch(s, ev, 'Semi-final', true, { gamesFor: 3, gamesAgainst: 0, opp: 'Marta', smashes: 3 });
  assert.equal(s.stats.shutouts, 1);
  let got = claimObjectives(s);
  assert.deepEqual(got.map((o) => o.id).sort(), ['first-win', 'shutout']);
  assert.equal(claimObjectives(s).length, 0, 'never granted twice');
  const coinsBefore = s.coins;
  completeEvent(s, ev, 'champion');
  got = claimObjectives(s);
  assert.ok(got.some((o) => o.id === 'first-title'));
  assert.ok(s.unlockedRackets.includes(6), 'the title unlocked a racket');
  assert.ok(s.coins > coinsBefore && s.points > 0 && s.done['club-open'].result === 'champion' && s.active === null);

  s.devPoints = 3;
  assert.deepEqual(buyAttr(s, 'smash'), { ok: true });      // costs 1
  assert.deepEqual(buyAttr(s, 'smash'), { ok: true });      // costs 2
  assert.equal(buyAttr(s, 'smash').ok, false);              // would cost 3, has 0
  assert.equal(s.attrs.smash, 2);
  assert.equal(buyAttr(s, 'nope').ok, false);
  s.attrs.lob = 10; s.devPoints = 99; assert.equal(buyAttr(s, 'lob').ok, false, 'capped at 10');
});

test('attributes are bounded (about +15% at most), and only slot 0 of a local match benefits', () => {
  const maxed = Object.fromEntries(ATTRS.map((a) => [a, 10]));
  const m = attrMods(maxed);
  assert.ok(m.runMul <= 1.15 + 1e-9 && m.power <= 1.15 + 1e-9 && m.drainMul >= 0.6 - 1e-9 && m.q.ground <= 0.1 + 1e-9 && m.window <= 1.1 + 1e-9);
  assert.deepEqual(attrMods(defaultSave().attrs).q, { ground: 0, volley: 0, smash: 0, lob: 0, defense: 0 });
  const dist = (settings) => { const g = new PadelGame({ settings }); g.drainEvents(); g.applyInput(0, { mx: 1 }); g.applyInput(2, { mx: 1 }); for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) g.tick(1 / 60); return [g.players[0], g.players[2]].map((p) => Math.abs(p.x - (p === g.players[0] ? 0 : 0))); };
  const base = new PadelGame({ settings: {} }); const dev = new PadelGame({ settings: { attrMods: m } });
  assert.equal(base.players[0].speedMul, undefined);
  assert.ok(dev.players[0].speedMul > 1 && dev.players[2].speedMul === undefined);
  void dist;
});

test('drills: stars thresholds, only improvements pay', () => {

  const ev = EVENT_BY_ID['club-open'];
  const a = simulateMatch(['marta'], ['luis'], ev, 5), b = simulateMatch(['marta'], ['luis'], ev, 5);
  assert.deepEqual(a, b);
  assert.ok(a.winner === 0 || a.winner === 1);
  assert.ok(a.gamesA + a.gamesB >= 3);
  const d = simulateMatch(['kai', 'lucia'], ['pablo', 'sofi'], { ...EVENT_BY_ID['club-doubles'] }, 3);
  assert.ok(d.winner === 0 || d.winner === 1);
});

test('styles change how bots play, levels still order them', () => {
  const net = skillFor({ level: 'normal', style: 'net' }), counter = skillFor({ level: 'normal', style: 'counter' }), plain = skillFor('normal');
  assert.ok(net.netZ < (plain.netZ ?? 2.9));
  assert.ok(net.netZ < counter.netZ && net.backZ < counter.backZ, 'net players stand closer to the net than counter-punchers');
  assert.ok(skillFor({ level: 'normal', style: 'power' }).smash > plain.smash);
  assert.ok(skillFor({ level: 'normal', style: 'wild' }).flub > plain.flub);
  assert.ok(skillFor({ level: 'easy', style: 'retriever' }).speed > skillFor('easy').speed);
  assert.ok(skillFor({ level: 'expert', style: 'wild' }).aggr <= 1);
});

test('commentary is concise and matches the result', () => {
  assert.match(commentary(true, 4, 0), /perfect/i);
  assert.match(commentary(false, 1, 5, 'Keep it low.'), /scouting|Outplayed/);
  assert.match(commentary(false, 3, 4, 'Keep it low.'), /Keep it low/);

test('fatigue: long rallies drain stamina even standing still, and a tired player is slower and hits worse', async () => {
  const { stepPlayer } = await import('../shared/physics.js');
  const still = { x: 0, z: -6, vx: 0, vz: 0, stamina: 100, team: 0, inRally: true };
  for (let i = 0; i < 60 * 40; i++) stepPlayer(still, { mx: 0, mz: 0 }, 1 / 60);
  assert.ok(still.stamina < 45, `40 s rally leaves ${still.stamina.toFixed(0)}`);
  const rest = { ...still, inRally: false };
  for (let i = 0; i < 60 * 3; i++) stepPlayer(rest, { mx: 0, mz: 0 }, 1 / 60);
  assert.ok(rest.stamina > still.stamina + 25, 'recovers quickly between points');
  const fresh = { x: 0, z: -6, vx: 0, vz: 0, stamina: 100, team: 0, inRally: true }, spent = { ...fresh, stamina: 5 };
  for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) { stepPlayer(fresh, { mx: 1, mz: 0 }, 1 / 60); stepPlayer(spent, { mx: 1, mz: 0 }, 1 / 60); }
  assert.ok(spent.x < fresh.x * 0.9, 'exhausted players are slower');
  const { PadelGame } = await import('../shared/game.js');
  const hitQ = (stamina) => {
    const g = new PadelGame({ rng: () => 0.5 });
    g.phase = 'rally';
    g.rally = { serving: false, faults: 0, lastTeam: 1, lastSlot: 2, bounces: 1, hits: 3, netTouched: false, serveXSign: 1 };
    Object.assign(g.ball, { x: 1, y: 0.9, z: -6, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0 });
    Object.assign(g.players[0], { x: 0.2, z: -6.6, stamina });
    g.startSwing(g.players[0], { ax: 0, az: 8, mod: 0, lob: false });
    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) { g.players[0].stamina = stamina; g.tick(1 / 60); const h = g.drainEvents().find((e) => e.e === 'hit'); if (h) return h.q; }
    return null;
  };
  const fresh100 = hitQ(100), exhausted = hitQ(0);
  assert.ok(fresh100 !== null && exhausted !== null && exhausted < fresh100 - 0.1, `exhausted contact is worse (${exhausted} vs ${fresh100})`);
});

test('power and stamina upgrades change the numbers', () => {
  const a = attrMods({ ...defaultSave().attrs, power: 10, stamina: 10, movement: 10 });
  assert.ok(a.power > 1.1 && a.runMul > 1.1 && a.drainMul < 0.7 && a.regenMul > 1.3);
});

===== FILE: test/contact.test.js =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { PadelGame } from '../shared/game.js';
import { makeBall, simulateShot } from '../shared/physics.js';

const DT = 1 / 60;
const seeded = (seed) => () => { seed = (seed * 1664525 + 1013904223) % 4294967296; return seed / 4294967296; };
const ball = () => ({ ...makeBall(), x: -1, y: 1, z: -4 });
const speedOf = (v) => Math.hypot(v.vx, v.vz);

test('racket face angle: an open face launches higher with more backspin, a closed face is flatter with topspin', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng: () => 0.5 });
  const b = { ...ball(), y: 1.8, z: -6 }; // a deep target, so the net does not set the arc
  const open = g.planShot('forehand', b, 1, 2, 9, 1, 10, null, { face: 0.5, e: 0, lateral: 0, forward: 0 });
  const closed = g.planShot('forehand', b, 1, 2, 9, 1, 10, null, { face: -0.5, e: 0, lateral: 0, forward: 0 });
  assert.ok(open.vy > closed.vy + 0.3, `launch ${open.vy} vs ${closed.vy}`);
  assert.ok(open.sb > closed.sb, 'spin follows the face angle');
  // the solver still lands both where they were aimed (the angle changes the arc, not the target)
  for (const v of [open, closed]) {
    const sim = simulateShot(b, v, { sb: v.sb, ss: v.ss });
    assert.equal(sim.kind, 'floor');
    assert.ok(Math.hypot(sim.x - 2, sim.z - 9) < 0.6, `lands near the target (${sim.x.toFixed(2)}, ${sim.z.toFixed(2)})`);
  }
});

test('off-centre contact loses pace and is less reliable than a centred hit', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng: () => 0.5 });
  const b = { ...ball(), y: 1.8, z: -6 }; // a deep target, so the net does not set the arc
  const centred = g.planShot('forehand', b, 1, 2, 9, 0.9, 10, null, { face: 0, e: 0, lateral: 0, forward: 0 });
  const edge = g.planShot('forehand', b, 1, 2, 9, 0.9, 10, null, { face: 0, e: 0.8, lateral: 0, forward: 0 });
  assert.ok(speedOf(edge) < speedOf(centred) * 0.9, 'edge hit is slower');

  const spread = (ctx) => {
    const h = new PadelGame({ rng: seeded(7) });
    const xs = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 300; i++) { const v = h.planShot('forehand', b, 1, 0, 7, 0.8, 10, null, ctx); const s = simulateShot(b, v, { sb: v.sb, ss: v.ss }); xs.push(s.x); }
    const mean = xs.reduce((a, c) => a + c, 0) / xs.length;
    return Math.sqrt(xs.reduce((a, c) => a + (c - mean) ** 2, 0) / xs.length);
  };
  const c = spread({ face: 0, e: 0, lateral: 0, forward: 0 });
  const eg = spread({ face: 0, e: 0.8, lateral: 0, forward: 0 });
  const run = spread({ face: 0, e: 0, lateral: 6, forward: 0 });
  assert.ok(eg > c * 1.4, `edge scatter ${eg.toFixed(2)} vs ${c.toFixed(2)}`);
  assert.ok(run > c * 1.1, 'running sideways costs accuracy');
});

test('swing timing sets the face; label and numbers are reported honestly', () => {
  const hitAt = (swingT, dist) => {
    const g = new PadelGame({ rng: () => 0.5 });
    g.drainEvents();
    g.phase = 'rally';
    g.rally = { serving: false, faults: 0, lastTeam: 0, lastSlot: 0, bounces: 1, hits: 3, netTouched: false, serveXSign: 1 };
    const p = g.players[2];
    Object.assign(p, { x: 0, z: 5 });
    Object.assign(g.ball, { x: 0, y: 1.0, z: 5 - dist, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: -2, sb: 0, ss: 0 });
    p.swing = { t: swingT, done: false, ax: 0, az: -6, mod: 0, lob: false };
    p.input = { ...p.input, ax: 0, az: -6 };
    assert.ok(g.contact(p));
    return g.drainEvents().find((e) => e.e === 'hit');
  };
  const late = hitAt(0.06, 0.95), ontime = hitAt(0.17, 0.95), early = hitAt(0.3, 0.95); // swing time at contact
  assert.ok(early.face < 0 && late.face > 0 && Math.abs(ontime.face) < 0.05, 'met early in the swing = open face, late = closed');
  const edge = hitAt(0.17, 1.7);
  assert.ok(edge.off > 0.6);
  assert.equal(edge.label, 'off-centre');
  assert.ok(['perfect', 'clean'].includes(ontime.label));
  assert.equal(hitAt(0.17, 0.95).label, ontime.label, 'deterministic');
});

test('the contact model keeps the game consistent: bots still complete a match', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ settings: { sets: 1, gamesPerSet: 2 } });
  let done = null;
  // deterministic movement-free smoke check: serving and point flow still work after the change
  for (let i = 0; i < 60 * 25 && !done; i++) {
    g.tick(DT);
    done = g.drainEvents().find((e) => e.e === 'point');
  }
  assert.ok(done, 'an unreturned serve ends the point');
});

test('the racket waits for the sweet spot: a fast ball is struck near the body, not at the edge of the reach', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng: () => 0.5 });
  g.drainEvents();
  g.phase = 'rally';
  g.rally = { serving: false, faults: 0, lastTeam: 0, lastSlot: 0, bounces: 1, hits: 3, netTouched: false, serveXSign: 1 };
  const p = g.players[2];
  Object.assign(p, { x: 0, z: 5 });
  Object.assign(g.ball, { x: 0, y: 1.0, z: 1.5, vx: 0, vy: 0.3, vz: 15, sb: 0, ss: 0 }); // fast ball travelling toward the player
  g.applyInput(2, { swing: true, ax: 0, az: -6 });
  let hit = null;
  for (let i = 0; i < 40 && !hit; i++) { g.tick(DT); hit = g.drainEvents().find((e) => e.e === 'hit'); }
  assert.ok(hit, 'it connects');
  assert.ok(hit.off < 0.5, 'struck near the sweet spot: off=' + hit.off);
});
===== END FILE: test/contact.test.js =====

===== FILE: test/drills.test.js =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { PadelGame } from '../shared/game.js';
import { botInput, makeBotMemory } from '../shared/ai.js';
import { DrillController } from '../client/js/drills.js';
import { DRILLS } from '../shared/career2.js';

// Run a drill headlessly with a bot standing in for the human (slot 0), through the real game engine.
function runDrill(id, level = 'hard', seed = 11) {
  let s = seed;
  const rng = () => { s = (s * 1664525 + 1013904223) % 4294967296; return s / 4294967296; };
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng, settings: { sets: 1, gamesPerSet: 6 } });
  const ctl = new DrillController(id);
  const messages = [];
  let result = null;
  const lm = { emit: (m) => messages.push(m), finishDrill: (r) => { result = r; } };
  const mem = makeBotMemory();
  for (let i = 0; i < 60 * 60 * 6 && !result; i++) {
    ctl.update(g, lm);
    g.applyInput(0, botInput(g, 0, mem, level, 1 / 60));
    g.tick(1 / 60);
    ctl.events(g.drainEvents(), g, lm);
  }
  return { result, messages, ctl };
}

test('every drill finishes after exactly its number of attempts, using the real engine', () => {
  for (const d of DRILLS) {
    const { result, ctl } = runDrill(d.id);
    assert.ok(result, `${d.id} finished`);
    assert.equal(result.id, d.id);
    assert.ok(result.attempts >= d.n, `${d.id}: attempts ${result.attempts}`);
    assert.ok(result.score >= 0 && result.score <= (d.id === 'serve' ? d.n * 2 : d.n), `${d.id}: score ${result.score}`);
    assert.equal(ctl.done, true);
  }
});

test('the serve drill rewards serves that land in the box and deep; faults only count as attempts', () => {
  const { result } = runDrill('serve', 'expert', 5);
  assert.ok(result.score > 0, 'a competent server lands serves');
  assert.ok(result.attempts >= 10);
});

test('status messages carry the target zone for the court overlay', () => {
  const { messages } = runDrill('forehand');
  const st = messages.filter((m) => m.t === 'drillStatus');
  assert.ok(st.length > 3);
  assert.ok(st[0].zone && st[0].zone.x1 > st[0].zone.x0);
  assert.match(st.at(-1).text, /10\/10/);
});

test('shot kind matters: only a globo counts in the lob drill, and an off-target ball scores nothing', () => {
  const ctl = new DrillController('lob');
  const lm = { emit: () => {}, finishDrill: () => {} };
  const g = new PadelGame({});
  g.drainEvents();
  const feed = (kind, x, z) => {
    ctl.events([{ e: 'hit', slot: 0, kind }, { e: 'ball', k: 'floor', x, y: 0, z }], g, lm);
    ctl.events([{ e: 'point' }], g, lm);
  };
  feed('forehand', 0, 8.5);   // deep but not a lob
  assert.equal(ctl.score, 0);
  feed('lob', 0, 8.5);        // a lob into the zone
  assert.equal(ctl.score, 1);
  feed('lob', 0, 3);          // a lob that lands short
  assert.equal(ctl.score, 1);
  assert.equal(ctl.attempts, 3);
});
===== END FILE: test/drills.test.js =====

===== FILE: test/game.test.js =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { PadelGame } from '../shared/game.js';
import { stepBall, makeBall } from '../shared/physics.js';
import { botInput, makeBotMemory } from '../shared/ai.js';

const DT = 1 / 60;
const fixedRng = () => 0.5; // zero aiming error

function rallyGame(over = {}) {
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng: fixedRng, settings: { sets: 3 } });
  g.drainEvents();
  g.phase = 'rally';
  g.rally = { serving: false, faults: 0, lastTeam: 0, lastSlot: 0, bounces: 0, hits: 3, netTouched: false, serveXSign: 1, ...over.rally };
  Object.assign(g.ball, { x: 0, y: 1, z: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0 }, over.ball);
  return g;
}
function runUntilPoint(g, seconds = 8) {
  const out = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < seconds * 60; i++) {
    g.tick(DT);
    const evs = g.drainEvents();
    out.push(...evs);
    if (evs.some((e) => e.e === 'point')) break;
  }
  return out;
}
const pointOf = (evs) => evs.find((e) => e.e === 'point');

test('ball bounces on the floor with energy loss and never goes through it', () => {
  const b = makeBall(); b.y = 2;
  const ev = [];
  let maxAfterBounce = 0, bounced = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < 300; i++) {
    stepBall(b, DT, ev);
    assert.ok(b.y >= 0);
    if (ev.some((e) => e.type === 'floor')) bounced = true;
    if (bounced) maxAfterBounce = Math.max(maxAfterBounce, b.y);
  }
  assert.ok(bounced);
  assert.ok(maxAfterBounce < 2 && maxAfterBounce > 0.5, `rebound ${maxAfterBounce}`);
});

test('glass rebounds reflect velocity with loss', () => {
  const b = makeBall(); Object.assign(b, { x: 4.9, y: 1.5, z: 0, vx: 10, vy: 0, vz: 0 });
  const ev = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) stepBall(b, DT, ev);
  assert.ok(ev.some((e) => e.type === 'wall' && e.axis === 'x'));
  assert.ok(b.vx < 0 && b.vx > -10);
});

test('ball landing on the hitter own side loses the point', () => {
  const g = rallyGame({ ball: { x: 1, y: 1, z: -4 }, rally: { lastTeam: 0 } });
  const p = pointOf(runUntilPoint(g));
  assert.equal(p.winner, 1);
  assert.equal(p.reason, 'own-side');
});

test('second bounce on the receiving side wins the point for the hitter', () => {
  const g = rallyGame({ ball: { x: 1, y: 1, z: 4 }, rally: { lastTeam: 0 } });
  const p = pointOf(runUntilPoint(g));
  assert.equal(p.winner, 0);
  assert.equal(p.reason, 'bounce');
});

test('hitting the glass before bouncing is out', () => {
  const g = rallyGame({ ball: { x: 0, y: 2.5, z: 9, vz: 12 }, rally: { lastTeam: 0 } });
  const p = pointOf(runUntilPoint(g));
  assert.equal(p.winner, 1);
  assert.equal(p.reason, 'out');
});

test('a wall rebound AFTER the first bounce keeps the rally alive', () => {
  const g = rallyGame({ ball: { x: 0, y: 1.2, z: 5.5, vz: 9, vy: -3 }, rally: { lastTeam: 0 } });
  const evs = runUntilPoint(g);
  const wallAt = evs.findIndex((e) => e.e === 'ball' && e.k === 'wall');
  const p = pointOf(evs);
  assert.ok(wallAt >= 0, 'ball reached the glass');
  assert.ok(evs.indexOf(p) > wallAt, 'point only after the wall rebound');
  // legal: the glass is not out. It ends on the second floor bounce, won by the hitter.
  assert.equal(p.reason, 'bounce');
  assert.equal(p.winner, 0);
});

test('ball over the fence is out (before bounce: hitter loses; after: hitter wins)', () => {
  let g = rallyGame({ ball: { x: 4.8, y: 3.9, z: 3, vx: 6, vy: 4 }, rally: { lastTeam: 0, bounces: 0 } });
  let p = pointOf(runUntilPoint(g));
  assert.equal(p.winner, 1);
  g = rallyGame({ ball: { x: 4.8, y: 3.9, z: 3, vx: 6, vy: 4 }, rally: { lastTeam: 0, bounces: 1 } });
  p = pointOf(runUntilPoint(g));
  assert.equal(p.winner, 0);
});

test('ball hitting the net and dropping back is a net error', () => {
  const g = rallyGame({ ball: { x: 0, y: 0.6, z: -1.5, vz: 8 }, rally: { lastTeam: 0 } });
  const evs = runUntilPoint(g);
  assert.ok(evs.some((e) => e.e === 'ball' && e.k === 'net'));
  const p = pointOf(evs);
  assert.equal(p.winner, 1);
  assert.equal(p.reason, 'net');
});

test('two serve faults are a double fault', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng: fixedRng });
  g.drainEvents();
  const server = g.serverSlot;
  const team = server >> 1;
  const os = team === 0 ? 1 : -1;
  const drop = () => { // ball lands on the wrong half of the court
    g.phase = 'rally';
    Object.assign(g.ball, { x: g.rally.serveXSign * 2, y: 1, z: os * 4, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0 });
  };
  drop();
  let evs = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) { g.tick(DT); evs.push(...g.drainEvents()); }
  assert.ok(evs.some((e) => e.e === 'fault' && e.n === 1), 'first fault');
  assert.equal(g.phase, 'serve');
  drop();
  evs = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) { g.tick(DT); evs.push(...g.drainEvents()); }
  const p = pointOf(evs);
  assert.equal(p.reason, 'double-fault');
  assert.equal(p.winner, 1 - team);
});

test('a real serve lands in the diagonal box and the receivers must play it', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng: fixedRng });
  g.drainEvents();
  const s = g.serverSlot;
  for (let i = 0; i < 80; i++) g.tick(DT); // serve wait elapsed
  const os = (s >> 1) === 0 ? 1 : -1;
  g.applyInput(s, { swing: true, ax: -g.rally.serveXSign * 2.5, az: os * 5 });
  const evs = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) { g.tick(DT); evs.push(...g.drainEvents()); }
  assert.ok(evs.some((e) => e.e === 'hit' && e.kind === 'serve'));
  assert.ok(!evs.some((e) => e.e === 'fault'));
  assert.equal(g.rally.serving, false);
  assert.equal(g.rally.bounces, 1);
});

test('unreturned serve is an ace for the server', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng: fixedRng });
  g.drainEvents();
  const s = g.serverSlot;
  for (let i = 0; i < 80; i++) g.tick(DT);
  const os = (s >> 1) === 0 ? 1 : -1;
  g.applyInput(s, { swing: true, ax: -g.rally.serveXSign * 2.5, az: os * 5 });
  const p = pointOf(runUntilPoint(g, 10));
  assert.equal(p.winner, s >> 1);
  assert.equal(g.stats.players[s].aces, 1);
});

test('a player hits an incoming ball, and the same team cannot hit twice in a row', () => {
  const g = rallyGame({ ball: { x: 0.3, y: 1.1, z: 5.5, vz: -3, vy: 0 }, rally: { lastTeam: 0 } });
  Object.assign(g.players[2], { x: 0, z: 5 });
  g.applyInput(2, { swing: true, ax: 1, az: -6 });
  const evs = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) { g.tick(DT); evs.push(...g.drainEvents()); }
  const hit = evs.find((e) => e.e === 'hit');
  assert.ok(hit, 'hit registered');
  assert.equal(hit.slot, 2);
  assert.equal(g.rally.lastTeam, 1);
  // the partner may not return their own team's shot
  Object.assign(g.players[3], { x: 0, z: 3 });
  Object.assign(g.ball, { x: 0, y: 1, z: 3.5, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0 });
  g.applyInput(3, { swing: true, ax: 0, az: -6 });
  const evs2 = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) { g.tick(DT); evs2.push(...g.drainEvents()); }
  assert.ok(!evs2.some((e) => e.e === 'hit'));
});

test('players cannot cross the net', () => {
  const g = rallyGame();
  g.applyInput(0, { mz: 1 });
  for (let i = 0; i < 240; i++) g.tick(DT);
  assert.ok(g.players[0].z < 0);
});

test('simulation is deterministic for identical inputs and rng', () => {
  const run = () => {
    let s = 12345;
    const rng = () => { s = (s * 1664525 + 1013904223) % 4294967296; return s / 4294967296; };
    const g = new PadelGame({ rng });
    const mem = [0, 1, 2, 3].map(makeBotMemory);
    for (let i = 0; i < 60 * 25; i++) {
      for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) g.applyInput(k, botInput(g, k, mem[k], 'normal'));
      g.tick(DT);
    }
    return JSON.stringify([g.snapshot(), g.match.totalPoints]);
  };
  assert.equal(run(), run());
});

test('bots play a full short match to completion with valid scoring', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ settings: { sets: 1, gamesPerSet: 2 } });
  const mem = [0, 1, 2, 3].map(makeBotMemory);
  let ended = null;
  for (let i = 0; i < 60 * 60 * 25 && !ended; i++) {
    for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) g.applyInput(k, botInput(g, k, mem[k], 'hard'));
    g.tick(DT);
    ended = g.drainEvents().find((e) => e.e === 'match');
  }
  assert.ok(ended, 'match finished');
  assert.equal(g.phase, 'ended');
  const r = ended.results;
  assert.ok(r.teamPoints[0] + r.teamPoints[1] >= 5);
  assert.equal(r.winner, g.match.winner);
});

test('before the serve is hit: receivers stand still and the server stays behind the service line', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng: fixedRng });
  g.drainEvents();
  const s = g.serverSlot;
  const os = (s >> 1) === 0 ? 1 : -1;
  for (const i of [0, 1, 2, 3]) g.applyInput(i, { mx: 1, mz: (i >> 1) === (s >> 1) ? os : -os }); // everyone charges the net
  const others = [0, 1, 2, 3].filter((i) => i !== s);
  const before = others.map((i) => [g.players[i].x, g.players[i].z]);
  for (let k = 0; k < 240; k++) g.tick(DT);
  assert.equal(g.phase, 'serve');
  others.forEach((i, j) => assert.deepEqual([g.players[i].x, g.players[i].z], before[j]));
  assert.ok(Math.abs(g.players[s].z) >= 6.95, 'server never crosses the service line');
});

test('steering decides the shot at contact: hold left/right/forward/back while hitting', () => {
  const land = (steerInput) => {
    const g = new PadelGame({ rng: fixedRng });
    g.drainEvents();
    g.phase = 'rally';
    g.rally = { serving: false, faults: 0, lastTeam: 0, lastSlot: 0, bounces: 1, hits: 3, netTouched: false, serveXSign: 1 };
    Object.assign(g.ball, { x: 0, y: 1.0, z: 5.5, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: -2, sb: 0, ss: 0 });
    Object.assign(g.players[2], { x: 0, z: 5 });
    g.applyInput(2, { swing: true, steer: true, ax: 0, az: -6, ...steerInput });
    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) g.tick(DT);
    return { vx: g.ball.vx, vz: g.ball.vz };
  };
  assert.ok(land({ mx: 1 }).vx > 1, 'holding +x sends it to +x');
  assert.ok(land({ mx: -1 }).vx < -1, 'holding -x sends it to -x');
  const deep = land({ mz: -1 }), short = land({ mz: 1 });
  assert.ok(Math.abs(deep.vz) > Math.abs(short.vz), 'forward = deeper (faster away) than back = short');
});

test('the player is planted while swinging', () => {
  const g = rallyGame({ ball: { x: 0, y: 3, z: -9, vz: 0 }, rally: { lastTeam: 1 } });
  Object.assign(g.players[0], { x: 0, z: -3 });
  g.applyInput(0, { mx: 1, mz: 0, swing: true });
  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) g.tick(DT);
  const planted = g.players[0].x;
  const g2 = rallyGame({ ball: { x: 0, y: 3, z: -9, vz: 0 }, rally: { lastTeam: 1 } });
  Object.assign(g2.players[0], { x: 0, z: -3 });
  g2.applyInput(0, { mx: 1, mz: 0 });
  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) g2.tick(DT);
  assert.ok(planted < g2.players[0].x * 0.6, `planted ${planted} vs free ${g2.players[0].x}`);
});

test('serve: the ball is bounced by hand, and R/Q choose a slice or kick serve', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng: fixedRng });
  g.drainEvents();
  const ys = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) { g.tick(DT); ys.push(g.ball.y); }
  assert.ok(Math.max(...ys) > 0.8 && Math.min(...ys) < 0.3, 'ball bounces between hand and floor');
  const serve = (mod) => {
    const h = new PadelGame({ rng: fixedRng });
    h.drainEvents();
    for (let i = 0; i < 80; i++) h.tick(DT);
    const s = h.serverSlot, os = (s >> 1) === 0 ? 1 : -1;
    h.applyInput(s, { swing: true, mod, ax: -h.rally.serveXSign * 2.5, az: os * 5 });
    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) h.tick(DT);
    return h;
  };
  assert.ok(serve(3).ball.sb > serve(0).ball.sb, 'slice serve has more backspin');
  assert.ok(serve(1).ball.sb < 0, 'kick serve has topspin');
});
