===== FILE: test/history.test.js =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { entryFrom, addEntry, summarize, loadHistory, saveHistory, HISTORY_MAX } from '../shared/history.js';

const results = (winner) => ({
  winner, score: { sets: [[4, 2]] }, duration: 600, longestRally: 14, rallies: 40, teamPoints: [30, 22],
  players: [{ hits: 50, winners: 7, errors: 3, aces: 2, smashes: 1, volleys: 4, doubleFaults: 0 }, {}, {}, {}],
});

test('an entry is written from the player\'s point of view', () => {
  const win = entryFrom(results(0), { slot: 0, names: ['Me', 'Mate', 'A', 'B'], mode: 'doubles', court: 'night', kind: 'career', at: 5 });
  assert.equal(win.won, true);
  assert.deepEqual(win.sets, [[4, 2]]);
  assert.deepEqual(win.mates, ['Mate']);
  assert.deepEqual(win.opponents, ['A', 'B']);
  assert.equal(win.stats.aces, 2);
  const loss = entryFrom(results(0), { slot: 2, names: ['A', 'B', 'Me', 'Mate'], mode: 'doubles' });
  assert.equal(loss.won, false);
  assert.deepEqual(loss.sets, [[2, 4]], 'sets are flipped for team 1');
  assert.deepEqual(loss.points, [22, 30]);
  const solo = entryFrom(results(0), { slot: 0, names: ['Me', 'x', 'Bot', 'x'], mode: 'singles' });
  assert.deepEqual(solo.opponents, ['Bot']);
  assert.deepEqual(solo.mates, []);
});

test('history keeps the newest first, is capped, and survives reloads', () => {
  let list = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < HISTORY_MAX + 20; i++) list = addEntry(list, entryFrom(results(i % 2), { slot: 0, names: ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd'], at: i }));
  assert.equal(list.length, HISTORY_MAX);
  assert.equal(list[0].at, HISTORY_MAX + 19);
  const store = new Map();
  const storage = { getItem: (k) => store.get(k) ?? null, setItem: (k, v) => store.set(k, v) };
  assert.ok(saveHistory(storage, list));
  assert.equal(loadHistory(storage).length, HISTORY_MAX);
  store.set('padel.history', '{not json');
  assert.deepEqual(loadHistory(storage), [], 'corrupt data is ignored, not thrown');
  store.set('padel.history', JSON.stringify([{ junk: 1 }, list[0]]));
  assert.equal(loadHistory(storage).length, 1, 'invalid rows are dropped');
});

test('summary counts wins, streaks and records', () => {
  const mk = (won, at, rally = 5) => ({ ...entryFrom(results(won ? 0 : 1), { slot: 0, names: ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd'], at }), longestRally: rally });
  const list = [mk(true, 5, 9), mk(true, 4, 30), mk(false, 3), mk(true, 2), mk(true, 1)]; // newest first
  const s = summarize(list);
  assert.equal(s.matches, 5);
  assert.equal(s.wins, 4);
  assert.equal(s.winRate, 80);
  assert.equal(s.streak, 2);
  assert.equal(s.bestStreak, 2);
  assert.equal(s.longestRally, 30);
  assert.equal(summarize([]).winRate, 0);
});
===== END FILE: test/history.test.js =====

===== FILE: test/input.test.js =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {
  ACTIONS, SCHEMES, defaultSettings, applyScheme, sanitizeSettings, loadSettings, saveSettings,
  findConflict, rebind, resetBindings, shapeStick, readPad, codeLabel, InputManager,
} from '../client/js/input.js';

const memStore = () => { const m = new Map(); return { getItem: (k) => m.get(k) ?? null, setItem: (k, v) => m.set(k, v), raw: m }; };
const btn = (pressed) => ({ pressed, value: pressed ? 1 : 0 });
const pad = (over = {}) => ({ axes: [0, 0, 0, 0], buttons: Array.from({ length: 17 }, () => btn(false)), ...over });

test('defaults bind every action on both devices and there are no accidental duplicates on the keyboard', () => {
  const s = defaultSettings();
  for (const dev of ['keyboard', 'pad']) for (const a of ACTIONS) assert.ok(s.bindings[dev][a].length >= 1, `${dev}:${a}`);
  const seen = new Map();
  for (const a of ACTIONS) for (const c of s.bindings.keyboard[a]) {
    assert.ok(!seen.has(c), `${c} used by both ${seen.get(c)} and ${a}`);
    seen.set(c, a);
  }
  assert.ok(s.bindings.keyboard.hit.includes('Space') && s.bindings.keyboard.lob.includes('KeyG'));
});

test('schemes: classic has no mouse aiming, mouse scheme has it, switching restores that scheme\'s bindings', () => {
  const s = defaultSettings();
  assert.equal(s.mouseAim, false);
  applyScheme(s, 'mouse');
  assert.equal(s.mouseAim, true); assert.equal(s.scheme, 'mouse');
  rebind(s, 'keyboard', 'slice', 'KeyF');
  assert.equal(s.scheme, 'custom');
  applyScheme(s, 'classic');
  assert.deepEqual(s.bindings.keyboard.slice, ['KeyR']);
  applyScheme(s, 'nonsense');
  assert.equal(s.scheme, 'classic', 'unknown scheme ignored');
});

test('rebinding actually changes the binding, and duplicates are detected and resolved by swapping', () => {
  const s = defaultSettings();
  assert.equal(findConflict(s, 'keyboard', 'slice', 'KeyQ'), 'soft');
  assert.equal(findConflict(s, 'keyboard', 'slice', 'KeyZ'), null);
  let r = rebind(s, 'keyboard', 'slice', 'KeyZ');
  assert.deepEqual(r, {});
  assert.deepEqual(s.bindings.keyboard.slice, ['KeyZ']);
  r = rebind(s, 'keyboard', 'slice', 'KeyQ'); // Q belongs to "soft": swap
  assert.equal(r.swappedWith, 'soft');
  assert.equal(s.bindings.keyboard.slice[0], 'KeyQ');
  assert.ok(s.bindings.keyboard.soft.includes('KeyZ'), 'the other action received the old binding');
  assert.equal(findConflict(s, 'keyboard', 'soft', 'KeyQ'), 'slice');
  // pad bindings are separate from keyboard bindings
  rebind(s, 'pad', 'hit', 5);
  assert.equal(s.bindings.pad.hit[0], 5);
  assert.equal(s.bindings.pad.jump.includes(5), false);
  assert.deepEqual(s.bindings.keyboard.hit, defaultSettings().bindings.keyboard.hit, 'keyboard untouched');
});

test('mouse buttons can be bound like keys', () => {
  const s = defaultSettings();
  rebind(s, 'keyboard', 'slice', 'Mouse1');
  assert.deepEqual(s.bindings.keyboard.slice, ['Mouse1']);
  assert.equal(codeLabel('keyboard', 'Mouse1'), 'Mouse M');
  assert.equal(codeLabel('pad', 0), 'A');
  assert.equal(codeLabel('keyboard', 'KeyW'), 'W');
});

test('settings persist across reloads, and corrupt or hostile storage falls back safely', () => {
  const st = memStore();
  const s = defaultSettings();
  rebind(s, 'keyboard', 'jump', 'KeyV');
  s.deadzone = 0.3; s.toggleSprint = true; s.mouseAim = true;
  assert.equal(saveSettings(st, s), true);
  const back = loadSettings(st);
  assert.equal(back.bindings.keyboard.jump[0], 'KeyV');
  assert.equal(back.deadzone, 0.3); assert.equal(back.toggleSprint, true); assert.equal(back.mouseAim, true);
  assert.equal(back.scheme, 'custom');

  st.raw.set('padel.input', '{not json');
  assert.deepEqual(loadSettings(st), defaultSettings());
  const hostile = sanitizeSettings({ scheme: 'x', deadzone: 99, curve: -5, bindings: { keyboard: { hit: 'Space', lob: [1, 2, 3], jump: ['A'.repeat(50)] }, pad: { hit: ['a'], lob: [99] } }, mouseAim: 'yes' });
  assert.equal(hit(hostile), 'Space', 'invalid keyboard bindings fall back to defaults');
  assert.deepEqual(hostile.bindings.pad.hit, [0]);
  assert.deepEqual(hostile.bindings.pad.lob, [1]);
  assert.ok(hostile.deadzone <= 0.6 && hostile.curve >= 0.6);
  assert.equal(typeof hostile.mouseAim, 'boolean');
  function hit(x) { return x.bindings.keyboard.hit[0]; }
});

test('gamepad: dead zone, response curve, button mapping and remapped buttons', () => {
  const s = defaultSettings();
  assert.deepEqual(shapeStick(0.1, 0.1, 0.18, 1.4), { x: 0, y: 0 }, 'inside the dead zone');
  const full = shapeStick(1, 0, 0.18, 1.4);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(full.x - 1) < 1e-9);
  const half = shapeStick(0.59, 0, 0.18, 2);
  assert.ok(half.x > 0 && half.x < 0.3, 'curve keeps small tilts precise');

  const r = readPad(pad({ axes: [1, -1, 0.9, 0], buttons: Object.assign(pad().buttons, { 0: btn(true), 7: btn(true) }) }), s);
  assert.ok(r.move.x > 0.6 && r.move.y > 0.6, 'stick up is forward (screen y flipped), diagonal normalised');
  assert.ok(r.aim.x > 0.5 && Math.abs(r.aim.y) < 1e-9);
  assert.ok(r.down.has('hit') && r.down.has('sprint') && !r.down.has('lob'));

  rebind(s, 'pad', 'lob', 0); // A now lobs
  const r2 = readPad(pad({ buttons: Object.assign(pad().buttons, { 0: btn(true) }) }), s);
  assert.ok(r2.down.has('lob') && !r2.down.has('hit'));
});

test('input manager: events, held state, toggle sprint, capture cancel with Escape, device switching', () => {
  const st = memStore();
  const im = new InputManager(st);
  const fired = [];
  im.on('hit', () => fired.push('hit')); im.on('pause', () => fired.push('pause'));
  // simulate what the DOM handlers do
  for (const a of im.actionsFor('keyboard', 'Space')) im.press(a);
  for (const a of im.actionsFor('keyboard', 'Escape')) im.press(a);
  assert.deepEqual(fired, ['hit', 'pause']);

  im.down.add('KeyD'); im.down.add('KeyW');
  const ax = im.axes();
  assert.ok(ax.x > 0 && ax.y > 0 && Math.hypot(ax.x, ax.y) <= 1 + 1e-9);
  im.down.clear();

  im.settings.toggleSprint = true;
  assert.equal(im.actionDown('sprint'), false);
  im.press('sprint'); assert.equal(im.actionDown('sprint'), true);
  im.press('sprint'); assert.equal(im.actionDown('sprint'), false);

  let got = null;
  im.capture((d, c) => { got = [d, c]; }, 'keyboard');
  im.finishCapture('keyboard', 'Escape'); // cancelled, nothing bound
  assert.equal(got, null); assert.equal(im.captureCb, null);
  im.capture((d, c) => { got = [d, c]; }, 'pad');
  im.finishCapture('keyboard', 'KeyP'); // wrong device for this tab: keeps waiting
  assert.equal(got, null);
  im.finishCapture('pad', 3);
  assert.deepEqual(got, ['pad', 3]);

  im.padStick = { move: { x: -0.5, y: 0.5 }, aim: { x: 0.8, y: 0 } };
  assert.ok(im.axes().x < 0, 'analog movement is read');
  assert.equal(im.aimStick().active, true);
  im.settings.stickAim = false;
  assert.equal(im.aimStick().active, false, 'stick aiming can be switched off');
  assert.ok(Object.keys(SCHEMES).length >= 4);
});

===== FILE: test/lob.test.js =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { PadelGame } from '../shared/game.js';
import { stepBall } from '../shared/physics.js';

function flight(q) {
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng: () => 0.5 });
  const b = { x: 0, y: 1, z: -3, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0, sb: 0, ss: 0 };
  const v = g.planShot('lob', b, 1, 0, 8.5, q, 8, null, { face: 0, e: 0.1, lateral: 0, forward: 0, spinMul: 1, powerMul: 1 });
  const ball = { ...b, vx: v.vx, vy: v.vy, vz: v.vz, sb: v.sb, ss: v.ss };
  let apex = 0, land = null;
  for (let i = 0; i < 300 && !land; i++) {
    const ev = [];
    stepBall(ball, 1 / 60, ev);
    apex = Math.max(apex, ball.y);
    const f = ev.find((e) => e.type === 'floor');
    if (f) land = f;
  }
  return { apex, land };
}

test('a clean lob goes high and deep; a poor one is low and short (easy to smash)', () => {
  const good = flight(0.95), poor = flight(0.35);
  assert.ok(good.land && poor.land);
  assert.ok(good.land.z > 7, `perfect lob lands deep (${good.land.z.toFixed(1)} m)`);
  assert.ok(poor.land.z < good.land.z - 2, `poor lob lands short (${poor.land.z.toFixed(1)} m)`);
  assert.ok(good.apex > poor.apex + 0.6, `and higher (${good.apex.toFixed(1)} vs ${poor.apex.toFixed(1)} m)`);
  assert.ok(poor.apex > 2.2, 'a poor lob is still a lob: in smash range, not a drive');
});

import { botInput, makeBotMemory } from '../shared/ai.js';

test('doubles: when a lob is coming the pair drops back (the one not chasing goes to the baseline) and keeps its width', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng: () => 0.5 });
  const mem = [0, 1, 2, 3].map(makeBotMemory);
  g.phase = 'rally';
  g.rally = { serving: false, faults: 0, lastTeam: 1, lastSlot: 2, bounces: 0, hits: 4, netTouched: false, serveXSign: 1 };
  Object.assign(g.players[0], { x: -2, z: -3 }); Object.assign(g.players[1], { x: 2, z: -2.6 });
  Object.assign(g.players[2], { x: -1, z: 3.2 }); Object.assign(g.players[3], { x: 1.5, z: 3.0 });
  // a high ball from the opponents, heading for team 0's side
  Object.assign(g.ball, { x: 0, y: 3.5, z: 1, vx: 0, vy: 2, vz: -3 });
  for (let i = 0; i < 70; i++) {
    for (const k of [0, 1]) g.applyInput(k, botInput(g, k, mem[k], 'hard', 1 / 60));
    g.players[2].input = g.players[3].input = { mx: 0, mz: 0 };
    g.tick(1 / 60);
    g.ball.y = 3.5; g.ball.vy = 2; g.ball.z = 1; // hold the lob in the air for the test
  }
  const [a, b] = [g.players[0], g.players[1]];
  assert.ok(Math.min(a.z, b.z) < -6.5, `one drops to the baseline (${a.z.toFixed(1)}, ${b.z.toFixed(1)})`);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(a.x - b.x) > 1.2, 'and the pair keeps its width');
});

test('a ball reached at full stretch or hit with great pace gets a weaker reply', () => {
  const reply = (px, speed) => {
    const g = new PadelGame({ rng: () => 0.5 });
    g.phase = 'rally';
    g.rally = { serving: false, faults: 0, lastTeam: 1, lastSlot: 2, bounces: 0, hits: 3, netTouched: false, serveXSign: 1 };
    Object.assign(g.ball, { x: 0, y: 1.0, z: -3, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: -speed });
    Object.assign(g.players[0], { x: px, z: -3.0, vx: 0, vz: 0 });
    g.startSwing(g.players[0], { ax: 0, az: 8, mod: 0, lob: false });
    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
      g.ball.x = 0; g.ball.z = -3; g.ball.y = 1.0; // hold the ball where it is for the test
      g.tick(1 / 60);
      const h = g.drainEvents().find((e) => e.e === 'hit');
      if (h) return { q: h.q, label: h.label, speed: Math.hypot(g.ball.vx, g.ball.vz) };
    }
    return null;
  };
  const easy = reply(-0.95, 6), stretched = reply(-1.75, 6), fast = reply(-0.95, 26);
  assert.ok(easy && stretched && fast);
  assert.ok(stretched.q < easy.q - 0.15, `stretched ${stretched.q} vs ${easy.q}`);
  assert.ok(fast.q < easy.q - 0.1, `fast ${fast.q} vs ${easy.q}`);
  assert.ok(stretched.speed < easy.speed, 'and the reply is slower');
});
===== END FILE: test/lob.test.js =====

===== FILE: test/looks.test.js =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { ARCHETYPES, RACKETS, COURTS, COURT_IDS, DEFAULT_LOOKS, sanitizeLook } from '../shared/looks.js';
import { Store } from '../server/store.js';
import { RoomManager } from '../server/rooms.js';
import { stepBall, makeBall } from '../shared/physics.js';
import { PadelGame } from '../shared/game.js';

test('catalogue: four distinct archetypes, eight distinct rackets, three courts', () => {
  assert.equal(ARCHETYPES.length, 4);
  assert.equal(new Set(ARCHETYPES.map((a) => a.height)).size, 4, 'different heights');
  assert.equal(RACKETS.length, 8);
  assert.equal(new Set(RACKETS.map((r) => r.name)).size, 8);
  assert.equal(new Set(RACKETS.map((r) => `${r.face}-${r.shape}-${r.holes}`)).size, 8, 'distinct designs');
  assert.deepEqual(COURT_IDS.sort(), ['indoor', 'night', 'outdoor', 'rooftop']);
});

test('looks from clients are always clamped to valid ids', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(sanitizeLook(null, 2), DEFAULT_LOOKS[2]);
  assert.deepEqual(sanitizeLook({ arch: 99, hair: -3, skin: 'x', racket: 1e9 }, 1), DEFAULT_LOOKS[1]);
  assert.deepEqual(sanitizeLook({ arch: 3, hair: 4, skin: 2, racket: 7 }, 0), { arch: 3, hair: 4, skin: 2, racket: 7 });
});

test('court surface is a small, documented difference: outdoor turf is slightly slower', () => {
  const bounce = (court) => {
    const g = new PadelGame({ settings: { court } });
    const b = { ...makeBall(), x: 0, y: 1.5, z: 2, vz: 8, vy: -2, fe: g.ball.fe, ff: g.ball.ff };
    const ev = [];
    let peak = 0, first = false, vz = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) { ev.length = 0; stepBall(b, 1 / 60, ev); if (!first && ev.some((e) => e.type === 'floor')) { first = true; vz = b.vz; } if (first) peak = Math.max(peak, b.y); }
    return { peak, vz };
  };
  const indoor = bounce('indoor'), outdoor = bounce('outdoor');
  assert.ok(outdoor.vz < indoor.vz && outdoor.peak < indoor.peak);
  assert.ok(indoor.vz / outdoor.vz < 1.05, 'but only by a few percent');
});

test('server keeps looks and court consistent for everybody in the room', () => {
  const dir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'padel-looks-'));
  const mgr = new RoomManager(new Store(dir));
  const sent = [];
  const fakeWs = { readyState: 1, send: (d) => sent.push(JSON.parse(d)) };
  const a = mgr.create({ token: 'a', name: 'Ana', ws: fakeWs, look: { arch: 2, hair: 5, skin: 1, racket: 6 } });
  const b = mgr.join(a.room.code, { token: 'b', name: 'Ben', ws: fakeWs, look: { arch: 99 } });
  assert.deepEqual(a.member.look, { arch: 2, hair: 5, skin: 1, racket: 6 });
  assert.deepEqual(b.member.look, DEFAULT_LOOKS[0], 'garbage falls back to a valid look');
  assert.equal(a.room.setSettings(a.member, { court: 'night' }), null);
  assert.equal(a.room.setSettings(a.member, { court: 'moon' }), null);
  assert.equal(a.room.settings.court, 'night', 'unknown courts are ignored');
  b.member.ready = true;
  assert.equal(a.room.start(a.member), null);
  const start = sent.filter((m) => m.t === 'start');
  assert.equal(start.length, 2);
  for (const s of start) { assert.equal(s.settings.court, 'night'); assert.equal(s.looks.length, 4); assert.equal(s.looks[0].racket, 6); }
  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});
===== END FILE: test/looks.test.js =====

===== FILE: test/multiplayer.test.js =====
import test, { before, after } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { WebSocket } from 'ws';
import { createGameServer } from '../server/index.js';
import { Store } from '../server/store.js';

let srv;
let dataDir;
const url = () => `ws://127.0.0.1:${srv.port}/ws`;

class Client {
  constructor(name) {
    this.name = name;
    this.msgs = [];
    this.waiters = [];
    this.token = null;
  }
  async connect(token) {
    this.ws = new WebSocket(url());
    this.ws.on('message', (d) => {
      const m = JSON.parse(d.toString());
      this.msgs.push(m);
      this.waiters = this.waiters.filter((w) => !w.try(m));
    });
    await new Promise((r) => this.ws.once('open', r));
    this.send({ t: 'hello', name: this.name, token });
    const w = await this.wait((m) => m.t === 'welcome');
    this.token = w.token;
    return this;
  }
  send(o) { this.ws.send(JSON.stringify(o)); }
  /** resolves with the first message (past or future) matching pred */
  wait(pred, ms = 8000, { past = true } = {}) {
    if (past) { const hit = this.msgs.find(pred); if (hit) return Promise.resolve(hit); }
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const t = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(`${this.name}: timeout waiting for message`)), ms);
      this.waiters.push({ try: (m) => { if (pred(m)) { clearTimeout(t); resolve(m); return true; } return false; } });
    });
  }
  waitEvent(kind, ms = 15000, opts) {
    return this.wait((m) => m.t === 'ev' && m.ev.some((e) => e.e === kind), ms, opts)
      .then((m) => m.ev.find((e) => e.e === kind));
  }
  lastRoom() { return [...this.msgs].reverse().find((m) => m.t === 'room'); }
  close() { this.ws.close(); }
}

async function fourPlayers() {
  const cs = ['Ana', 'Ben', 'Cleo', 'Dan'].map((n) => new Client(n));
  await Promise.all(cs.map((c) => c.connect()));
  cs[0].send({ t: 'create' });
  const room = await cs[0].wait((m) => m.t === 'room');
  for (const c of cs.slice(1)) {
    c.send({ t: 'join', code: room.code });
    await c.wait((m) => m.t === 'room');
  }
  return { cs, code: room.code };
}

before(async () => {
  dataDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'padel-test-'));
  srv = await createGameServer({ port: 0, dataDir, host: '127.0.0.1' });
});
after(async () => {
  await srv.close();
  fs.rmSync(dataDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('a room can be created and has a shareable code', async () => {
  const a = await new Client('Solo').connect();
  a.send({ t: 'create' });
  const room = await a.wait((m) => m.t === 'room');
  assert.match(room.code, /^[A-Z2-9]{5}$/);
  assert.equal(room.hostId, room.you);
  assert.equal(room.state, 'lobby');
  a.close();
});

test('invalid code and unknown room give clear errors', async () => {
  const a = await new Client('Lost').connect();
  a.send({ t: 'join', code: 'ZZZZZ' });
  const err = await a.wait((m) => m.t === 'error');
  assert.equal(err.code, 'join-failed');
  assert.match(err.message, /not found/i);
  a.close();
});

test('four clients join, teams are assigned, ready state syncs, a fifth is rejected', async () => {
  const { cs, code } = await fourPlayers();
  const view = await cs[3].wait((m) => m.t === 'room' && m.slots.every(Boolean));
  assert.deepEqual(view.slots.map((s) => s.name), ['Ana', 'Ben', 'Cleo', 'Dan']);
  assert.equal(view.youSlot, 3); // Dan sits in team B

  // host swaps Ana (team A) with Dan (team B)
  const room = cs[0].lastRoom();
  const danId = room.slots[3].id;
  cs[0].send({ t: 'assign', id: danId, slot: 0 });
  const swapped = await cs[3].wait((m) => m.t === 'room' && m.slots[0]?.name === 'Dan', 3000, { past: false });
  assert.equal(swapped.slots[3].name, 'Ana');

  // a non-host cannot move people
  cs[1].send({ t: 'assign', id: danId, slot: 1 });
  await cs[1].wait((m) => m.t === 'error' && m.code === 'rejected');

  // ready is visible to everyone
  cs[2].send({ t: 'ready', ready: true });
  const r = await cs[0].wait((m) => m.t === 'room' && m.slots.find((s) => s?.name === 'Cleo')?.ready, 3000, { past: false });
  assert.ok(r);

  // the host cannot start until everybody is ready
  cs[0].send({ t: 'start' });
  const err = await cs[0].wait((m) => m.t === 'error' && /not ready/.test(m.message), 3000, { past: false });
  assert.ok(err);

  // room is full
  const e = await new Client('Eve').connect();
  e.send({ t: 'join', code });
  const full = await e.wait((m) => m.t === 'error');
  assert.match(full.message, /full/i);
  [e, ...cs].forEach((c) => c.close());
});

test('full match flow: consistent state, server-side scoring, cheating ignored, reconnect, persistence', async () => {
  const { cs } = await fourPlayers();
  const [host, ...others] = cs;
  host.send({ t: 'settings', settings: { sets: 1, gamesPerSet: 2 } });
  for (const c of others) c.send({ t: 'ready', ready: true });
  await host.wait((m) => m.t === 'room' && m.slots.every((s) => s.ready) && m.settings.gamesPerSet === 2, 3000, { past: false }).catch(() => {});
  host.send({ t: 'start' });

  const starts = await Promise.all(cs.map((c) => c.wait((m) => m.t === 'start')));
  assert.deepEqual(starts.map((s) => s.slot), [0, 1, 2, 3]);
  assert.deepEqual(starts[0].names, ['Ana', 'Ben', 'Cleo', 'Dan']);

  // all four see the same snapshot (same tick => identical ball + players)
  const snaps = await Promise.all(cs.map((c) => c.wait((m) => m.t === 'snap' && m.n >= 30, 4000)));
  const byTick = new Map(snaps.map((s) => [s.n, s]));
  const common = [...cs[0].msgs, ...cs[1].msgs].find((m) => m.t === 'snap' && cs.every((c) => c.msgs.some((x) => x.t === 'snap' && x.n === m.n)));
  if (common) {
    const copies = cs.map((c) => c.msgs.find((x) => x.t === 'snap' && x.n === common.n));
    for (const s of copies) assert.deepEqual(s, copies[0]);
  }
  assert.ok(byTick.size >= 1);

  // cheating: clients cannot award points, set scores or send absurd inputs
  const serveEv = await cs[0].waitEvent('serve');
  const serverSlot = serveEv.slot;
  const cheat = cs[(serverSlot + 1) % 4];
  cheat.send({ t: 'score', team: 0, points: 99 });
  cheat.send({ t: 'point', winner: 0 });
  cheat.send({ t: 'in', inputs: [{ seq: 1, mx: 9999, mz: -9999, swing: true, ax: 1e9, az: 1e9, ball: { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 } }] });
  cheat.send({ t: 'results', winner: 0 });

  // the server's player serves an ace-ish serve: nobody returns, so the server's team wins the point
  const srvClient = cs[serverSlot];
  const dir = serverSlot < 2 ? 1 : -1;
  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1200));
  srvClient.send({ t: 'in', inputs: [{ seq: 5, swing: true, ax: 0, az: dir * 5 }] });
  const pts = await Promise.all(cs.map((c) => c.waitEvent('point', 15000)));
  assert.equal(pts[0].winner, serverSlot < 2 ? 0 : 1);
  assert.equal(pts[0].reason, 'bounce');
  for (const p of pts) assert.deepEqual(p.score, pts[0].score); // everyone sees the same score
  assert.deepEqual(pts[0].score.totalPoints.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0), 1);
  assert.equal(pts[0].score.points[pts[0].winner], '15');

  // disconnect + reconnect with the same guest token keeps the seat
  const victim = cs[2];
  const token = victim.token;
  victim.close();
  await host.wait((m) => m.t === 'room' && m.slots[2]?.connected === false, 4000, { past: false });
  const back = await new Client('Cleo').connect(token);
  const restart = await back.wait((m) => m.t === 'start');
  assert.equal(restart.slot, 2);
  const roomBack = await back.wait((m) => m.t === 'room' && m.slots[2]?.connected === true);
  assert.equal(roomBack.youSlot, 2);
  cs[2] = back;

  // Force the match to the last point so we can test completion + persistence quickly.
  const room = [...srv.rooms.rooms.values()].find((r) => r.state === 'match');
  const g = room.game;
  g.match.games = [1, 0];
  g.match.points = [0, 3];
  g.match.pointsInGame = 3;
  // The next point is decided by the server's own physics: team 1 wins it with a game-winning ace.
  // Let the current point end, then serve again.
  const nextServe = await cs[0].waitEvent('serve', 15000, { past: false });
  const s2 = nextServe.slot;
  const winnerTeam = s2 < 2 ? 0 : 1;
  // make the team that is about to win the point be the one on 40 and a game up
  g.match.games = [winnerTeam === 0 ? 1 : 0, winnerTeam === 1 ? 1 : 0];
  g.match.points = [winnerTeam === 0 ? 3 : 0, winnerTeam === 1 ? 3 : 0];
  const dir2 = s2 < 2 ? 1 : -1;
  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1200));
  cs[s2].send({ t: 'in', inputs: [{ seq: 9, swing: true, ax: 0, az: dir2 * 5 }] });
  const done = await Promise.all(cs.map((c) => c.waitEvent('match', 20000)));
  assert.equal(done[0].winner, winnerTeam);
  const results = await Promise.all(cs.map((c) => c.wait((m) => m.t === 'results', 12000)));
  for (const r of results) assert.deepEqual(r.results, results[0].results);
  assert.equal(results[0].results.winner, winnerTeam);
  assert.ok(results[0].results.duration > 0);

  // persistence: the server wrote the result from its own simulation
  srv.store.flush();
  const reloaded = new Store(dataDir);
  assert.ok(reloaded.data.matches.length >= 1);
  const last = reloaded.data.matches.at(-1);
  assert.equal(last.winner, winnerTeam);
  const prof = reloaded.data.profiles[cs[winnerTeam * 2].token];
  assert.equal(prof.wins, 1);
  assert.ok(prof.xp >= 100);

  // rematch without creating a new room
  cs.forEach((c) => c.send({ t: 'rematch' }));
  const second = await Promise.all(cs.map((c) => c.wait((m) => m.t === 'start' && m !== starts[0] && c.msgs.indexOf(m) > c.msgs.findIndex((x) => x.t === 'results'), 6000)));
  assert.equal(second.length, 4);
  cs.forEach((c) => c.close());
});

test('bots fill empty seats for practice, and a lone player can serve against them', async () => {
  const a = await new Client('Practice').connect();
  a.send({ t: 'create' });
  await a.wait((m) => m.t === 'room');
  a.send({ t: 'start' });
  const start = await a.wait((m) => m.t === 'start');
  assert.deepEqual(start.names, ['Practice', 'AI', 'AI', 'AI']);
  const room = await a.wait((m) => m.t === 'room' && m.state === 'match');
  assert.deepEqual(room.slots.map((s) => s.kind), ['human', 'bot', 'bot', 'bot']);
  await a.wait((m) => m.t === 'snap');
  a.close();
});
===== END FILE: test/multiplayer.test.js =====

===== FILE: test/openings.test.js =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { stepBall, stepPlayer, makeBall } from '../shared/physics.js';
import { PadelGame } from '../shared/game.js';
import { COURT } from '../shared/constants.js';
import { botInput, makeBotMemory } from '../shared/ai.js';

const door = (COURT.DOOR.z0 + COURT.DOOR.z1) / 2;
const doorB = -door; // door on team 0's half

function fly(b, secs = 3) {
  const ev = [];
  for (let t = 0; t < secs * 60; t++) stepBall(b, 1 / 60, ev);
  return ev;
}

test('a ball aimed at a door leaves the court; elsewhere the glass bounces it', () => {
  const a = { ...makeBall(), doors: true, x: 4, y: 1, z: door, vx: 8, vy: 1, vz: 0 };
  const evA = fly(a, 0.5);
  assert.ok(evA.some((e) => e.type === 'door'));
  assert.ok(a.x > 5);
  const b = { ...makeBall(), doors: true, x: 4, y: 1, z: 5, vx: 8, vy: 1, vz: 0 };
  const evB = fly(b, 0.5);
  assert.ok(evB.some((e) => e.type === 'wall') && !evB.some((e) => e.type === 'door'));
  assert.ok(b.x < 5);
});

test('above the door height the glass still bounces the ball', () => {
  const a = { ...makeBall(), doors: true, x: 4, y: 2.6, z: door, vx: 6, vy: 0, vz: 0 };
  const ev = fly(a, 0.4);
  assert.ok(ev.some((e) => e.type === 'wall'));
});

test('the outer fence keeps the ball in the strip, and it can return through the door', () => {
  const a = { ...makeBall(), doors: true, x: 4.5, y: 1, z: door, vx: 14, vy: 2, vz: 0 };
  const ev = fly(a, 2);
  assert.ok(ev.some((e) => e.type === 'door'));
  assert.ok(Math.abs(a.x) <= COURT.HALF_W + COURT.PIT.w);
});

test('players only reach the strip through the door', () => {
  const p = { x: 4.7, z: -door, vx: 0, vz: 0, stamina: 100, team: 0, pit: true };
  for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) stepPlayer(p, { mx: 1, mz: 0 }, 1 / 60);
  assert.ok(p.x > 5.3, 'walked out through the door');
  const q = { x: 4.7, z: -5, vx: 0, vz: 0, stamina: 100, team: 0, pit: true };
  for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) stepPlayer(q, { mx: 1, mz: 0 }, 1 / 60);
  assert.ok(q.x <= 4.75 + 1e-9, 'blocked by the wall away from the door');
});

test('1v1 has no doors', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ settings: { mode: 'singles' } });
  assert.equal(g.ball.doors, false);
});

test('rules: a ball that leaves through a door before bouncing is out; after a bounce the receiver may play it from outside', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ settings: { mode: 'doubles' } });
  g.phase = 'rally';
  g.rally = { serving: false, faults: 0, lastTeam: 0, lastSlot: 0, bounces: 0, hits: 2, netTouched: false, serveXSign: 1 };
  g.onBallEvent({ type: 'door', x: 5.1, y: 1, z: door });
  assert.equal(g.phase, 'point');
  assert.equal(g.events.find((e) => e.e === 'point').winner, 1);

  const h = new PadelGame({ settings: { mode: 'doubles' } });
  h.phase = 'rally';
  h.rally = { serving: false, faults: 0, lastTeam: 0, lastSlot: 0, bounces: 1, hits: 2, netTouched: false, serveXSign: 1 };
  h.onBallEvent({ type: 'door', x: 5.1, y: 1, z: door });
  assert.equal(h.phase, 'rally');
});

test('a return from outside must land inside the court', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ settings: { mode: 'doubles' } });
  g.phase = 'rally';
  g.rally = { serving: false, faults: 0, lastTeam: 1, lastSlot: 2, bounces: 0, hits: 3, netTouched: false, serveXSign: 1 };
  g.onBallEvent({ type: 'floor', x: 6.2, z: 5.5, speed: 3 });
  assert.equal(g.events.find((e) => e.e === 'point').winner, 0);
});

test('a bot-free full scenario: the receiver goes out, hits a lob over the glass and the ball lands in the opponent court', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ settings: { mode: 'doubles', openings: true }, rng: () => 0.5 });
  g.phase = 'rally';
  g.rally = { serving: false, faults: 0, lastTeam: 1, lastSlot: 2, bounces: 1, hits: 2, netTouched: false, serveXSign: 1 };
  const b = g.ball;
  Object.assign(b, { x: 5.6, y: 0.9, z: -door, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0, ex: true });
  const p = g.players[0];
  Object.assign(p, { x: 5.2, z: -door, vx: 0, vz: 0 });
  g.players[1].x = -3; g.players[1].z = -2;
  p.input = { ...p.input, mx: 0, mz: 0, ax: -1, az: 8 };
  g.startSwing(p, { ax: -1, az: 8, mod: 0, lob: false });
  let landed = null;
  for (let i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && g.phase === 'rally'; i++) {
    g.tick(1 / 60);
    for (const e of g.drainEvents()) if (e.e === 'ball' && e.k === 'floor' && !landed) landed = e;
  }
  assert.ok(landed, 'ball landed');
  assert.ok(landed.z > 0 && Math.abs(landed.x) < 5, `landed in the opponent court (${landed.x}, ${landed.z})`);
});

test('bots follow a ball out of the door and return it into play', () => {
  let outHits = 0;
  for (let m = 0; m < 12; m++) {
    let s = m + 7;
    const rng = () => { s = (s * 1664525 + 1013904223) >>> 0; return s / 4294967296; };
    const g = new PadelGame({ rng, settings: { mode: 'doubles' } });
    const mem = [0, 1, 2, 3].map(makeBotMemory);
    g.phase = 'rally';
    g.rally = { serving: false, faults: 0, lastTeam: 1, lastSlot: 2, bounces: 1, hits: 3, netTouched: false, serveXSign: 1 };
    const side = m % 2 ? 1 : -1;
    Object.assign(g.ball, { x: 4.9 * side, y: 1.0, z: -2.2, vx: 2.6 * side, vy: 1.0, vz: -0.3, ex: false });
    Object.assign(g.players[0], { x: -2, z: -6 }); Object.assign(g.players[1], { x: 1, z: -3 });
    Object.assign(g.players[2], { x: 0, z: 6 }); Object.assign(g.players[3], { x: 1, z: 2 });
    for (let i = 0; i < 60 * 6 && g.phase === 'rally'; i++) {
      for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) g.applyInput(k, botInput(g, k, mem[k], 'hard', 1 / 60));
      g.tick(1 / 60);
      for (const e of g.drainEvents()) if (e.e === 'hit' && Math.abs(e.x) > 5) outHits++;
    }
  }
  assert.ok(outHits >= 6, `bots hit from outside in most cases (${outHits}/12)`);
});

test('the net tape can pop a ball over (cord) without it being a fault', () => {
  const b = { ...makeBall(), x: 0, y: 0.97, z: -0.05, vx: 0, vy: -0.4, vz: 6 };
  const ev = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) stepBall(b, 1 / 60, ev);
  assert.ok(ev.some((e) => e.type === 'cord') || ev.some((e) => e.type === 'net') || b.z > 0);
});
===== END FILE: test/openings.test.js =====

===== FILE: test/pause.test.js =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { Room, PAUSE_MAX_MS, PAUSE_REQUEST_MS } from '../server/rooms.js';

const fakeWs = () => ({ readyState: 1, sent: [], send(m) { this.sent.push(JSON.parse(m)); }, last(t) { return [...this.sent].reverse().find((x) => x.t === t); } });

function room(humans = 2) {
  const r = new Room('TEST1', { recordMatch: () => ({}) });
  const ms = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < humans; i++) ms.push(r.addMember({ token: 't' + i, name: 'P' + i, ws: fakeWs(), look: null }));
  r.beginMatch();
  return { r, ms };
}

test('a pause needs the other player to accept, then freezes the match until both resume', () => {
  const { r, ms } = room(2);
  const [a, b] = ms;
  const t0 = Date.now();
  assert.equal(r.pauseCtl(a, 'req', t0), null);
  assert.equal(r.pause.phase, 'request', 'only a request so far');
  assert.equal(b.ws.last('pause').phase, 'request');
  const time = r.game.time;
  r.step(1 / 60);
  assert.ok(r.game.time > time, 'the match keeps running until the other player accepts');
  r.pauseCtl(b, 'accept', t0 + 1000);
  assert.equal(r.pause.phase, 'active');
  const frozen = r.game.time;
  for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) r.step(1 / 60);
  assert.equal(r.game.time, frozen, 'frozen while paused');
  r.pauseCtl(a, 'resume', t0 + 2000);
  assert.equal(r.pause.phase, 'active', 'one player resuming is not enough');
  r.pauseCtl(b, 'resume', t0 + 3000);
  assert.equal(r.pause, null);
  assert.equal(a.ws.last('pause').phase, 'off');
  r.step(1 / 60);
  assert.ok(r.game.time > frozen, 'and the match goes on');
});

test('a pause ends by itself after 30 seconds, a request expires after 10, and a player can decline', () => {
  const { r, ms } = room(2);
  const [a, b] = ms;
  r.pauseCtl(a, 'req', 0);
  r.pauseEvaluate(PAUSE_REQUEST_MS + 1);
  assert.equal(r.pause, null, 'unanswered request expired');
  r.pauseCtl(a, 'req', 20_000);
  r.pauseCtl(b, 'deny', 21_000);
  assert.equal(r.pause, null);
  assert.equal(a.ws.last('pause').phase, 'denied');
  r.pauseCtl(a, 'req', 30_000); r.pauseCtl(b, 'accept', 30_500);
  assert.equal(r.pause.phase, 'active');
  r.pauseEvaluate(30_500 + PAUSE_MAX_MS + 1);
  assert.equal(r.pause, null, 'never longer than 30 s');
});

test('limits: two pauses per player, a lone human pauses at once, and bots do not need to accept', () => {
  const { r, ms } = room(2);
  const [a, b] = ms;
  for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) { r.pauseCtl(a, 'req', i * 100_000); r.pauseCtl(b, 'accept', i * 100_000 + 1); r.pauseCtl(a, 'resume', i * 100_000 + 2); r.pauseCtl(b, 'resume', i * 100_000 + 3); }
  assert.match(r.pauseCtl(a, 'req', 500_000), /used your pauses/);
  const solo = room(1);
  assert.equal(solo.r.pauseCtl(solo.ms[0], 'req', 0), null);
  assert.equal(solo.r.pause.phase, 'active', 'with only bots around nobody has to accept');
});

test('a player who disconnects does not block the pause', () => {
  const { r, ms } = room(2);
  const [a, b] = ms;
  r.pauseCtl(a, 'req', 0); r.pauseCtl(b, 'accept', 1);
  r.disconnect(b);
  r.pauseCtl(a, 'resume', 2);
  assert.equal(r.pause, null, 'the remaining connected human is enough');
});

import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { WebSocket } from 'ws';
import { createGameServer } from '../server/index.js';

test('over real WebSockets: request, accept, frozen snapshots, resume', async () => {
  const dir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'padel-pause-'));
  const srv = await createGameServer({ port: 0, dataDir: dir, host: '127.0.0.1' });
  const open = async (name) => {
    const ws = new WebSocket(`ws://127.0.0.1:${srv.port}/ws`);
    const msgs = [];
    ws.on('message', (d) => msgs.push(JSON.parse(d.toString())));
    await new Promise((r) => ws.once('open', r));
    const send = (o) => ws.send(JSON.stringify(o));
    send({ t: 'hello', name });
    const until = async (pred, ms = 6000) => { const t0 = Date.now(); for (;;) { const m = msgs.find(pred); if (m) return m; if (Date.now() - t0 > ms) throw new Error(name + ': timeout'); await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 20)); } };
    await until((m) => m.t === 'welcome');
    return { ws, msgs, send, until };
  };
  try {
    const a = await open('A'), b = await open('B');
    a.send({ t: 'create', name: 'A' });
    const code = (await a.until((m) => m.t === 'room')).code;
    b.send({ t: 'join', code, name: 'B' });
    await b.until((m) => m.t === 'room');
    b.send({ t: 'ready', ready: true });
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
    a.send({ t: 'start' });
    await a.until((m) => m.t === 'start'); await b.until((m) => m.t === 'start');
    a.send({ t: 'pauseReq' });
    const req = await b.until((m) => m.t === 'pause' && m.phase === 'request');
    assert.equal(req.by, 'A');
    b.send({ t: 'pauseAccept' });
    await a.until((m) => m.t === 'pause' && m.phase === 'active');
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 150));
    const n0 = a.msgs.filter((m) => m.t === 'snap').length;
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 400));
    assert.equal(a.msgs.filter((m) => m.t === 'snap').length, n0, 'no snapshots while paused');
    a.send({ t: 'pauseResume' });
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 150));
    assert.equal(a.msgs.filter((m) => m.t === 'pause' && m.phase === 'off').length, 0, 'one Resume is not enough');
    b.send({ t: 'pauseResume' });
    await a.until((m) => m.t === 'pause' && m.phase === 'off');
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 300));
    assert.ok(a.msgs.filter((m) => m.t === 'snap').length > n0, 'the match runs again');
    a.ws.close(); b.ws.close();
  } finally { await srv.close(); fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
});

import { PadelGame } from '../shared/game.js';

test('the server stays planted on the line until the serve is hit', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng: () => 0.5 });
  const s = g.players[g.serverSlot];
  const { x, z } = s;
  for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) { g.applyInput(g.serverSlot, { mx: 1, mz: 1, sprint: true }); g.tick(1 / 60); }
  assert.equal(g.phase, 'serve');
  assert.equal(s.x, x); assert.equal(s.z, z);
});

test('after a fault everybody goes back to their starting place for the second serve', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng: () => 0.5 });
  const start = g.players.map((p) => [p.x, p.z]);
  g.phase = 'rally';
  g.rally = { serving: true, faults: 0, lastTeam: g.players[g.serverSlot].team, lastSlot: g.serverSlot, bounces: 0, hits: 1, netTouched: false, serveXSign: 1 };
  for (const [i, p] of g.players.entries()) { p.x += 1.5; p.z += (i % 2 ? 1 : -1) * 0.8; p.vx = 3; } // the receivers moved after the serve
  g.fault('long-or-wide');
  assert.equal(g.phase, 'serve');
  g.players.forEach((p, i) => { assert.deepEqual([p.x, p.z], start[i]); assert.equal(p.vx, 0); });
});

===== FILE: test/positioning.test.js =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { PadelGame } from '../shared/game.js';
import { botInput, makeBotMemory } from '../shared/ai.js';

function simulate(level, seeds, { human = false } = {}) {
  const M = { t: 0, net: 0, back: 0, stag: 0, rallies: 0, pts: 0, stuckBack: 0, long: 0 };
  for (let seed = 1; seed <= seeds; seed++) {
    let s = seed * 7919;
    const rng = () => { s = (s * 1664525 + 1013904223) % 4294967296; return s / 4294967296; };
    const g = new PadelGame({ rng, settings: { sets: 1, gamesPerSet: 3 } });
    const mem = [0, 1, 2, 3].map(makeBotMemory);
    if (human) g.players[0].human = true;
    let netTicks = [[0, 0], [0, 0]], rt = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < 60 * 60 * 10; i++) {
      for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) g.applyInput(k, botInput(g, k, mem[k], k === 0 && human ? 'normal' : level));
      g.tick(1 / 60);
      if (g.phase === 'rally' && g.rally.hits >= 2) {
        rt++;
        for (let t = 0; t < 2; t++) {
          const a = Math.abs(g.players[t * 2].z), b = Math.abs(g.players[t * 2 + 1].z);
          M.t++;
          if (a < 4.6 && b < 4.6) M.net++; else if (a > 5.6 && b > 5.6) M.back++; else if ((a < 4.6 && b > 5.6) || (b < 4.6 && a > 5.6)) M.stag++;
          netTicks[t][0] += a < 4.6 ? 1 : 0; netTicks[t][1] += b < 4.6 ? 1 : 0;
        }
      }
      let end = false;
      for (const e of g.drainEvents()) {
        if (e.e === 'point') {
          M.pts++; M.rallies += e.rally;
          if (rt > 180) for (let t = 0; t < 2; t++) for (let j = 0; j < 2; j++) { M.long++; if (netTicks[t][j] / rt < 0.07) M.stuckBack++; }
          netTicks = [[0, 0], [0, 0]]; rt = 0;
        }
        if (e.e === 'match') end = true;
      }
      if (end) break;
    }
  }
  return M;
}

test('bot pairs move like real pairs: mostly on the same line, both ways, never glued to one end', () => {
  const m = simulate('hard', 3);
  const f = (x) => x / m.t;
  assert.ok(f(m.net) > 0.15, `both at the net a fair share of the time (${(100 * f(m.net)).toFixed(0)}%)`);
  assert.ok(f(m.back) > 0.2, `both defending a fair share (${(100 * f(m.back)).toFixed(0)}%)`);
  assert.ok(f(m.stag) < 0.3, `one up / one back is the exception (${(100 * f(m.stag)).toFixed(0)}%)`);
  assert.ok(m.long === 0 || m.stuckBack / m.long < 0.15, 'nobody stays on the baseline for a whole long point');
});

test('with a person as partner the bot follows their line instead of playing its own game', () => {
  const m = simulate('hard', 3, { human: true });
  assert.ok(m.stag / m.t < 0.3, `the pair stays together (${(100 * m.stag / m.t).toFixed(0)}% split)`);
});

test('bots mishit instead of just watching the ball: rallies end, and it is mostly errors at lower levels', () => {
  const m = simulate('normal', 2);
  assert.ok(m.pts > 20);
  assert.ok(m.rallies / m.pts < 25, `rallies of a sensible length (${(m.rallies / m.pts).toFixed(1)} shots)`);
});
===== END FILE: test/positioning.test.js =====

===== FILE: test/quick.test.js =====
import test, { before, after } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { WebSocket } from 'ws';
import { createGameServer } from '../server/index.js';
import { QuickQueue, WAIT_MS } from '../server/quick.js';
import { RoomManager } from '../server/rooms.js';
import { Store } from '../server/store.js';

let srv, dataDir;
before(async () => { dataDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'padel-quick-')); srv = await createGameServer({ port: 0, dataDir, host: '127.0.0.1' }); });
after(async () => { await srv.close(); fs.rmSync(dataDir, { recursive: true, force: true }); });

const open = async (name) => {
  const ws = new WebSocket(`ws://127.0.0.1:${srv.port}/ws`);
  const msgs = [];
  ws.on('message', (d) => msgs.push(JSON.parse(d.toString())));
  await new Promise((r) => ws.once('open', r));
  ws.send(JSON.stringify({ t: 'hello', name }));
  const until = async (pred, ms = 6000) => { const t0 = Date.now(); while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) { const m = msgs.find(pred); if (m) return m; await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 25)); } throw new Error(`${name}: timeout`); };
  await until((m) => m.t === 'welcome');
  return { ws, msgs, until, send: (o) => ws.send(JSON.stringify(o)) };
};

test('four players who press Quick match are put in the same doubles match, and the server reports how many are online', async () => {
  const c = await Promise.all(['a', 'b', 'c', 'd'].map(open));
  assert.ok(c[0].msgs.find((m) => m.t === 'welcome').online >= 1, 'the welcome says how many are online');
  for (const x of c) x.send({ t: 'quick', name: 'p' });
  const starts = await Promise.all(c.map((x) => x.until((m) => m.t === 'start')));
  assert.equal(new Set(starts.map((s) => s.slot)).size, 4, 'four different seats');
  assert.equal(starts[0].settings.mode, 'doubles');
  const online = await c[0].until((m) => (m.t === 'online' || m.t === 'queue') && m.online !== undefined || m.t === 'online');
  assert.ok((online.n ?? online.online) >= 4);
  for (const x of c) x.ws.close();
});

test('the queue reports its size, can be cancelled, and nobody waits forever (bots fill in after the wait)', async () => {
  const store = new Store(fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'padel-q2-')));
  const rooms = new RoomManager(store);
  let t = 1000;
  const q = new QuickQueue(rooms, () => t);
  const fakeWs = () => ({ readyState: 1, sent: [], send(m) { this.sent.push(JSON.parse(m)); } });
  const mk = (n) => ({ token: store.newToken(), name: n, ws: fakeWs(), look: null });
  const a = mk('a'), b = mk('b');
  q.add(a); q.add(b);
  assert.equal(q.size(), 2);
  assert.equal(q.status(a.token).waiting, 2);
  assert.deepEqual(q.tick(), [], 'two players are not enough yet');
  q.remove(b.token);
  assert.equal(q.size(), 1);
  t += WAIT_MS + 1;
  const made = q.tick();
  assert.equal(made.length, 1, 'after the wait the lone player gets a match');
  const room = made[0];
  assert.equal(room.state, 'match');
  assert.equal(room.slots.filter((s) => s?.kind === 'bot').length, 3, 'bots fill the other seats');
  assert.equal(q.size(), 0);
});
===== END FILE: test/quick.test.js =====

===== FILE: test/scoring.test.js =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { createMatch, pointWon, displayPoints, scoreView, currentServer, serveSide } from '../shared/scoring.js';

const win = (m, team, n = 1) => { let ev = []; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) ev = pointWon(m, team); return ev; };
const winGame = (m, team) => { for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) pointWon(m, team); };

test('15, 30, 40, game', () => {
  const m = createMatch();
  assert.deepEqual(displayPoints(m), ['0', '0']);
  win(m, 0); assert.deepEqual(displayPoints(m), ['15', '0']);
  win(m, 0); assert.deepEqual(displayPoints(m), ['30', '0']);
  win(m, 0); assert.deepEqual(displayPoints(m), ['40', '0']);
  const ev = win(m, 0);
  assert.ok(ev.some((e) => e.type === 'game' && e.team === 0));
  assert.deepEqual(m.games, [1, 0]);
  assert.deepEqual(m.points, [0, 0]);
});

test('deuce and advantage', () => {
  const m = createMatch();
  win(m, 0, 3); win(m, 1, 3);
  assert.deepEqual(displayPoints(m), ['40', '40']);
  assert.equal(scoreView(m).label, 'Deuce');
  win(m, 0); assert.deepEqual(displayPoints(m), ['AD', '40']);
  win(m, 1); assert.deepEqual(displayPoints(m), ['40', '40']); // back to deuce
  win(m, 1); assert.deepEqual(displayPoints(m), ['40', 'AD']);
  const ev = win(m, 1);
  assert.ok(ev.some((e) => e.type === 'game' && e.team === 1));
});

test('golden point decides the game at 40-40', () => {
  const m = createMatch({ goldenPoint: true });
  win(m, 0, 3); win(m, 1, 3);
  assert.equal(scoreView(m).label, 'Golden point');
  const ev = win(m, 1);
  assert.ok(ev.some((e) => e.type === 'game' && e.team === 1));
});

test('set won 6-0, needs two-game lead, tiebreak at 6-6', () => {
  const m = createMatch({ sets: 3 });
  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) winGame(m, 0);
  assert.deepEqual(m.sets, [[6, 0]]);
  assert.deepEqual(m.setsWon, [1, 0]);

  const t = createMatch({ sets: 3 });
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { winGame(t, 0); winGame(t, 1); }
  winGame(t, 0); // 6-5, not a set yet
  assert.deepEqual(t.sets, []);
  winGame(t, 1); // 6-6 -> tiebreak
  assert.equal(t.tiebreak, true);
  win(t, 0, 6); win(t, 1, 6);
  assert.equal(t.tiebreak, true); // 6-6 in the tiebreak, needs a 2-point lead
  win(t, 0);
  assert.equal(t.tiebreak, true);
  win(t, 0);
  assert.equal(t.tiebreak, false);
  assert.deepEqual(t.sets, [[7, 6]]);
});

test('7-5 set without tiebreak', () => {
  const m = createMatch({ sets: 1 });
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { winGame(m, 0); winGame(m, 1); }
  winGame(m, 0); winGame(m, 0); // 7-5
  assert.deepEqual(m.sets, [[7, 5]]);
  assert.equal(m.winner, 0);
});

test('best of three: match is won on the second set and no more points count', () => {
  const m = createMatch({ sets: 3 });
  for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) winGame(m, 1);
  assert.equal(m.winner, 1);
  assert.deepEqual(m.setsWon, [0, 2]);
  assert.deepEqual(pointWon(m, 0), []);
});

test('short formats: single set to 4 games', () => {
  const m = createMatch({ sets: 1, gamesPerSet: 4 });
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) winGame(m, 0);
  assert.equal(m.winner, 0);
});

test('service rotation alternates teams and players, and side alternates per point', () => {
  const m = createMatch();
  const servers = [];
  for (let g = 0; g < 4; g++) { servers.push(currentServer(m)); winGame(m, 0); }
  assert.deepEqual(servers, [
    { team: 0, idx: 0 }, { team: 1, idx: 0 }, { team: 0, idx: 1 }, { team: 1, idx: 1 },
  ]);
  const s = createMatch();
  assert.equal(serveSide(s), 0);
  win(s, 0); assert.equal(serveSide(s), 1);
  win(s, 0); assert.equal(serveSide(s), 0);
});

test('tiebreak service: 1 point, then two each', () => {
  const m = createMatch();
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { winGame(m, 0); winGame(m, 1); }
  winGame(m, 0); winGame(m, 1);
  assert.equal(m.tiebreak, true);
  const order = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) { order.push(currentServer(m)); pointWon(m, i % 2); }
  const teams = order.map((s) => s.team);
  const first = teams[0];
  assert.deepEqual(teams.slice(0, 5), [first, 1 - first, 1 - first, first, first]);
  assert.notEqual(order[0].idx, order[3].idx); // partners alternate between their turns
});

test('match point is flagged in the view', () => {
  const m = createMatch({ sets: 1, gamesPerSet: 2 });
  winGame(m, 0);
  win(m, 0, 3);
  const v = scoreView(m);
  assert.equal(v.gamePoint, 0);
  assert.equal(v.matchPoint, true);
});
===== END FILE: test/scoring.test.js =====

===== FILE: test/serve-shots.test.js =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { PadelGame } from '../shared/game.js';
import { simulateShot, serveBallHeight } from '../shared/physics.js';

test('well-struck slices, drives and volleys clear the net', () => {
  let s = 3;
  const rng = () => { s = (s * 1664525 + 1013904223) >>> 0; return s / 4294967296; };
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng });
  let net = 0, n = 0;
  for (const kind of ['slice', 'forehand', 'backhand', 'volley', 'chiquita', 'bandeja']) {
    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
      const b = { x: (rng() - 0.5) * 6, y: 0.7 + rng() * 0.6, z: -(2.5 + rng() * 6), vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0, sb: 0, ss: 0 };
      const v = g.planShot(kind, b, 1, (rng() - 0.5) * 5, 3 + rng() * 6, 0.95, 12, null, { face: 0, e: 0.1, lateral: 0, forward: 0, spinMul: 1 });
      const sim = simulateShot(b, v, { sb: v.sb, ss: v.ss });
      n++;
      if (sim.kind === 'net') net++;
    }
  }
  assert.ok(net / n < 0.03, `net rate ${net}/${n}`);
});

test('serve quality needs a narrow timing window and a good serve is faster', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng: () => 0.5 });
  const fast = g.planShot('serve', { x: 2, y: 0.62, z: -8, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0 }, 1, 2, 5, 0.98, 0, null);
  const slow = g.planShot('serve', { x: 2, y: 0.62, z: -8, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0 }, 1, 2, 5, 0.35, 0, null);
  assert.ok(Math.hypot(fast.vx, fast.vz) > Math.hypot(slow.vx, slow.vz) * 1.05, 'better timing = more pace');
  const ys = []; for (let t = 0; t < 0.8; t += 0.01) ys.push(serveBallHeight(t));
  const inWindow = ys.filter((y) => Math.abs(y - 0.62) < 0.1).length / ys.length;
  assert.ok(inWindow < 0.3, 'the good window is a small part of the bounce cycle');
});

test('a full-power serve often flies long; a tap almost never does', () => {
  const run = (pace) => {
    let long = 0, n = 60;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      let s = i * 977 + 3;
      const rng = () => { s = (s * 1664525 + 1013904223) >>> 0; return s / 4294967296; };
      const g = new PadelGame({ rng });
      const p = g.players[g.serverSlot];
      g.phase = 'serve'; g.serveWait = 0;
      Object.assign(g.ball, { x: p.x, y: 0.62, z: p.z, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0 });
      g.holdBall = () => {};
      p.swing = { t: 0.05, done: false, ax: 0, az: 5.4 * (p.team === 0 ? 1 : -1), mod: 0, lob: false, pace };
      p.input = { ...p.input, ax: 0, az: 5.4 * (p.team === 0 ? 1 : -1), mx: 0, mz: 0 };
      g.doServe(p);
      const sim = simulateShot(g.ball, { vx: g.ball.vx, vy: g.ball.vy, vz: g.ball.vz }, { sb: g.ball.sb, ss: g.ball.ss });
      const sideOk = Math.abs(sim.z) <= 6.95;
      if (sim.kind !== 'floor' || !sideOk) long++;
    }
    return long / n;
  };
  const tap = run(0.9), full = run(1.12);
  assert.ok(full > tap + 0.25, `full power ${full.toFixed(2)} vs tap ${tap.toFixed(2)}`);
  assert.ok(tap < 0.2, `a normal serve stays in (${tap.toFixed(2)})`);
});
===== END FILE: test/serve-shots.test.js =====

===== FILE: test/singles.test.js =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { PadelGame } from '../shared/game.js';
import { botInput, makeBotMemory } from '../shared/ai.js';
import { createMatch, pointWon, currentServer } from '../shared/scoring.js';
import { Room } from '../server/rooms.js';

const DT = 1 / 60;

test('singles: only two players are active and the server is always the first player of the team', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ settings: { mode: 'singles' } });
  assert.deepEqual(g.players.map((p) => p.active), [true, false, true, false]);
  const m = createMatch({ singles: true, sets: 1, gamesPerSet: 6 });
  const servers = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { servers.push(currentServer(m)); for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) pointWon(m, 0); }
  assert.ok(servers.every((s) => s.idx === 0));
  assert.deepEqual(servers.map((s) => s.team), [0, 1, 0, 1]);
});

test('singles: the court is narrower and fully walled: the ball plays off the side glass, players stay inside', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ settings: { mode: 'singles' }, rng: () => 0.5 });
  g.drainEvents();
  g.phase = 'rally';
  g.rally = { serving: false, faults: 0, lastTeam: 0, lastSlot: 0, bounces: 1, hits: 3, netTouched: false, serveXSign: 1 };
  Object.assign(g.ball, { x: 3.2, y: 1.8, z: 4, vx: 5, vy: 0, vz: 1 });
  const evs = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) { g.tick(DT); evs.push(...g.drainEvents()); }
  assert.ok(evs.some((e) => e.e === 'ball' && e.k === 'wall' && e.axis === 'x'), 'ball hit the side glass at 3.5 m');
  assert.ok(!evs.some((e) => e.e === 'point'), 'no out call: walls are in play');
  assert.ok(Math.abs(g.ball.x) <= 3.5);
  g.applyInput(0, { mx: 1 });
  for (let i = 0; i < 200; i++) g.tick(DT);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(g.players[0].x) <= 3.25 + 1e-6, 'player cannot leave the narrow court');
});

test('singles: two bots play a full short match', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ settings: { mode: 'singles', sets: 1, gamesPerSet: 2, difficulty: 'hard' } });
  const mem = [0, 1, 2, 3].map(makeBotMemory);
  let done = null;
  for (let i = 0; i < 60 * 60 * 30 && !done; i++) {
    for (const s of [0, 2]) g.applyInput(s, botInput(g, s, mem[s], 'hard'));
    g.tick(DT);
    done = g.drainEvents().find((e) => e.e === 'match');
  }
  assert.ok(done, 'singles match finished');
  assert.ok(g.stats.players[1].hits === 0 && g.stats.players[3].hits === 0, 'inactive slots never play');
});

test('room: switching to 1v1 moves players into the singles seats and limits the room to two', () => {
  const store = { recordMatch: () => ({}) };
  const room = new Room('TEST1', store);
  const mk = (n) => room.addMember({ token: n, name: n, ws: null });
  const a = mk('Ana'), b = mk('Ben'), c = mk('Cleo');
  assert.deepEqual([a, b, c].map((m) => room.slotOf(m.id)), [0, 1, 2]);
  assert.match(room.setSettings(a, { mode: 'singles' }), /Too many/);
  room.removeMember(c.id);
  assert.equal(room.setSettings(a, { mode: 'singles' }), null);
  assert.deepEqual([a, b].map((m) => room.slotOf(m.id)), [0, 2]);
  assert.equal(room.canJoin(), 'Room is full');
  assert.equal(room.sit(a, 1), 'Bad slot');
});
===== END FILE: test/singles.test.js =====

===== FILE: test/spin.test.js =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { PadelGame } from '../shared/game.js';
import { stepBall, makeBall, simulateShot } from '../shared/physics.js';

const DT = 1 / 60;
const fly = (b, seconds) => { const ev = []; for (let i = 0; i < seconds * 60; i++) stepBall(b, DT, ev); return ev; };
const launched = (over) => Object.assign(makeBall(), { x: 0, y: 1.2, z: -6, vx: 0, vy: 2, vz: 14 }, over);

test('backspin floats, topspin dives (Magnus effect)', () => {
  const flat = launched({}), back = launched({ sb: 18 }), top = launched({ sb: -18 });
  fly(flat, 0.5); fly(back, 0.5); fly(top, 0.5);
  assert.ok(back.y > flat.y + 0.1, 'backspin higher');
  assert.ok(top.y < flat.y - 0.1, 'topspin lower');
});

test('sidespin curves the ball sideways', () => {
  const a = launched({ ss: 16 }), b = launched({ ss: -16 });
  fly(a, 0.6); fly(b, 0.6);
  assert.ok(a.x > 0.15 && b.x < -0.15, `curve ${a.x} ${b.x}`);
});

test('backspin bounces lower and slower than topspin', () => {
  const run = (sb) => {
    const b = Object.assign(makeBall(), { x: 0, y: 1.5, z: 1, vz: 10, vy: -2, sb });
    const ev = [];
    let peak = 0, after = false, vzAfter = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) {
      ev.length = 0; stepBall(b, DT, ev);
      if (!after && ev.some((e) => e.type === 'floor')) { after = true; vzAfter = b.vz; }
      if (after) peak = Math.max(peak, b.y);
    }
    return { peak, vzAfter };
  };
  const slice = run(17), drive = run(-9);
  assert.ok(slice.peak < drive.peak, `peak ${slice.peak} < ${drive.peak}`);
  assert.ok(slice.vzAfter < drive.vzAfter, 'slice slows down');
});

test('sidespin kicks the ball along the glass on rebound', () => {
  const run = (ss) => {
    const b = Object.assign(makeBall(), { x: 0, y: 2, z: 5, vz: 14, vy: 0, ss });
    const ev = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 80; i++) { stepBall(b, DT, ev); if (ev.some((e) => e.type === 'wall')) break; }
    return b.vx;
  };
  assert.ok(Math.abs(run(17) - run(0)) > 0.3);
});

test('shot solver lands spun balls where they were aimed (spin and drag compensated)', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng: () => 0.5 });
  const b = { ...makeBall(), x: -1, y: 1, z: -4 };
  for (const kind of ['forehand', 'slice', 'bandeja', 'vibora', 'drop', 'lob', 'volley']) {
    const v = g.planShot(kind, b, 1, 2, 7, 1);
    assert.ok(v.sb !== 0, `${kind} has spin`);
    const sim = simulateShot(b, v, { sb: v.sb, ss: v.ss });
    assert.equal(sim.kind, 'floor', `${kind} should land on the floor first`);
    if (kind === 'vibora') continue; // sidespin keeps curving, tested separately
    assert.ok(Math.hypot(sim.x - 2, sim.z - Math.max(7, 0)) < 3.2 || ['drop'].includes(kind), `${kind} lands near aim (${sim.x.toFixed(1)}, ${sim.z.toFixed(1)})`);
  }
});

test('shots have distinct signatures: slice is lower/slower than drive, bandeja slower than smash, vibora has sidespin', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng: () => 0.5 });
  const b = { ...makeBall(), x: 0, y: 1, z: -4 };
  const drive = g.planShot('forehand', b, 1, 0, 7, 1);
  const slice = g.planShot('slice', b, 1, 0, 7, 1);
  assert.ok(slice.sb > 0 && drive.sb < 0);
  assert.ok(Math.hypot(slice.vx, slice.vz) < Math.hypot(drive.vx, drive.vz));
  const hi = { ...makeBall(), x: 0, y: 2.6, z: -3 };
  const smash = g.planShot('smash', hi, 1, 0, 6, 1);
  const bandeja = g.planShot('bandeja', hi, 1, 0, 6, 1);
  const vibora = g.planShot('vibora', hi, 1, 2, 6, 1);
  assert.ok(Math.hypot(bandeja.vx, bandeja.vz) < Math.hypot(smash.vx, smash.vz));
  assert.ok(Math.abs(vibora.ss) > 5);
  assert.ok(bandeja.sb > 5);
});

test('holding the slice modifier plays a slice', () => {
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng: () => 0.5 });
  g.drainEvents();
  g.phase = 'rally';
  g.rally = { serving: false, faults: 0, lastTeam: 0, lastSlot: 0, bounces: 1, hits: 3, netTouched: false, serveXSign: 1 };
  Object.assign(g.ball, { x: 0.3, y: 1, z: 5.5, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: -2 });
  Object.assign(g.players[2], { x: 0, z: 5 });
  g.applyInput(2, { swing: true, mod: 3, ax: 0, az: -6 });
  const evs = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) { g.tick(DT); evs.push(...g.drainEvents()); }
  const hit = evs.find((e) => e.e === 'hit');
  assert.equal(hit.kind, 'slice');
  assert.ok(g.ball.sb > 0);
});
===== END FILE: test/spin.test.js =====

===== FILE: test/story.test.js =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { STORY, sceneFor } from '../shared/story.js';
import { EVENTS } from '../shared/career2.js';
import { ROSTER_BY_ID } from '../shared/roster.js';

test('every career event has an intro and an ending, spoken by a real character', () => {
  for (const e of EVENTS) {
    for (const k of ['intro', 'champion', 'other']) {
      const s = sceneFor(e.id, k);
      assert.ok(s && s.text.length > 20, `${e.id} ${k}`);
      assert.ok(s.who === 'narrator' || ROSTER_BY_ID[s.who], `${e.id} speaker ${s.who}`);
    }
  }
  assert.equal(Object.keys(STORY).length, EVENTS.length);
});

===== FILE: test/walls.test.js =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { stepBall, makeBall, wallE } from '../shared/physics.js';
import { PHYS } from '../shared/constants.js';

const rebound = (b) => {
  const ev = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 90 && !ev.some((e) => e.type === 'wall'); i++) stepBall(b, 1 / 60, ev);
  return ev.find((e) => e.type === 'wall');
};

test('glass is lively, the fence (verja) is dead', () => {
  const side = (z, y) => ({ ...makeBall(), doors: false, x: 4.0, y, z, vx: 14, vy: 0, vz: 0 });
  const glass = side(8, 1.2); const glassHit = rebound(glass);
  const fence = side(4.5, 1.2); const fenceHit = rebound(fence);
  assert.equal(glassHit.mesh, false); assert.equal(fenceHit.mesh, true);
  assert.ok(-glass.vx > 8, `glass returns most of the speed (${glass.vx.toFixed(1)})`);
  assert.ok(-fence.vx < 0.45 * -glass.vx, `the fence takes most of it (${fence.vx.toFixed(1)} vs ${glass.vx.toFixed(1)})`);
  const back = { ...makeBall(), x: 0, y: 1.2, z: 9.0, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 14 }; assert.equal(rebound(back).mesh, false);
  assert.ok(-back.vz > 8, 'the back glass returns the ball');
  const high = { ...makeBall(), x: 0, y: 3.5, z: 9.0, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 14 }; assert.equal(rebound(high).mesh, true);
  assert.ok(-high.vz < 0.45 * -back.vz, 'the mesh above the glass is dead');
});

test('1v1 side walls are glass all along', () => {
  const b = { ...makeBall(), hw: 3.5, x: 2.5, y: 1.2, z: 1.0, vx: 14, vy: 0, vz: 0 };
  assert.equal(rebound(b).mesh, false);
});

test('the floor bounces higher than before and a hard drop keeps most of its height', () => {
  const b = { ...makeBall(), fe: PHYS.FLOOR_E, x: 0, y: 2, z: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0 };
  let peak = 0, bounced = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < 200; i++) {
    const ev = []; stepBall(b, 1 / 60, ev);
    if (ev.some((e) => e.type === 'floor')) bounced = true;
    if (bounced) peak = Math.max(peak, b.y);
  }
  assert.ok(peak > 1.15 && peak < 1.7, `bounce height ${peak.toFixed(2)} m from 2 m`);
  assert.ok(wallE(10, false) > wallE(10, true) * 2);
});

test('spin meets glass: topspin climbs the wall, backspin drops it', () => {
  const hit = (sb) => {
    const b = { ...makeBall(), x: 0, y: 1.5, z: 9.0, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 14, sb };
    rebound(b);
    return b.vy;
  };
  const top = hit(-12), none = hit(0), back = hit(12);
  assert.ok(top > none + 0.5 && back < none - 0.5, `topspin ${top.toFixed(1)}, flat ${none.toFixed(1)}, backspin ${back.toFixed(1)} m/s vertical off the glass`);
});

test('"por 4": a clean short smash from the net bounces out of the court over the fence, a poor one does not', async () => {
  const { PadelGame } = await import('../shared/game.js');
  const g = new PadelGame({ rng: () => 0.5 });
  const fly = (q) => {
    const b = { x: 0.5, y: 2.6, z: -2.4, vx: 0, vy: 0, vz: 0, sb: 0, ss: 0, hw: 5, fe: 0.85, ff: 0.86 };
    const v = g.planShot('smash', b, 1, 1.5, 3.5, q, 8, null, { face: 0, e: 0.1, lateral: 0, forward: 0, spinMul: 1, powerMul: 1 });
    const ball = { ...b, vx: v.vx, vy: v.vy, vz: v.vz, sb: v.sb, ss: v.ss };
    let bounced = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < 400; i++) {
      const ev = []; stepBall(ball, 1 / 60, ev);
      for (const e of ev) { if (e.type === 'floor') bounced = true; if (e.type === 'out' && bounced) return true; }
    }
    return false;
  };
  assert.equal(fly(0.95), true);
  assert.equal(fly(0.6), false);
});

test('a ball that has settled on the floor rolls and slows gently instead of stopping dead, without spamming bounce events', () => {
  const b = { ...makeBall(), x: 0, y: 0.0335, z: 0, vx: 6, vy: 0, vz: 0 };
  let events = 0, dist = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) { const ev = []; const x0 = b.x; stepBall(b, 1 / 60, ev); events += ev.filter((e) => e.type === 'floor').length; dist += b.x - x0; }
  assert.equal(events, 0, 'rolling is not a bounce');
  assert.ok(dist > 2.5, `rolled ${dist.toFixed(1)} m in a second`);
  assert.ok(b.vx > 1 && b.vx < 6, 'slowing, not stopped');
});

    plan: free
    buildCommand: npm ci --omit=dev

    healthCheckPath: /health
